# CUET Vocabulary — Complete Notes | Part 1: A
### All words from transcript | Synonyms · Antonyms · Bengali · Example

> **Format per entry:**
> **WORD** | Bengali | Meaning | Synonyms | Antonyms | Example

---

**ABANDON** | ত্যাগ করা
To give up or desert completely.
✅ desert, forsake, relinquish, renounce, discard, quit
❌ keep, retain, maintain, hold, continue
📝 She chose to **abandon** her plan when funding fell through.

**ABASE** | অপমান করা, হেয় করা
To lower in rank, dignity, or self-respect; to humiliate.
✅ humiliate, degrade, demean, dishonor, belittle, shame
❌ honor, exalt, elevate, respect, dignify
📝 The tyrant tried to **abase** his rivals in front of the crowd.

**ABASH** | লজ্জিত করা, বিব্রত করা
To make someone feel embarrassed or ashamed.
✅ embarrass, shame, mortify, disconcert, humiliate, discomfit
❌ comfort, encourage, embolden, reassure, flatter
📝 She was **abashed** when she walked into the wrong meeting room.

**ABET** | উসকানি দেওয়া, প্ররোচনা দেওয়া
To encourage or assist someone in doing something wrong; to incite.
✅ incite, instigate, provoke, encourage, aid, support, egg on
❌ prevent, stop, discourage, deter, hinder, restrain
📝 He was arrested for aiding and **abetting** the robbery.

**ABETMENT** | উসকানি, প্ররোচনা
The act of inciting or encouraging someone to commit a crime.
✅ instigation, incitement, provocation, encouragement, facilitation
❌ prevention, deterrence, discouragement, restraint
📝 The court found him guilty of **abetment** of suicide.

**ABIDE BY** | মেনে চলা, পালন করা
To accept and follow a rule, decision, or agreement.
✅ comply with, follow, obey, adhere to, respect, observe
❌ violate, break, defy, ignore, disobey
📝 Every member must **abide by** the rules of the organization.

**ABJECT** | হীন, লজ্জাজনক, দুর্দশাগ্রস্ত
Extremely bad, miserable, or shameful; utterly hopeless.
✅ wretched, miserable, pitiful, shameful, deplorable, ignominious
❌ honorable, admirable, proud, dignified, noble, respectable
📝 Millions live in **abject** poverty without clean water or food.

**ABOMINABLE / ABMOMINABLE** | ঘৃণ্য, জঘন্য
Causing disgust; extremely offensive or unpleasant.
✅ repulsive, offensive, horrible, loathsome, disgusting, reprehensible, heinous
❌ pleasant, admirable, delightful, agreeable, lovable
📝 The **abominable** conditions in the prison shocked the inspectors.

**ABSOLVE** | দায়মুক্ত করা, মুক্তি দেওয়া
To formally declare someone free from guilt or blame.
✅ exonerate, acquit, pardon, clear, exculpate, forgive, release
❌ blame, convict, condemn, charge, indict, accuse
📝 The jury **absolved** the defendant due to insufficient evidence.

**ABUNDANT** | প্রচুর, সমৃদ্ধ
Present in large quantities; more than enough.
✅ plentiful, ample, copious, profuse, bountiful, lavish, rich
❌ scarce, insufficient, lacking, meager, sparse, deficient, rare
📝 The valley has **abundant** rainfall throughout the year.

**ACCURATE** | নির্ভুল, সঠিক
Correct in all details; without error.
✅ precise, exact, correct, faithful, flawless, true, perfect
❌ inaccurate, wrong, incorrect, imprecise, faulty, erroneous
📝 The **accurate** report helped the team make the right decision.

**ACERBIC** | কটু, তীক্ষ্ণ, তিক্ত
Sharp and bitter in tone; cutting and sarcastic.
✅ bitter, sharp, sarcastic, caustic, cutting, tart, pungent, biting
❌ sweet, gentle, pleasant, mild, kind, soothing
📝 His **acerbic** criticism stung even the most experienced writers.

**ACHIEVE** | অর্জন করা, হাসিল করা
To successfully accomplish a goal or task.
✅ accomplish, attain, reach, fulfill, realize, complete, gain
❌ fail, miss, lose, abandon, give up, fall short
📝 She worked for years to **achieve** her dream of becoming a pilot.

**ACUMEN** | তীক্ষ্ণ বুদ্ধি, বিচক্ষণতা
The ability to make good judgments; keenness of insight.
✅ insight, intelligence, shrewdness, astuteness, brilliance, wisdom, perception
❌ stupidity, ignorance, dullness, foolishness, naivety, obtuseness
📝 His financial **acumen** made him one of the most respected investors.

**ADAMANT** | অটল, একরোখা
Refusing to change one's mind; stubbornly firm and unyielding.
✅ stubborn, inflexible, unyielding, obstinate, resolute, bull-headed, firm
❌ flexible, yielding, persuadable, compliant, open-minded, willing
📝 She was **adamant** that no one else would be blamed for her mistake.

**ADEPT** | দক্ষ, পারদর্শী, নিপুণ
Very skilled or proficient at something.
✅ skilled, expert, proficient, capable, talented, masterful, accomplished
❌ inept, unskilled, incompetent, amateur, clumsy, unqualified
📝 He is **adept** at handling difficult negotiations under pressure.

**ADEQUATE** | পর্যাপ্ত, যথেষ্ট
Satisfactory or acceptable in quality or quantity; enough.
✅ sufficient, enough, satisfactory, acceptable, ample, reasonable
❌ inadequate, insufficient, lacking, unsatisfactory, deficient, poor
📝 The food supply was barely **adequate** for the winter months.

**ADJOURN** | স্থগিত করা, মুলতবি করা
To postpone or suspend a meeting to a later time.
✅ postpone, defer, suspend, delay, recess, put off, discontinue
❌ continue, proceed, convene, resume, begin, open
📝 The court decided to **adjourn** the hearing until new evidence was found.

**ADMONISH / ADMONITION** | সতর্ক করা / সতর্কবাণী, ভর্ৎসনা
To firmly warn or reprimand; a gentle but serious warning.
✅ warn, rebuke, reprimand, caution, counsel, chide, scold
❌ praise, compliment, approve, commend, encourage, reward
📝 The teacher gave him a quiet **admonition** about his behavior in class.

**ADVERSE** | প্রতিকূল, বিপরীত
Preventing success; harmful; unfavorable conditions or circumstances.
✅ unfavorable, negative, harmful, hostile, contrary, detrimental, opposing
❌ favorable, beneficial, positive, helpful, supportive, advantageous
📝 The expedition continued despite **adverse** weather conditions.

**ADVERSITY** | বিপদ, দুর্ভোগ, প্রতিকূলতা
Difficulty, misfortune, or a difficult situation.
✅ misfortune, hardship, difficulty, trouble, calamity, setback, trial
❌ prosperity, fortune, comfort, luck, ease, happiness, success
📝 His character was shaped by the **adversity** he faced growing up.

**ADVOCATE** | সমর্থন করা / সমর্থক, আইনজীবী
To publicly support or recommend; one who argues for a cause.
✅ support, champion, endorse, defend, promote, uphold, back
❌ oppose, criticize, reject, condemn, discourage, attack
📝 She has always **advocated** for stronger environmental laws.

**AFFLUENT** | ধনী, সমৃদ্ধ, বিত্তশালী
Having a great deal of money or wealth; prosperous.
✅ wealthy, rich, prosperous, opulent, well-off, moneyed, comfortable
❌ poor, destitute, impoverished, needy, penniless, indigent
📝 The school was located in an **affluent** part of the city.

**AFFIRM** | নিশ্চিত করা, সমর্থন করা
To state something as a fact; to confirm or declare firmly.
✅ confirm, assert, declare, state, validate, maintain, uphold
❌ deny, contradict, refute, negate, reject, dispute
📝 The witness was asked to **affirm** that the account was truthful.

**AGITATE / AGITATION** | উত্তেজিত করা / উত্তেজনা, আন্দোলন
To make someone troubled or anxious; to stir up public feeling.
✅ disturb, unsettle, excite, stir, provoke, rouse, agitate
❌ calm, soothe, pacify, settle, comfort, reassure
📝 The speech began to **agitate** the already tense crowd.

**AGGRESSIVE** | আক্রমণাত্মক, উগ্র
Ready to attack; forceful and assertive in a hostile way.
✅ assertive, hostile, forceful, belligerent, combative, militant, fierce
❌ passive, peaceful, calm, gentle, submissive, timid, docile
📝 The **aggressive** marketing campaign pushed competitors out of the market.

**AGONY** | যন্ত্রণা, কষ্ট, পীড়া
Extreme physical or mental suffering; intense pain.
✅ pain, suffering, torment, anguish, distress, misery, torture
❌ comfort, pleasure, relief, ease, joy, bliss
📝 The patient was in **agony** before the painkiller took effect.

**ALACRITY** | উদ্যম, তৎপরতা, উৎসাহ
Brisk and cheerful readiness; eagerness and willingness.
✅ eagerness, willingness, enthusiasm, promptness, zeal, readiness, speed
❌ laziness, reluctance, hesitation, slowness, indifference, apathy, unwillingness
📝 The volunteers took up the task with remarkable **alacrity**.

**ALLIANCE** | মৈত্রী, জোট, সংঘ
A union or association formed for mutual benefit.
✅ union, coalition, partnership, confederation, league, pact, agreement
❌ opposition, rivalry, enmity, conflict, division, separation
📝 The two parties formed a political **alliance** before the elections.

**ALLAY** | প্রশমিত করা, কমানো
To diminish or put to rest fear, suspicion, or worry.
✅ ease, calm, soothe, reduce, relieve, pacify, lessen
❌ worsen, heighten, increase, aggravate, stoke, provoke
📝 The doctor's words helped to **allay** the patient's fears about the surgery.

**ALOOF** | একাকী, নিরাসক্ত, উদাসীন
Not friendly or interested in others; emotionally distant and detached.
✅ detached, distant, isolated, indifferent, cold, reserved, unsociable, withdrawn
❌ friendly, sociable, warm, gregarious, convivial, approachable, involved
📝 He remained **aloof** from office gossip, focusing only on his work.

**AMBIGUOUS** | দ্ব্যর্থবোধক, অস্পষ্ট
Open to more than one interpretation; unclear or vague in meaning.
✅ vague, unclear, obscure, equivocal, indistinct, indefinite, uncertain
❌ clear, explicit, definite, unambiguous, certain, obvious, precise
📝 The **ambiguous** phrasing of the law caused years of legal disputes.

**AMIABLE** | মিষ্টভাষী, বন্ধুবৎসল
Friendly and pleasant in manner; easy to get along with.
✅ friendly, pleasant, likable, affable, cordial, congenial, warm, agreeable
❌ hostile, unfriendly, rude, disagreeable, cold, unpleasant
📝 The **amiable** receptionist made every visitor feel welcome immediately.

**ANONYMOUS** | বেনামী, অজ্ঞাত
Having an unknown or unacknowledged name or identity.
✅ unknown, unidentified, unnamed, incognito, nameless, unacknowledged
❌ identified, named, known, famous, recognized, public
📝 The tip came from an **anonymous** source who refused to be named.

**ANTAGONIZE / ANTAGONIST** | বিরোধিতা করা / প্রতিপক্ষ, শত্রু
To provoke someone into becoming hostile; an adversary or opponent.
✅ provoke, irritate, alienate, aggravate, anger, offend, oppose
❌ appease, please, soothe, befriend, reconcile, support
📝 His constant criticism began to **antagonize** even his closest allies.

**APPARENT** | স্পষ্ট, দৃশ্যমান
Clearly visible or understood; obvious; seeming real or true.
✅ obvious, visible, clear, evident, noticeable, plain, manifest
❌ hidden, unclear, obscure, invisible, concealed, ambiguous
📝 It was **apparent** to everyone that she was the most qualified candidate.

**APPREHENSION** | আশঙ্কা, উদ্বেগ; গ্রেপ্তার
Anxiety about the future; dread; also, the act of arresting someone.
✅ anxiety, dread, fear, worry, concern, unease, foreboding
❌ confidence, calm, ease, reassurance, composure, certainty
📝 She felt a wave of **apprehension** before the final presentation.

**ARDENT** | আবেগপ্রবণ, উৎসাহী, তীব্র
Enthusiastic or passionate; feeling or showing very strong emotion.
✅ passionate, fervent, enthusiastic, zealous, devoted, intense, keen
❌ indifferent, apathetic, cold, unenthusiastic, lukewarm, uncaring
📝 He was an **ardent** admirer of classical music from childhood.

**ARDUOUS** | কঠিন, কষ্টসাধ্য, পরিশ্রমসাধ্য
Requiring great effort and energy; extremely difficult and tiring.
✅ difficult, strenuous, laborious, exhausting, onerous, tough, demanding
❌ easy, simple, effortless, light, comfortable, manageable
📝 The **arduous** journey across the mountains took three weeks on foot.

**AROUSE** | উত্তেজিত করা, জাগ্রত করা
To evoke or awaken a feeling or reaction; to excite or provoke.
✅ provoke, stimulate, excite, awaken, stir, trigger, incite
❌ calm, soothe, suppress, pacify, lull, quieten
📝 The speech **aroused** strong feelings of patriotism in the audience.

**ARROGANT** | অহংকারী, দম্ভী, ঔদ্ধত্যপূর্ণ
Having an exaggerated sense of one's own importance; haughty.
✅ haughty, conceited, proud, pompous, condescending, overbearing, self-important
❌ humble, modest, polite, respectful, unassuming, meek
📝 His **arrogant** refusal to listen to advice cost him many opportunities.

**ASKEW** | বাঁকা, তেরচা
Not in a straight or level position; crooked; twisted to one side.
✅ crooked, slanted, tilted, lopsided, bent, awry, twisted
❌ straight, level, aligned, upright, even, centered
📝 After the storm, every signpost in the village was **askew**.

**ASPIRE / ASPIRATION** | আকাঙ্ক্ষা করা / স্বপ্ন, লক্ষ্য
To direct hope or ambition toward a goal; a strong desire for achievement.
✅ aim, desire, seek, strive, dream, yearn, hope, reach for
❌ give up, despair, abandon, settle, neglect, resign
📝 She always **aspired** to run her own business someday.

**ASPERITY** | রূঢ়তা, কঠোরতা, তিক্ততা
Harshness of tone or manner; roughness and severity.
✅ harshness, severity, roughness, sharpness, sternness, acrimony, tartness
❌ mildness, gentleness, kindness, warmth, softness, pleasantness
📝 He responded to every question with unexpected **asperity**.

**ASTUTE** | বিচক্ষণ, চতুর, বুদ্ধিমান
Having ability to assess situations clearly; clever and perceptive.
✅ shrewd, clever, perceptive, sharp, sagacious, intelligent, cunning, wise
❌ naive, foolish, dull, gullible, stupid, simple, oblivious
📝 The **astute** lawyer spotted the flaw in the contract immediately.

**ATROCIOUS** | অত্যাচারী, নৃশংস, জঘন্য
Shockingly wicked, cruel, or brutal; horrifyingly bad.
✅ brutal, savage, monstrous, cruel, heinous, barbaric, horrifying, wicked
❌ kind, gentle, humane, compassionate, benevolent, merciful
📝 The **atrocious** crimes committed during the war shocked the world.

**AUTHENTIC** | খাঁটি, আসল, প্রামাণিক
Genuine; of undisputed origin; real and true.
✅ genuine, real, original, legitimate, true, verified, bona fide, credible
❌ fake, counterfeit, false, fraudulent, imitation, forged, spurious
📝 Experts confirmed the painting was **authentic** and over 400 years old.

**AUSTERE** | কঠোর, সংযমী, বিলাসবর্জিত
Severe or strict in manner; simple and without luxury; harsh.
✅ severe, strict, rigorous, stern, spartan, frugal, ascetic, self-disciplined
❌ indulgent, luxurious, lenient, mild, permissive, lavish
📝 The monk chose an **austere** life in a remote mountain monastery.

**AUTONOMOUS / AUTONOMY** | স্বায়ত্তশাসিত / স্বাধীনতা, স্বশাসন
Having freedom to govern itself; self-governing; independence.
✅ independent, self-governing, sovereign, free, self-reliant, self-directed
❌ dependent, controlled, submissive, governed, restricted, colonized
📝 The region was granted **autonomy** after decades of political struggle.

**AVERSION** | বিরাগ, ঘৃণা, অনীহা
A strong dislike or disinclination; feeling of repulsion toward something.
✅ dislike, hatred, repulsion, loathing, antipathy, reluctance, disgust
❌ love, attraction, fondness, liking, enthusiasm, desire
📝 She had a deep **aversion** to dishonesty in any form.
# CUET Vocabulary — Complete Notes | Part 2: B–C
### All words from transcript | Synonyms · Antonyms · Bengali · Example

---

## 🔤 B

**BARREN** | অনুর্বর, বন্ধ্যা
Too poor to produce vegetation; unproductive; empty and bleak.
✅ unproductive, infertile, sterile, desolate, arid, empty, bare
❌ fertile, productive, lush, rich, fruitful, abundant, green
📝 Decades of overfarming turned the once-rich land completely **barren**.

**BELLIGERENT** | যুদ্ধংদেহী, লড়াকু, আক্রমণাত্মক
Hostile and aggressive; eager to quarrel or fight; warlike.
✅ aggressive, hostile, combative, militant, quarrelsome, pugnacious, antagonistic
❌ peaceful, friendly, gentle, benign, non-aggressive, cooperative
📝 The **belligerent** neighbour started arguments over the smallest issues.

**BENIGN** | নিরীহ, দয়ালু, ক্ষতিহীন
Gentle and kind; not harmful; (of a growth or disease) not dangerous.
✅ harmless, mild, gentle, kind, non-threatening, favorable, safe
❌ malign, harmful, dangerous, hostile, malignant, sinister
📝 The doctor confirmed the cyst was **benign** and required no treatment.

**BENEVOLENT** | দয়ালু, পরোপকারী, মহানুভব
Well-meaning and kindly; generous and charitable toward others.
✅ kind, charitable, generous, compassionate, merciful, humane, philanthropic
❌ cruel, selfish, malevolent, unkind, greedy, ruthless, mean
📝 The **benevolent** landlord reduced rent for families hit by the floods.

**BIZARRE** | অদ্ভুত, বিচিত্র, অস্বাভাবিক
Very strange or unusual; odd in an unsettling or surprising way.
✅ strange, weird, peculiar, odd, eccentric, outlandish, absurd, extraordinary
❌ normal, ordinary, conventional, typical, familiar, standard
📝 The **bizarre** events of that night were never fully explained.

**BLATANT** | ডাহা, প্রকাশ্য, লজ্জাহীন
Done openly and unashamedly; completely obvious and undisguised.
✅ obvious, brazen, open, flagrant, outright, shameless, glaring, undisguised
❌ subtle, hidden, concealed, discreet, covert, understated
📝 His **blatant** disregard for the rules angered the entire committee.

**BOISTEROUS** | হৈচৈপূর্ণ, উচ্ছৃঙ্খল, দুরন্ত
Noisy, energetic, and cheerful; rough and turbulent in manner.
✅ noisy, rowdy, loud, raucous, unruly, disorderly, turbulent, wild
❌ quiet, calm, peaceful, restrained, orderly, well-mannered, subdued
📝 The **boisterous** crowd celebrated the victory late into the night.

**BOORISH** | অভদ্র, রুক্ষ স্বভাবের, অসভ্য
Rough and badly mannered; rude and unpleasant; ill-mannered.
✅ rude, ill-mannered, crude, coarse, uncouth, impolite, offensive, vulgar
❌ polite, refined, courteous, elegant, well-mannered, cultured, civil
📝 His **boorish** comments at the dinner table silenced the whole room.

**BOLD / BLATANT** | সাহসী / ডাহা
Showing courage; willing to take risks; (blatant) shamelessly obvious.
✅ daring, fearless, brave, audacious, confident, forthright
❌ timid, cowardly, shy, hesitant, cautious
📝 She made a **bold** decision to leave her job and start her own venture.

**BRUTAL** | নিষ্ঠুর, নির্দয়, পাশবিক
Savagely violent and cruel; unpleasantly harsh or difficult.
✅ savage, cruel, vicious, barbaric, ferocious, ruthless, merciless, harsh
❌ gentle, kind, humane, compassionate, merciful, tender
📝 The **brutal** cold of the arctic winter was beyond anything they had trained for.

**BUOYANT** | আশাবাদী, প্রাণচঞ্চল, ভাসমান
Cheerful and optimistic; able to float; lively and resilient.
✅ cheerful, optimistic, lively, elated, hopeful, resilient, energetic
❌ depressed, gloomy, pessimistic, heavy, sad, downcast, sinking
📝 Despite the setbacks, the team remained **buoyant** and motivated.

**BULL-HEADED** | একগুঁয়ে, জেদী
Stubbornly determined; refusing to change one's mind; obstinate.
✅ stubborn, obstinate, inflexible, headstrong, adamant, unyielding
❌ flexible, open-minded, agreeable, compliant, yielding
📝 His **bull-headed** refusal to listen led to a costly mistake.

---

## 🔤 C

**CALLOUS** | নির্দয়, নিষ্ঠুর, সংবেদনহীন
Showing insensitive and cruel disregard for others; emotionally hard.
✅ insensitive, heartless, cruel, cold, unfeeling, ruthless, indifferent, thick-skinned
❌ sensitive, compassionate, caring, empathetic, warm-hearted, feeling
📝 The **callous** response to the disaster victims shocked aid workers.

**CANDID** | সৎ, সরল, খোলামেলা
Truthful and straightforward; frank and direct; unpretentious and honest.
✅ honest, frank, open, straightforward, sincere, transparent, direct, genuine
❌ dishonest, deceptive, secretive, sly, cunning, evasive, pretentious
📝 He gave a **candid** interview about his failures as well as his successes.

**CAPRICIOUS** | খামখেয়ালী, অস্থিরমতি
Given to sudden unpredictable changes of mood or behavior; whimsical.
✅ unpredictable, whimsical, erratic, fickle, impulsive, mercurial, changeable
❌ consistent, stable, reliable, predictable, steady, rational, dependable
📝 The **capricious** nature of the stock market surprised even experienced investors.

**CAPTIVATE** | মুগ্ধ করা, বশ করা
To attract and hold the interest and attention of; to charm or fascinate.
✅ fascinate, charm, enchant, mesmerize, attract, enthrall, bewitch
❌ repel, bore, repulse, alienate, disgust, disinterest
📝 The performer completely **captivated** the audience from the very first act.

**CAUSTIC** | ক্ষয়কারী, তীক্ষ্ণ, কটু
Capable of burning or corroding; sharply critical or sarcastic in tone.
✅ corrosive, burning, biting, sarcastic, acerbic, sharp, cutting, scathing
❌ mild, gentle, soothing, pleasant, complimentary, kind
📝 His **caustic** review of the novel was brutally honest but effective.

**CAUTION / CAUTIOUS** | সতর্কতা / সতর্ক
Care taken to avoid danger or mistakes; showing careful thought.
✅ careful, wary, vigilant, prudent, guarded, attentive, circumspect
❌ reckless, careless, rash, impulsive, negligent, hasty
📝 She approached the negotiation with extreme **caution**.

**CEASE** | থামা, বন্ধ হওয়া
To come or bring to an end; to stop happening or doing something.
✅ stop, halt, end, discontinue, terminate, pause, desist
❌ continue, persist, start, begin, proceed, resume
📝 The fighting finally **ceased** after the peace agreement was signed.

**CHAGRINED / CHAGRIN** | মনোবেদনা, হতাশা, বিরক্তি
Feeling distress or embarrassment due to failure or humiliation.
✅ disappointment, dismay, irritation, frustration, embarrassment, mortification
❌ satisfaction, pride, pleasure, joy, contentment, delight
📝 To her great **chagrin**, she realized she had forgotten the key presentation.

**CHAOTIC** | বিশৃঙ্খল, অবিন্যস্ত, এলোমেলো
In a state of complete disorder and confusion; anarchic.
✅ disorderly, confused, turbulent, anarchic, messy, disorganized, uncontrolled
❌ orderly, organized, systematic, calm, structured, peaceful, controlled
📝 The evacuation became **chaotic** when the second alarm went off.

**CHARISMATIC** | আকর্ষণীয়, ব্যক্তিত্বসম্পন্ন
Exercising a compelling charm that inspires devotion in others; magnetic.
✅ charming, magnetic, attractive, compelling, dynamic, captivating, influential
❌ boring, dull, unappealing, uninspiring, repellent, forgettable
📝 The **charismatic** leader drew thousands to listen to him speak.

**CIRCUMSPECT** | সতর্ক, বিচক্ষণ, সাবধানী
Wary and unwilling to take risks; careful to consider all circumstances.
✅ cautious, careful, wary, prudent, vigilant, attentive, guarded, discreet
❌ reckless, careless, rash, impulsive, negligent, heedless, hasty
📝 A surgeon must always be **circumspect** before making any decision.

**CLEMENCY** | দয়া, ক্ষমা, ক্ষমাশীলতা
Mercy and leniency shown toward someone, especially an offender.
✅ mercy, leniency, compassion, forgiveness, grace, pardon, mildness
❌ cruelty, harshness, severity, mercilessness, ruthlessness, strictness
📝 The prisoner appealed for **clemency** after twenty years of good behavior.

**COARSE** | রুক্ষ, মোটা, অমার্জিত
Rough or loose in texture; crude and unrefined in manner or language.
✅ rough, crude, unrefined, harsh, uncultured, vulgar, coarse-grained, bumpy
❌ smooth, fine, refined, polished, delicate, elegant, sophisticated
📝 The **coarse** texture of the burlap bag scratched her hands badly.

**COLOSSAL** | বিশাল, অতিকায়, প্রচণ্ড বড়
Extremely large or impressive; enormous in size, extent, or degree.
✅ enormous, massive, huge, gigantic, immense, mammoth, vast, towering
❌ tiny, small, miniature, microscopic, slight, petite, negligible
📝 The **colossal** statue at the harbor entrance greeted every arriving ship.

**COMICAL** | হাস্যকর, মজাদার
Causing laughter; amusing in a silly or clumsy way; humorous.
✅ funny, amusing, humorous, absurd, ridiculous, laughable, entertaining
❌ serious, solemn, grave, unfunny, dull, tragic
📝 The **comical** misunderstanding between the two coworkers made everyone laugh.

**COMMEMORATE** | স্মরণ করা, উদযাপন করা
To recall and show respect for someone or something in a ceremony.
✅ celebrate, honor, remember, mark, observe, memorialize, pay tribute to
❌ forget, ignore, dishonor, disrespect, overlook, neglect
📝 The city built a monument to **commemorate** the soldiers who gave their lives.

**COMMODIOUS** | প্রশস্ত, বড়, স্থানসম্পন্ন
Roomy and comfortable; having ample space; spacious.
✅ spacious, roomy, large, ample, expansive, comfortable, capacious
❌ cramped, small, tiny, narrow, confined, restricted, limited
📝 The **commodious** conference hall could seat five hundred people easily.

**COMPEL** | বাধ্য করা, মজবুর করা
To force or oblige someone to do something; to drive by overwhelming pressure.
✅ force, oblige, coerce, pressure, drive, make, necessitate, require
❌ allow, permit, free, release, let, encourage, invite
📝 The drought **compelled** thousands of farmers to abandon their fields.

**COMPLACENT** | আত্মতুষ্ট, নিশ্চিন্ত
Showing uncritical satisfaction with oneself or one's achievements; smug.
✅ self-satisfied, content, smug, unconcerned, overconfident, careless, complacent
❌ ambitious, concerned, worried, vigilant, diligent, motivated, driven
📝 After three consecutive wins, the team became **complacent** and lost badly.

**COMPULSORY / COMPULSION** | বাধ্যতামূলক / মজবুরি
Required by law or rules; obligatory; (compulsion) an irresistible urge.
✅ mandatory, obligatory, required, necessary, enforced, binding
❌ optional, voluntary, unnecessary, avoidable, elective, free
📝 Wearing helmets is **compulsory** for all motorcycle riders in this city.

**CONCEITED** | অহংকারী, আত্মাভিমানী, দম্ভী
Excessively proud of oneself; having an inflated idea of one's own importance.
✅ arrogant, vain, self-important, pompous, haughty, egotistical, proud
❌ humble, modest, self-effacing, unassuming, meek, down-to-earth
📝 The **conceited** young actor refused to take direction from the experienced director.

**CONCUR** | সম্মত হওয়া, একমত হওয়া
To be of the same opinion; to agree; to happen simultaneously.
✅ agree, assent, consent, accord, coincide, approve, comply
❌ disagree, oppose, dissent, object, differ, contradict
📝 Both scientists **concurred** that the experiment needed to be repeated.

**CONFINE / CONFINEMENT** | সীমাবদ্ধ করা / কারাবাস, বন্দিত্ব
To keep within limits; to imprison; the state of being restricted or imprisoned.
✅ restrict, limit, imprison, detain, contain, enclose, shut in
❌ release, free, liberate, open, expand, allow
📝 The tiger spent its life in **confinement** at a poorly managed zoo.

**CONGEAL** | জমে যাওয়া, জমাট বাঁধা
To change from a fluid to a solid state; to coagulate or thicken.
✅ solidify, coagulate, harden, thicken, clot, set, freeze, curdle
❌ melt, liquefy, dissolve, thaw, soften, loosen
📝 The gravy had **congealed** overnight into a thick, solid layer.

**CONGENIAL** | মনোরম, সদয়, মিলনশীল
Pleasant because of having similar qualities or interests; friendly and agreeable.
✅ pleasant, friendly, agreeable, compatible, cordial, warm, affable, likable
❌ unpleasant, hostile, incompatible, unfriendly, disagreeable, cold
📝 She finally found a **congenial** work environment where she could thrive.

**CONGESTED** | যানজটপূর্ণ, ভিড়াক্রান্ত, রুদ্ধ
Overfilled or blocked with people or traffic; overcrowded.
✅ overcrowded, blocked, overfull, jammed, packed, stuffed, clogged
❌ empty, clear, uncrowded, open, spacious, flowing, free
📝 The roads were so **congested** that ambulances could not get through.

**CONSECUTIVE** | ধারাবাহিক, ক্রমাগত
Following each other continuously without interruption; sequential.
✅ successive, continuous, sequential, uninterrupted, unbroken, back-to-back
❌ alternating, intermittent, sporadic, separate, non-sequential
📝 They won the championship for five **consecutive** years in a row.

**CONSCIENTIOUS** | বিবেকবান, দায়িত্বশীল, নিষ্ঠাবান
Wishing to do what is right; thorough and very careful in one's work.
✅ diligent, careful, thorough, responsible, ethical, virtuous, scrupulous
❌ negligent, careless, irresponsible, reckless, sloppy, lazy, heedless
📝 A **conscientious** worker always double-checks their output before submitting.

**CONSIDERATE** | অন্যের প্রতি সদয়, মনোযোগী, বিবেচনাশীল
Careful not to cause inconvenience or hurt to others; thoughtful and empathetic.
✅ thoughtful, mindful, sympathetic, caring, attentive, kind, sensitive
❌ inconsiderate, thoughtless, selfish, reckless, heedless, callous
📝 He was always **considerate** of others when working late in a shared space.

**CONSPICUOUS** | দৃষ্টিগোচর, লক্ষণীয়, স্পষ্ট
Standing out so as to be clearly visible; attracting notice or attention.
✅ noticeable, obvious, prominent, visible, apparent, striking, evident, outstanding
❌ inconspicuous, hidden, unnoticed, subtle, obscure, invisible, unremarkable
📝 The bright yellow jacket made her **conspicuous** in the sea of grey suits.

**CONTAMINATE** | দূষিত করা, কলুষিত করা
To make something impure or harmful by introducing a pollutant or poison.
✅ pollute, taint, corrupt, infect, poison, defile, adulterate, dirty
❌ purify, clean, cleanse, sterilize, decontaminate, filter
📝 The factory's waste was found to have **contaminated** the local groundwater.

**CONTEMPLATE** | গভীরভাবে চিন্তা করা, বিবেচনা করা
To think deeply and at length; to consider doing something; to meditate.
✅ consider, ponder, reflect, meditate, think about, muse, deliberate, study
❌ ignore, neglect, disregard, dismiss, overlook, avoid
📝 She sat for an hour to **contemplate** the consequences before deciding.

**CONTIGUOUS** | পাশাপাশি, সংলগ্ন, সীমানালগ্ন
Sharing a common border; next to or touching; in close proximity.
✅ adjacent, neighboring, bordering, touching, connected, adjoining, nearby
❌ distant, separate, remote, far, disconnected, isolated
📝 The two **contiguous** countries signed a trade deal after years of negotiation.

**CONVALESCE / CONVALESCENCE** | সুস্থ হওয়া / আরোগ্য লাভ
To recover health and strength gradually after illness or injury.
✅ recover, recuperate, heal, improve, mend, regain strength, get better
❌ worsen, deteriorate, weaken, relapse, decline, suffer
📝 He spent two months in the countryside to **convalesce** after the surgery.

**CONVIVIAL** | হাসিখুশি সামাজিক, আড্ডাপ্রিয়
Fond of feasting, drinking, and good company; sociable and lively.
✅ sociable, friendly, gregarious, jovial, warm, cheerful, amiable, outgoing
❌ antisocial, unsociable, unfriendly, reclusive, cold, aloof, reserved
📝 The **convivial** atmosphere at the reunion made everyone feel at home.

**CONVOLUTED** | জটিল, প্যাঁচানো, দুর্বোধ্য
Extremely complex and difficult to follow; intricate and unnecessarily complicated.
✅ complicated, complex, intricate, twisted, perplexing, confusing, labyrinthine
❌ simple, straightforward, clear, direct, uncomplicated, plain, easy
📝 The **convoluted** tax regulations confused even experienced accountants.

**CORDIAL** | আন্তরিক, উষ্ণ, বন্ধুসুলভ
Warm and friendly in manner; pleasantly polite and sincere.
✅ warm, friendly, affable, amiable, pleasant, welcoming, genial
❌ cold, unfriendly, hostile, distant, rude, aloof, indifferent
📝 Despite their business rivalry, they maintained a **cordial** relationship.

**CORROBORATE** | নিশ্চিত করা, সমর্থন করা, প্রমাণ করা
To confirm or give support to a statement or theory; to verify evidence.
✅ confirm, verify, support, validate, substantiate, authenticate, affirm, back up
❌ contradict, refute, deny, disprove, negate, undermine, dispute
📝 The second witness was able to **corroborate** everything the first had said.

**COUNTERFEIT** | নকল, জাল, প্রতারণামূলক অনুকরণ
Made in imitation of something valuable in order to deceive; fake.
✅ fake, forged, fraudulent, imitation, bogus, spurious, phony, artificial
❌ genuine, authentic, real, legitimate, original, true, bona fide
📝 Customs officers seized a shipment of **counterfeit** designer goods.

**COVERT** | গোপন, লুকানো, ছদ্মবেশী
Not openly acknowledged or displayed; concealed and secret.
✅ secret, hidden, undercover, clandestine, concealed, disguised, stealthy
❌ open, public, overt, visible, transparent, apparent, obvious
📝 The spy was assigned to a **covert** operation in a foreign country.

**COZY** | আরামদায়ক, উষ্ণ, নিরাপদ
Giving a feeling of comfort, warmth, and relaxation; snug and inviting.
✅ comfortable, warm, snug, relaxing, homely, inviting, pleasant, sheltered
❌ uncomfortable, cold, dreary, chilly, unwelcoming, harsh, exposed
📝 The **cozy** cottage by the lake was perfect for a winter holiday.

**CRANKY** | খিটখিটে, সনকী, বদমেজাজী
Irritable and bad-tempered; having a tendency to complain; grumpy.
✅ grumpy, irritable, bad-tempered, peevish, touchy, crotchety, testy
❌ cheerful, good-tempered, pleasant, calm, easygoing, happy
📝 She got **cranky** after missing her afternoon nap.

**CULPABLE** | দোষী, দায়ী, নিন্দার যোগ্য
Deserving blame or censure; guilty; responsible for a fault or wrong.
✅ guilty, blameworthy, liable, responsible, at fault, answerable, censurable
❌ innocent, blameless, guiltless, exempt, absolved, cleared
📝 The investigation found all three executives **culpable** for the data breach.

**CUPIDITY** | লোভ, লালসা, অর্থলিপ্সা
Greed for money or possessions; excessive and selfish desire for wealth.
✅ greed, avarice, covetousness, materialism, acquisitiveness, rapacity, hunger
❌ generosity, selflessness, contentment, charity, altruism, giving
📝 His **cupidity** blinded him to the ethical consequences of his decisions.

**CURTAIL** | কমানো, সংক্ষিপ্ত করা, ছেঁটে ফেলা
To reduce in extent or quantity; to impose a restriction on something.
✅ reduce, cut, shorten, limit, restrict, decrease, trim, diminish
❌ extend, expand, increase, lengthen, prolong, amplify, enlarge
📝 The government was forced to **curtail** spending due to the economic crisis.
# CUET Vocabulary — Complete Notes | Part 3: D–F
### All words from transcript | Synonyms · Antonyms · Bengali · Example

## 🔤 D

**DEARTH** | অভাব, সংকট, ঘাটতি
A scarcity or lack of something; insufficient supply of what is needed.
✅ scarcity, shortage, lack, insufficiency, deficit, paucity, want, absence
❌ abundance, surplus, plenty, excess, overflow, sufficiency, wealth
📝 There is a severe **dearth** of qualified teachers in rural schools.

**DEBONAIR** | সুদর্শন, মার্জিত, আকর্ষণীয়
Confident, stylish, and charming in manner; suave and sophisticated.
✅ suave, charming, sophisticated, stylish, elegant, smooth, refined, courteous
❌ awkward, clumsy, rude, uncouth, unsophisticated, gauche
📝 The **debonair** host greeted every guest with a warm handshake and a smile.

**DECADENT** | অবক্ষয়শীল, নৈতিকভাবে ক্ষয়িষ্ণু
Characterized by moral or cultural decline; self-indulgent and corrupt.
✅ corrupt, immoral, depraved, dissolute, indulgent, degenerate, self-indulgent
❌ moral, virtuous, principled, upright, pure, disciplined, austere
📝 The **decadent** lifestyle of the ruling class was resented by the poor.

**DEFER** | স্থগিত করা; মেনে নেওয়া
To postpone an action; to submit respectfully to another's judgment.
✅ postpone, delay, put off, adjourn, suspend, yield, submit
❌ proceed, rush, advance, hasten, continue, resist, refuse
📝 The decision was **deferred** until all members could attend the meeting.

**DEFUNCT** | অচল, মৃত, অকার্যকর
No longer existing or functioning; dead; no longer active or in use.
✅ dead, extinct, obsolete, inactive, non-existent, disused, gone
❌ active, operational, alive, functioning, current, existing, ongoing
📝 The **defunct** newspaper had once been the country's most read daily.

**DELECTABLE** | সুস্বাদু, অত্যন্ত আনন্দদায়ক
Delicious; extremely pleasing to the senses; charming and delightful.
✅ delicious, tasty, pleasant, charming, delightful, scrumptious, enjoyable
❌ disgusting, unpleasant, repulsive, horrible, revolting, awful
📝 The banquet featured a **delectable** spread of dishes from around the world.

**DELIBERATE** | ইচ্ছাকৃত, সুচিন্তিত
Done consciously and intentionally; careful and unhurried in speech or action.
✅ intentional, calculated, planned, careful, considered, measured, studied
❌ accidental, unintentional, hasty, impulsive, careless, spontaneous
📝 His **deliberate** choice of words showed he had thought deeply about the issue.

**DENOUNCE** | প্রকাশ্যে নিন্দা করা
To publicly declare something to be wrong or evil; to condemn openly.
✅ condemn, criticize, censure, accuse, attack, expose, decry, repudiate
❌ praise, commend, support, defend, endorse, approve, champion
📝 The international community was quick to **denounce** the human rights violation.

**DEPRECATE / DEPRECIATE** | অবমূল্যায়ন করা; নিন্দা করা
To express disapproval of; to belittle; to reduce in value over time.
✅ disparage, belittle, criticize, diminish, devalue, downplay, denigrate
❌ appreciate, praise, value, commend, elevate, admire, exalt
📝 He tried to **deprecate** her achievements to make himself look better.

**DESTITUTE** | দরিদ্র, নিঃস্ব, অসহায়
Without the basic necessities of life; extremely poor; lacking completely.
✅ poor, impoverished, penniless, needy, indigent, bankrupt, lacking
❌ wealthy, affluent, rich, prosperous, comfortable, well-off, opulent
📝 After losing everything in the flood, the family was left completely **destitute**.

**DETERIORATE** | অবনতি হওয়া, খারাপ হওয়া
To become progressively worse in quality, condition, or value.
✅ worsen, decline, degrade, decay, crumble, degenerate, weaken, fall apart
❌ improve, recover, advance, strengthen, enhance, progress, flourish
📝 Her health continued to **deteriorate** despite the treatment.

**DEVIOUS** | কুটিল, ধূর্ত, প্রতারণামূলক
Showing a skillful use of underhanded tactics; dishonest and crafty.
✅ cunning, sly, dishonest, tricky, deceitful, underhanded, scheming, crafty
❌ honest, straightforward, direct, sincere, trustworthy, candid, open
📝 The **devious** scheme was uncovered before it could cause any real damage.

**DILIGENT** | পরিশ্রমী, অধ্যবসায়ী, নিষ্ঠাবান
Having or showing care and effort in one's work; hardworking and persistent.
✅ hardworking, industrious, assiduous, conscientious, persistent, tireless, careful
❌ lazy, idle, careless, negligent, slothful, sluggish, half-hearted
📝 The **diligent** student spent every weekend preparing for the entrance exam.

**DIMINISH** | কমানো, হ্রাস করা, ছোট করা
To make or become less; to reduce in size, extent, or importance.
✅ reduce, lessen, decrease, shrink, dwindle, decline, weaken, abate
❌ increase, grow, enlarge, expand, amplify, strengthen, boost
📝 The rains helped to **diminish** the risk of wildfires in the region.

**DISMAL** | বিষণ্ণ, নিরানন্দ, হতাশাজনক
Causing gloom or depression; very bad; dreary and cheerless.
✅ gloomy, dreary, depressing, bleak, somber, melancholy, wretched, dark
❌ cheerful, bright, joyful, pleasant, happy, optimistic, lively
📝 The outlook for the company was **dismal** after three years of losses.

**DISPARAGE** | ছোট করা, নিন্দা করা, হেয় করা
To regard or represent as being of little worth; to belittle someone.
✅ belittle, criticize, denigrate, deprecate, demean, mock, disrespect, insult
❌ praise, compliment, honor, respect, admire, commend, exalt
📝 A good mentor never **disparages** a student's honest effort.

**DISMANTLE** | ভেঙে ফেলা, টুকরো টুকরো করা
To take something apart piece by piece; to demolish or disassemble.
✅ take apart, disassemble, demolish, break down, strip, deconstruct
❌ build, assemble, construct, put together, erect, create
📝 Workers began to **dismantle** the old bridge last Monday.

**DOCILE** | বাধ্য, নম্র, সহজে নিয়ন্ত্রণযোগ্য
Ready to accept control or instruction; easily managed or handled.
✅ obedient, submissive, manageable, compliant, tame, gentle, meek
❌ unruly, rebellious, stubborn, wild, defiant, disobedient, headstrong
📝 The **docile** students listened quietly throughout the long lecture.

**DORMANT** | নিষ্ক্রিয়, সুপ্ত, ঘুমন্ত অবস্থায়
Temporarily inactive; in a state of sleep or rest; latent.
✅ inactive, sleeping, latent, quiescent, resting, suspended, hibernating
❌ active, awake, functioning, alert, operational, alive, vigorous
📝 The volcano had been **dormant** for over a hundred years before it erupted.

**DUBIOUS** | সংশয়ী, সন্দেহজনক, অনিশ্চিত
Hesitating or doubting; not to be relied on; of questionable quality.
✅ doubtful, questionable, uncertain, suspicious, unreliable, skeptical, unsure
❌ certain, definite, reliable, trustworthy, confident, convinced, clear
📝 The scientist remained **dubious** about the extraordinary claims in the report.

**DUPLICITY** | ধোঁকাবাজি, প্রতারণা, দুই মুখো স্বভাব
Deceitfulness; saying one thing while meaning another; double-dealing.
✅ deceit, treachery, dishonesty, double-dealing, fraud, deception, guile
❌ honesty, sincerity, integrity, faithfulness, candor, openness
📝 His **duplicity** was exposed when both sides of the story were compared.

## 🔤 E

**EBULLIENT** | উচ্ছ্বসিত, আনন্দে উত্তেজিত, প্রাণবন্ত
Cheerful and full of energy; bubbling with enthusiasm or excitement.
✅ enthusiastic, exuberant, lively, spirited, animated, buoyant, vivacious
❌ subdued, gloomy, depressed, dull, listless, apathetic, withdrawn
📝 Her **ebullient** personality made every team meeting more productive and fun.

**EGREGIOUS** | ভয়াবহভাবে খারাপ, চরম ভুল, জঘন্য
Outstandingly bad; shockingly wrong or offensive; flagrant.
✅ outrageous, shocking, flagrant, appalling, terrible, glaring, monstrous, appalling
❌ minor, trivial, slight, acceptable, insignificant, negligible, small
📝 The **egregious** accounting errors cost the firm millions of dollars.

**ELATE / ELATED** | উৎফুল্ল করা / উৎফুল্ল, আনন্দিত
To make someone very happy and proud; feeling ecstatic and overjoyed.
✅ overjoyed, thrilled, ecstatic, jubilant, exhilarated, delighted, joyful
❌ dejected, sad, depressed, disappointed, gloomy, miserable, downcast
📝 She was **elated** when she found out she had been offered the scholarship.

**ELEVATE** | উন্নত করা, উপরে তোলা
To raise to a higher level; to improve in position, rank, or quality.
✅ raise, lift, promote, advance, improve, upgrade, boost, exalt
❌ lower, demote, decrease, reduce, degrade, drop, sink
📝 Reading regularly helps to **elevate** one's vocabulary and writing skills.

**ELUSIVE** | অধরা, দুর্গম, ধরা-ছোঁয়ার বাইরে
Difficult to find, catch, or achieve; hard to pin down or understand.
✅ evasive, baffling, hard to catch, fleeting, slippery, intangible, mysterious
❌ clear, obvious, accessible, attainable, reachable, straightforward, findable
📝 True contentment can be **elusive** when we keep chasing material things.

**EMINENT** | বিখ্যাত, বিশিষ্ট, প্রতিষ্ঠিত
Respected and famous, especially for achievements in a particular field.
✅ famous, renowned, distinguished, notable, respected, prominent, celebrated, illustrious
❌ unknown, obscure, ordinary, insignificant, anonymous, unrecognized
📝 The **eminent** professor was invited to deliver the keynote address.

**ENMITY** | শত্রুতা, বিদ্বেষ, দুশমনি
A state of deep hostility or strong dislike between people or groups.
✅ hostility, hatred, animosity, antagonism, acrimony, ill will, bitterness
❌ friendship, goodwill, harmony, peace, love, affection, camaraderie
📝 The **enmity** between the two clans had lasted for over three generations.

**ENUMERATE** | গণনা করা, তালিকা করা, একে একে বলা
To mention things one by one; to count or list items in order.
✅ list, count, itemize, specify, detail, catalogue, name, identify
❌ generalize, summarize, overlook, omit, ignore, group
📝 The teacher asked students to **enumerate** the causes of World War I.

**EPHEMERAL** | ক্ষণস্থায়ী, অস্থায়ী, ক্ষণভঙ্গুর
Lasting for a very short time; short-lived; existing only briefly.
✅ transient, fleeting, temporary, brief, short-lived, transitory, passing
❌ permanent, eternal, lasting, enduring, perpetual, everlasting, durable
📝 Social media fame is often **ephemeral** — here today and forgotten tomorrow.

**ERADICATE** | উচ্ছেদ করা, নির্মূল করা
To destroy something completely; to put an end to something; to root out.
✅ eliminate, destroy, remove, abolish, wipe out, exterminate, uproot
❌ preserve, protect, maintain, cultivate, nurture, support, keep
📝 The global health campaign aimed to **eradicate** polio worldwide by 2030.

**ERRATIC** | অনিয়মিত, অপ্রত্যাশিত, অস্থির
Not even or regular in pattern; unpredictable and inconsistent.
✅ unpredictable, irregular, inconsistent, random, unstable, capricious, changeable
❌ consistent, regular, predictable, steady, stable, reliable, constant
📝 His **erratic** behavior began to worry even his closest friends.

**EVADE** | এড়িয়ে যাওয়া, ফাঁকি দেওয়া
To escape or avoid something, especially in a clever or dishonest way.
✅ avoid, escape, dodge, elude, sidestep, circumvent, shun, flee
❌ face, confront, address, meet, tackle, encounter, deal with
📝 He tried to **evade** the question by changing the subject.

**EXASPERATE** | বিরক্ত করা, উত্তেজিত করা
To irritate someone intensely; to make someone extremely annoyed.
✅ irritate, annoy, frustrate, anger, infuriate, provoke, aggravate, enrage
❌ please, soothe, calm, pacify, delight, satisfy, comfort
📝 His constant interruptions began to **exasperate** the entire meeting room.

**EXONERATE** | দায়মুক্ত করা, নিরপরাধ ঘোষণা করা
To officially absolve someone from blame or criminal charges; to clear.
✅ absolve, acquit, clear, pardon, excuse, vindicate, free, release
❌ convict, blame, charge, condemn, accuse, indict, hold responsible
📝 New DNA evidence fully **exonerated** the man wrongly imprisoned for eight years.

**EXPEDITIOUS** | দ্রুত, তৎপর, কার্যকর
Done with speed and efficiency; prompt and effective in action.
✅ speedy, swift, prompt, efficient, quick, fast, brisk, immediate
❌ slow, sluggish, delayed, leisurely, tardy, inefficient, lazy
📝 The fire crew's **expeditious** response saved the building from total collapse.

**EXPLICIT** | স্পষ্ট, খোলাখুলি, নির্দিষ্ট
Stated clearly and in detail, leaving no room for confusion or doubt.
✅ clear, direct, specific, definite, unambiguous, straightforward, precise, stated
❌ implicit, vague, ambiguous, unclear, hidden, indirect, unstated
📝 The contract had **explicit** terms about what would happen if deadlines were missed.

**EXPUNGE** | মুছে ফেলা, বাতিল করা
To erase or remove completely; to obliterate or eliminate from a record.
✅ erase, delete, remove, obliterate, cancel, eliminate, wipe out, strike out
❌ add, include, restore, preserve, retain, keep, maintain, record
📝 He petitioned the court to **expunge** the minor offence from his record.

**EXTINCT** | বিলুপ্ত, অস্তিত্বহীন
No longer in existence; having died out completely; no longer active.
✅ defunct, vanished, obsolete, dead, lost, exterminated, wiped out, gone
❌ existing, alive, surviving, current, thriving, active, living
📝 Scientists have discovered fossils of creatures that went **extinct** millions of years ago.

**EXUBERANT** | উচ্ছ্বসিত, প্রাণবন্ত, আনন্দময়
Filled with lively energy and excitement; extremely enthusiastic and vigorous.
✅ lively, enthusiastic, vibrant, energetic, spirited, buoyant, ebullient, cheerful
❌ subdued, dull, depressed, listless, gloomy, apathetic, quiet
📝 The children gave an **exuberant** performance at the school annual function.

## 🔤 F

**FABRICATE** | মিথ্যা তৈরি করা, বানিয়ে বলা; নির্মাণ করা
To invent false information in order to deceive; also, to manufacture.
✅ invent, forge, fake, falsify, make up, concoct, create, manufacture
❌ reveal, expose, admit, confirm, authenticate, acknowledge
📝 He was caught trying to **fabricate** an alibi to avoid suspicion.

**FALLACIOUS** | ভ্রান্তিমূলক, মিথ্যা যুক্তিভিত্তিক
Based on a mistaken belief; logically unsound or misleading.
✅ false, misleading, erroneous, deceptive, flawed, unsound, incorrect, wrong
❌ correct, valid, sound, truthful, accurate, logical, factual
📝 The defense attorney dismantled the **fallacious** assumptions in the prosecution's case.

**FATHOM** | বোঝা, অনুধাবন করা; গভীরতা মাপা
To understand a difficult problem after much thought; to measure depth.
✅ understand, comprehend, grasp, decipher, penetrate, work out, figure out
❌ misunderstand, confuse, miss, ignore, overlook, be baffled by
📝 She couldn't **fathom** why the committee had rejected such a strong proposal.

**FATUOUS** | নির্বোধ, বোকামিপূর্ণ, অর্থহীন
Silly and pointless; showing a lack of intelligence; foolish and self-complacent.
✅ silly, stupid, foolish, idiotic, irrational, senseless, inane, vapid
❌ intelligent, clever, sensible, wise, thoughtful, rational, bright
📝 His **fatuous** response to the crisis showed he had no real plan at all.

**FEALTY** | বিশ্বস্ততা, আনুগত্য, ভক্তি
Loyalty and faithfulness; allegiance owed to a superior or cause.
✅ loyalty, faithfulness, devotion, allegiance, commitment, fidelity, dedication
❌ disloyalty, betrayal, treachery, faithlessness, desertion, rebellion
📝 The soldiers swore **fealty** to their commander before the battle.

**FECKLESS** | অলক্ষ্য, উদ্দেশ্যহীন, দায়িত্বহীন
Lacking initiative, determination, or strength; irresponsible and ineffective.
✅ aimless, irresponsible, careless, purposeless, helpless, futile, ineffective
❌ responsible, capable, determined, purposeful, effective, reliable
📝 His **feckless** attitude toward money left him in debt every month.

**FEEBLE** | দুর্বল, ক্ষীণ, অশক্ত
Lacking physical or mental strength; weak; unconvincing and ineffective.
✅ weak, frail, delicate, faint, infirm, powerless, fragile, helpless
❌ strong, powerful, robust, sturdy, vigorous, healthy, energetic
📝 His **feeble** excuse for missing the deadline convinced no one.

**FEROCIOUS** | হিংস্র, ভয়ংকর, ক্রূর
Savagely fierce or violent; extreme and intense in degree.
✅ fierce, savage, brutal, violent, vicious, ruthless, wild, dangerous
❌ gentle, tame, mild, peaceful, kind, calm, harmless
📝 The **ferocious** storm tore roofs off buildings across the entire district.

**FERVENT** | আবেগপ্রবণ, উদ্যমী, তীব্র অনুভূতিসম্পন্ন
Having or displaying a passionate intensity; very enthusiastic and devoted.
✅ passionate, ardent, intense, enthusiastic, devoted, zealous, sincere, burning
❌ indifferent, apathetic, cold, half-hearted, lukewarm, unenthusiastic
📝 She delivered a **fervent** speech that moved the audience to tears.

**FICTITIOUS** | কাল্পনিক, বানোয়াট, অবাস্তব
Created, invented, or imagined rather than being real; made up; false.
✅ imaginary, made-up, invented, false, fabricated, fake, unreal, fictional
❌ real, true, factual, genuine, authentic, actual, existing
📝 The report was based on **fictitious** data that had never been collected.

**FIDELITY** | বিশ্বস্ততা, আনুগত্য, সত্যনিষ্ঠা
Faithfulness to a person, cause, or belief; loyalty and accuracy.
✅ loyalty, faithfulness, devotion, allegiance, commitment, constancy, dedication
❌ infidelity, disloyalty, betrayal, treachery, faithlessness, inconstancy
📝 His **fidelity** to the truth, even when it was uncomfortable, earned him great respect.

**FIENDISH** | শয়তানী, অত্যন্ত নিষ্ঠুর, দুষ্ট
Extremely cruel or wicked; devilish; very complex and difficult.
✅ cruel, devilish, wicked, diabolical, monstrous, barbaric, sadistic, brutal
❌ kind, gentle, humane, saintly, angelic, benevolent, good
📝 The **fiendish** puzzle took the team of experts three days to solve.

**FILCH** | চুরি করা, লুকিয়ে নেওয়া
To steal something, especially small items in a petty way; to pilfer.
✅ steal, pilfer, take, pocket, swipe, lift, pinch, embezzle
❌ return, restore, give back, replace, repay
📝 Someone had **filched** the pens from the reception desk again.

**FLAGRANT** | প্রকাশ্যে জঘন্য, নির্লজ্জ, স্পষ্টভাবে আপত্তিকর
Conspicuously and obviously offensive; shockingly noticeable.
✅ obvious, blatant, outrageous, glaring, brazen, shameless, notorious, overt
❌ minor, subtle, hidden, concealed, discreet, mild, slight
📝 The **flagrant** violation of the agreement shocked all parties involved.

**FLIMSY** | দুর্বল, পলকা, অসার
Weak and easily damaged or destroyed; unconvincing and not serious.
✅ weak, fragile, insubstantial, thin, delicate, frail, inadequate, feeble
❌ strong, sturdy, solid, robust, durable, substantial, firm
📝 Her **flimsy** excuse for the delay fooled no one in the room.

**FLOURISH** | সমৃদ্ধ হওয়া, ফুলে ফেঁপে ওঠা, সফল হওয়া
To grow or develop in a healthy, vigorous way; to prosper; to thrive.
✅ thrive, prosper, bloom, succeed, grow, blossom, develop, advance
❌ wither, decline, fail, struggle, decay, perish, die, stagnate
📝 Independent bookshops continue to **flourish** in smaller towns and cities.

**FOE** | শত্রু, প্রতিপক্ষ, দুশমন
An enemy or opponent; one who is hostile or opposed to you.
✅ enemy, opponent, adversary, rival, antagonist, nemesis, competitor
❌ friend, ally, supporter, companion, partner, aide
📝 Even the most experienced warrior once had to face a powerful **foe**.

**FORTE** | বিশেষ দক্ষতা, শক্তির জায়গা
A thing at which someone excels; a particular area of strength or ability.
✅ strength, specialty, talent, gift, skill, ability, expertise, strong suit
❌ weakness, limitation, flaw, shortcoming, inadequacy, failing
📝 Public speaking has always been her **forte** in any professional setting.

**FRAIL** | ভঙ্গুর, দুর্বল, নাজুক
Weak and delicate in health or structure; easily broken or damaged.
✅ weak, delicate, fragile, feeble, infirm, slight, slender, vulnerable
❌ strong, robust, sturdy, healthy, durable, tough, vigorous
📝 The **frail** old man needed assistance getting up from his chair.

**FRIVOLOUS** | অর্থহীন, তুচ্ছ, গুরুত্বহীন
Not having any serious purpose or value; treating serious issues with humor.
✅ trivial, unimportant, silly, superficial, petty, irrelevant, pointless, glib
❌ serious, important, significant, meaningful, substantial, grave, weighty
📝 The judge dismissed the lawsuit as entirely **frivolous** and time-wasting.

**FRUGAL** | মিতব্যয়ী, সাশ্রয়ী
Sparing or economical with money or food; not wasteful or extravagant.
✅ economical, thrifty, careful, prudent, sparing, modest, saving, stingy
❌ extravagant, wasteful, lavish, generous, profligate, reckless, spendthrift
📝 Living a **frugal** lifestyle helped him save enough to start his own business.

**FUTILE** | বৃথা, নিষ্ফল, অর্থহীন
Incapable of producing any useful result; pointless and ineffective.
✅ useless, pointless, vain, fruitless, ineffective, wasteful, hopeless, worthless
❌ effective, worthwhile, productive, successful, useful, meaningful, rewarding
📝 Every attempt to reach the stranded climbers proved completely **futile**.

**FURIOUS / FURY** | ক্রুদ্ধ / তীব্র রাগ, ক্রোধ
Extremely angry; violently intense; (fury) wild or violent anger.
✅ enraged, livid, infuriated, irate, incensed, outraged, seething
❌ calm, pleased, content, happy, satisfied, composed, serene
📝 He was absolutely **furious** when he discovered his wallet had been stolen.

## 🔤 G

**GALLANT** | সাহসী, বীর, ভদ্র ও সম্মানজনক
Brave and noble; showing courage; politely attentive to women.
✅ brave, courageous, heroic, noble, chivalrous, valiant, bold, daring
❌ cowardly, fearful, rude, ignoble, timid, weak, disrespectful
📝 The **gallant** officer led his troops through the most dangerous part of the battle.

**GARRULOUS** | বাচাল, বকবকে, বেশি কথার
Excessively talkative, especially on trivial matters; chatty and long-winded.
✅ talkative, loquacious, chatty, verbose, long-winded, wordy, babbling
❌ taciturn, quiet, silent, reserved, reticent, concise, brief
📝 The **garrulous** candidate spent more time talking than actually listening.

**GAUCHE** | আনাড়ি, ফূলিশ, সামাজিকভাবে অদক্ষ
Lacking social grace; awkward and unsophisticated in manner.
✅ clumsy, awkward, tactless, unsophisticated, inept, ungainly, uncouth
❌ graceful, sophisticated, elegant, poised, polished, refined, tactful
📝 His **gauche** behaviour at the formal dinner made everyone uncomfortable.

**GREGARIOUS** | মিশুক, সামাজিক, দলপ্রিয়
Fond of company; sociable; living in groups; enjoying being with others.
✅ sociable, friendly, outgoing, convivial, social, amiable, extroverted, open
❌ unsociable, reclusive, introverted, solitary, aloof, antisocial, withdrawn
📝 Her **gregarious** nature meant she never stayed a stranger for long.

**HAUGHTY** | অহংকারী, ঔদ্ধত্যপূর্ণ, দম্ভী

✅ arrogant, proud, conceited, condescending, supercilious, overbearing, pompous
❌ humble, modest, respectful, polite, unassuming, down-to-earth, meek
📝 The **haughty** manner of the official put every visitor on edge.

Unfriendly and aggressive; showing opposition or resistance; like an enemy.
✅ unfriendly, aggressive, antagonistic, belligerent, combative, cold, warlike
❌ friendly, welcoming, supportive, amicable, cordial, warm, cooperative
📝 The proposal met with a **hostile** response from the local community.

The friendly and generous reception and entertainment of guests.
✅ warmth, welcome, generosity, friendliness, accommodation, graciousness
❌ coldness, hostility, rudeness, inhospitality, unfriendliness
📝 The family was renowned for their **hospitality** during festival seasons.

## 🔤 I

**IDLE** | অলস, কর্মহীন, বেকার
Not working or active; lazy; having no purpose or value.
✅ inactive, lazy, sluggish, unoccupied, jobless, unused, dormant
❌ active, busy, productive, employed, working, diligent, energetic
📝 He refused to spend even one **idle** day during the summer break.

**IGNOMINIOUS** | লজ্জাজনক, অপমানজনক, ধিক্কারের
Deserving or causing public disgrace or shame; deeply humiliating.
✅ shameful, disgraceful, humiliating, dishonorable, degrading, abject, scandalous
❌ honorable, glorious, dignified, respectful, praiseworthy, celebrated
📝 The champion's **ignominious** defeat was front-page news the next morning.

**IGNOMINIOUS** | লজ্জাজনক
see also: IGNOBLE | অখ্যাত, অসম্মানজনক
Lacking honor or nobility; dishonorable or shameful; unworthy.
✅ dishonorable, base, contemptible, shameful, unworthy, degrading
❌ noble, honorable, dignified, admirable, worthy, prestigious
📝 Betraying a friend for personal gain is truly **ignoble**.

**IMMACULATE** | নিখুঁত, দাগহীন, ত্রুটিমুক্ত
Perfectly clean, neat, or tidy; free from flaws or mistakes; spotless.
✅ spotless, flawless, perfect, pristine, pure, unblemished, impeccable, clean
❌ dirty, impure, flawed, stained, messy, imperfect, tarnished
📝 Her **immaculate** presentation left the judges with nothing to criticize.

**IMMENSE** | বিশাল, অতিকায়, প্রচুর
Extremely large or great; huge in size, scale, or degree.
✅ enormous, vast, colossal, massive, huge, gigantic, mammoth, boundless
❌ tiny, small, insignificant, miniature, negligible, slight, minor
📝 The project required an **immense** amount of time and resources to complete.

**IMMUNE** | রোগ প্রতিরোধী, সুরক্ষিত
Protected against something through previous exposure or by nature.
✅ resistant, protected, invulnerable, exempt, insusceptible, unsusceptible
❌ vulnerable, susceptible, unprotected, exposed, open, prone
📝 Some people believe they are **immune** to criticism when they are in power.

**IMMUTABLE** | অপরিবর্তনীয়, অচল
Unchanging over time; not able to be changed; fixed and permanent.
✅ unchanging, fixed, permanent, constant, invariable, inflexible, unalterable
❌ changeable, flexible, alterable, variable, adaptable, mutable
📝 The laws of physics are considered **immutable** constants of the universe.

**IMPARITY / IMPARTIAL** | বৈষম্য / নিরপেক্ষ
(Imparity) Inequality or difference; (Impartial) treating all equally and fairly.
✅ (impartial) fair, unbiased, neutral, objective, just, even-handed, equitable
❌ biased, prejudiced, partial, unfair, one-sided, subjective
📝 A judge must remain completely **impartial** in all matters that come before the court.

**IMPECCABLE** | নিখুঁত, নিষ্কলুষ, ত্রুটিহীন
Without fault or error; perfect in every respect and way.
✅ perfect, flawless, faultless, spotless, immaculate, irreproachable, excellent
❌ flawed, imperfect, faulty, defective, blemished, inferior, sloppy
📝 The chef had an **impeccable** reputation for the quality of his food.

**IMPERATIVE** | অপরিহার্য, অত্যাবশ্যক, বাধ্যতামূলক
Of vital importance; absolutely necessary and urgent; mandatory.
✅ necessary, essential, mandatory, compulsory, urgent, vital, required, critical
❌ optional, unnecessary, voluntary, avoidable, non-essential, unimportant
📝 It is **imperative** that all safety protocols be followed without exception.

**IMPETUOUS** | তাড়াহুড়ো, আবেগপ্রবণ, বেপরোয়া
Acting or done quickly without thought or care; hasty and rash.
✅ impulsive, rash, hasty, reckless, sudden, spontaneous, brash, headstrong
❌ cautious, deliberate, careful, thoughtful, restrained, prudent, measured
📝 His **impetuous** decision to invest all his savings in a single stock backfired badly.

**IMPEDE** | বাধা দেওয়া, রোধ করা
To delay or prevent someone or something by obstructing them; to hinder.
✅ hinder, obstruct, block, hamper, slow, restrain, interfere with, prevent
❌ assist, aid, facilitate, help, support, advance, enable, encourage
📝 The flooded road **impeded** the progress of the relief convoy.

**IMPLICIT** | অন্তর্নিহিত, পরোক্ষভাবে বোঝানো
Suggested though not directly expressed; implied; total and unquestioning.
✅ implied, inferred, understood, tacit, unspoken, indirect, inherent, latent
❌ explicit, stated, direct, clear, expressed, obvious, declared
📝 There was an **implicit** understanding that she would lead the next project.

**IMPLORE** | মিনতি করা, অনুনয় করা
To beg someone earnestly and desperately; to plead urgently.
✅ beg, plead, beseech, entreat, appeal, supplicate, pray, urge
❌ demand, order, command, insist, dictate, require
📝 She **implored** the court to show mercy toward her elderly father.

**IMPOSING** | মনোমুগ্ধকর, চিত্তাকর্ষক, বিশাল এবং গুরুত্বপূর্ণ
Grand and impressive in appearance; large and striking in size or manner.
✅ impressive, grand, majestic, commanding, magnificent, formidable, stunning
❌ unimpressive, humble, small, insignificant, modest, plain, ordinary
📝 The **imposing** cathedral dominated the skyline of the old city.

**IMPROPRIETY** | অনুপযুক্ততা, অশোভনীয় আচরণ
Behaviour or language that is improper, indecent, or unsuitable.
✅ indecency, misconduct, unsuitability, incorrectness, indiscretion, misconduct
❌ propriety, decency, correctness, appropriateness, suitability, decorum
📝 The committee reviewed the **impropriety** of the official's financial decisions.

**IMPUDENT / IMPUDENCE** | ঔদ্ধত্যপূর্ণ, বেয়াদব
Not showing due respect; brazenly disrespectful or impertinent.
✅ rude, disrespectful, insolent, impertinent, brazen, cheeky, bold, shameless
❌ respectful, polite, deferential, courteous, humble, considerate
📝 His **impudent** reply to the senior officer shocked everyone present.

**IMPULSIVE** | আবেগপ্রবণ, সহসা কাজ করে যে
Acting suddenly on a feeling without thinking about consequences.
✅ hasty, rash, reckless, impetuous, spontaneous, snap, instinctive, unplanned
❌ deliberate, careful, calculated, thoughtful, measured, cautious, planned
📝 She made an **impulsive** purchase and regretted it the very next day.

**INAPT / INEPT** | অদক্ষ, অযোগ্য, অপটু
(Inept) Having or showing no skill; incompetent and clumsy.
✅ incompetent, unskilled, clumsy, awkward, incapable, inefficient, hopeless
❌ skilled, capable, competent, adept, proficient, talented, able
📝 The **inept** handling of the crisis made the situation far worse.

**INCESSANT** | অবিরাম, নিরন্তর, অবিরত
Never stopping; continuing without pause or interruption; relentless.
✅ continuous, constant, ceaseless, unrelenting, perpetual, endless, non-stop
❌ intermittent, occasional, sporadic, brief, periodic, rare, broken
📝 The **incessant** barking of dogs kept the whole neighbourhood awake.

**INCLINATION** | ঝোঁক, প্রবণতা, আগ্রহ
A person's natural tendency or urge to act in a particular way.

❌ disinclination, reluctance, aversion, dislike, unwillingness, resistance
📝 She had a strong **inclination** toward the arts from a very young age.

**INDIFFERENT** | উদাসীন, নিরপেক্ষ, নির্বিকার
Having no particular interest or sympathy; unconcerned and neutral.
✅ unconcerned, apathetic, aloof, detached, cold, uninterested, neutral, unmoved
❌ concerned, passionate, caring, enthusiastic, interested, warm, involved
📝 He appeared completely **indifferent** to the suffering happening around him.

**INDIGENOUS** | স্থানীয়, দেশীয়, আদিবাসী
Originating or occurring naturally in a particular place; native.
✅ native, local, original, endemic, homegrown, aboriginal, domestic, autochthonous
❌ alien, foreign, imported, non-native, introduced, exotic, invasive

**INDIGENT** | দরিদ্র, অভাবগ্রস্ত, নিঃস্ব
Poor and lacking basic necessities; destitute; in dire financial need.

❌ wealthy, affluent, rich, prosperous, comfortable, well-off
📝 The shelter provides meals and clothing for **indigent** families every night.

Impossible to subdue or defeat; too strong to be overcome; invincible.
✅ unconquerable, invincible, unbeatable, resolute, determined, fearless, steadfast
❌ weak, submissive, easily defeated, yielding, fragile, powerless
📝 Her **indomitable** spirit kept her going through years of hardship.

**INEVITABLE** | অনিবার্য, অপরিহার্য, অবশ্যম্ভাবী
Certain to happen; unable to be avoided, stopped, or prevented.

❌ avoidable, preventable, uncertain, possible to avoid, escapable
📝 Change is **inevitable** — those who adapt thrive and those who resist struggle.

Well known for some bad quality or deed; notorious; having a very bad reputation.
✅ notorious, disreputable, scandalous, ignominious, ill-famed, disgraced
❌ reputable, famous for good, respected, celebrated, admirable, honorable
📝 The **infamous** criminal had been evading authorities for a decade.

**INGENIOUS** | বুদ্ধিমান, সৃজনশীল, উদ্ভাবনী
Clever, original, and inventive; showing great creative intelligence.
✅ clever, inventive, creative, resourceful, brilliant, original, smart, imaginative
❌ dull, unimaginative, ordinary, stupid, uncreative, pedestrian
📝 The **ingenious** water filtration system cost almost nothing to build.

Not harmful or offensive; completely harmless; unlikely to cause controversy.
✅ harmless, safe, inoffensive, benign, mild, gentle, non-threatening
❌ harmful, dangerous, offensive, toxic, hazardous, hurtful
📝 What seemed like a completely **innocuous** question caused an unexpected argument.

In a state of mind that prevents normal perception or behavior; crazy.
✅ mad, deranged, crazy, lunatic, demented, unhinged, psychotic, irrational
❌ sane, rational, lucid, sensible, normal, balanced, clearheaded
📝 The detective argued that temporary **insanity** had driven the defendant to act.

**INSOLENT** | ঔদ্ধত্যপূর্ণ, অসভ্য, বেয়াদব
Showing a rude and arrogant lack of respect; impudent and disrespectful.
✅ rude, arrogant, impudent, disrespectful, brazen, insubordinate, contemptuous
❌ polite, respectful, humble, courteous, deferential, well-mannered
📝 His **insolent** tone during the interview immediately ended his chances.

**INSULAR** | সংকীর্ণমনা, বিচ্ছিন্ন, একাকীত্বপ্রবণ
Ignorant of or uninterested in cultures or ideas outside one's own narrow world.
✅ isolated, narrow-minded, provincial, parochial, detached, introverted, closed
❌ open-minded, worldly, broadminded, outgoing, cosmopolitan, curious
📝 His **insular** worldview prevented him from understanding diverse perspectives.

**INTEGRATE / INTEGRITY** | একত্রিত করা / সততা, নৈতিক শক্তি
(Integrate) To combine; (Integrity) the quality of being honest and having strong moral principles.
✅ (integrity) honesty, honor, virtue, sincerity, uprightness, ethics, truthfulness
❌ dishonesty, corruption, fraud, deception, immorality, duplicity

Occurring at irregular intervals; not continuous or steady; sporadic.
✅ sporadic, occasional, irregular, periodic, on-and-off, fitful, erratic
❌ continuous, constant, unbroken, incessant, steady, regular, non-stop

Very complicated or detailed; having many small parts or elements.
✅ complicated, complex, detailed, elaborate, convoluted, tangled, delicate
❌ simple, straightforward, easy, plain, uncomplicated, basic

Fearless; adventurous; not frightened even in dangerous situations.
✅ fearless, brave, bold, courageous, dauntless, daring, valiant, audacious
❌ timid, cowardly, fearful, scared, hesitant, spineless, weak
📝 The **intrepid** journalist entered the war zone to report the truth.

**IRATE** | ক্রুদ্ধ, রাগান্বিত, উত্তেজিত
Feeling or characterized by great anger; furious and very upset.
✅ angry, furious, enraged, infuriated, outraged, livid, incensed, seething
❌ calm, pleased, content, happy, satisfied, composed, peaceful

Having or showing a tendency to get angry easily; irritable.
✅ irritable, hot-tempered, short-fused, cantankerous, grumpy, bad-tempered
❌ calm, even-tempered, patient, placid, mild, easygoing, serene
📝 The **irascible** coach shouted at players for even the smallest mistake.

Not connected with or relevant to something; not related to the matter at hand.

❌ relevant, applicable, pertinent, related, connected, important, fitting
📝 His response was completely **irrelevant** to the question that was asked.

Not sufficiently strict, severe, or careful; slack in discipline or standards.
✅ loose, lenient, slack, careless, negligent, permissive, sloppy, weak

📝 The **lax** security at the venue made the event organizers nervous.

**LENIENT** | সহানুভূতিশীল, নমনীয়, দয়ালু
More merciful or tolerant than expected; not strict in applying discipline.
✅ merciful, tolerant, easy-going, mild, forgiving, gentle, compassionate
❌ strict, harsh, severe, tough, rigorous, unforgiving, stern
📝 The judge was **lenient** with first-time offenders who showed genuine remorse.

**LETHARGIC** | অলস, নিস্তেজ, ঘুম ঘুম ভাব

❌ energetic, active, lively, alert, animated, vigorous, enthusiastic

**LISTLESS** | নিস্তেজ, উদ্যমহীন, প্রাণহীন
Lacking energy, enthusiasm, or interest; showing little desire to do anything.
✅ lethargic, languid, sluggish, apathetic, unenthusiastic, weary, dull
❌ energetic, enthusiastic, lively, active, spirited, motivated, keen

**LOQUACIOUS** | বাচাল, বেশি কথা বলে, কথকতায় পটু
Tending to talk a great deal; extremely talkative; very fond of talking.
✅ talkative, garrulous, chatty, verbose, wordy, long-winded, voluble
❌ silent, quiet, reserved, taciturn, reticent, brief, succinct

**LUCID** | স্পষ্ট, বোধগম্য, পরিষ্কার
Expressed clearly; easy to understand; showing clear thinking; transparent.
✅ clear, understandable, intelligible, coherent, transparent, obvious, comprehensible

## 🔤 M

**MAGNANIMOUS** | উদারমনা, মহানুভব, বড় হৃদয়ের

❌ petty, mean, selfish, unforgiving, stingy, resentful, small-minded
📝 The **magnanimous** champion praised his rival even after defeating him.

**MALEVOLENT** | বদ উদ্দেশ্যের, ক্ষতিকামী, বিদ্বেষী
Having or showing a wish to do evil to others; deeply malicious.
✅ malicious, evil, spiteful, vindictive, hostile, hateful, vicious, wicked
❌ benevolent, kind, good-natured, caring, friendly, helpful, loving
📝 The **malevolent** force behind the scheme was eventually exposed.

**MALIGN** | ক্ষতিকর, বদ, দোষারোপ করা
Evil in nature or effect; to say harmful and untrue things about someone.
✅ harmful, evil, slander, defame, criticize falsely, smear, vilify
❌ good, benign, praise, defend, commend, protect, uplift
📝 His enemies worked hard to **malign** his reputation in the press.

**MALIGNANT** | মারাত্মক, ক্ষতিকর, ঘাতক
Very virulent or infectious; causing harm; (of a disease) likely to spread and kill.
✅ harmful, dangerous, deadly, malevolent, pernicious, virulent, lethal
❌ benign, harmless, good-natured, kind, non-threatening, curable

**MANEUVER** | কৌশল, চালাকি, সরানো
A skillful move or series of moves; to move cleverly; a strategic action.
✅ strategy, tactic, scheme, plan, move, ploy, manipulation, orchestrate

📝 The negotiator used a clever **maneuver** to shift the discussion in his favor.

**MANIFEST** | স্পষ্ট করা, প্রকাশ করা, দৃশ্যমান
To display or show a quality or feeling; to make something evident.
✅ show, display, reveal, demonstrate, express, exhibit, indicate, make clear
❌ hide, conceal, suppress, cover, disguise, obscure, mask
📝 Her excitement began to **manifest** in the form of a wide, uncontrollable smile.

**MELANCHOLY** | বিষণ্নতা, মনমরা ভাব, বিষাদ
A feeling of pensive sadness with no obvious cause; a gloomy state of mind.
✅ sadness, gloom, sorrow, depression, dejection, wistfulness, mournfulness
❌ happiness, joy, cheerfulness, elation, optimism, delight, euphoria
📝 The rainy season brought with it a deep, unexplained **melancholy**.

**METICULOUS** | নিখুঁত, অত্যন্ত সতর্ক, সূক্ষ্মদর্শী
Showing great attention to detail or correct behaviour; very careful and precise.
✅ careful, precise, thorough, detailed, scrupulous, painstaking, exact, fastidious
❌ careless, sloppy, negligent, rough, haphazard, inattentive, casual
📝 The **meticulous** restorer spent weeks on a single painting's corner detail.

**MISCREANT** | দুষ্কৃতিকারী, অপরাধী, দুর্বৃত্ত
A person who has done something wrong or illegal; a villain or criminal.
✅ criminal, villain, wrongdoer, offender, troublemaker, menace, delinquent
❌ hero, upright person, honest person, law-abiding citizen, saint
📝 The **miscreant** who vandalized the memorial was identified on CCTV.

**MISANTHROPIST** | মানববিদ্বেষী
A person who dislikes humankind and avoids human society.
✅ cynic, recluse, hermit, hater of people, antisocial person
❌ philanthropist, humanitarian, people-lover, altruist, social person
📝 After years of betrayal, he became a bitter **misanthropist** who trusted no one.

**MISERLY** | কৃপণ, কঞ্জুস
Unwilling to spend money; excessively stingy and greedy about money.
✅ stingy, mean, greedy, tight-fisted, niggardly, avaricious, cheap, close-fisted
❌ generous, charitable, lavish, giving, open-handed, liberal, big-hearted
📝 Despite being a millionaire, he was so **miserly** that he refused to heat his home.

**MITIGATE** | লাঘব করা, কমানো, হ্রাস করা
To make less severe, serious, or painful; to reduce the impact or intensity.
✅ lessen, reduce, alleviate, diminish, ease, moderate, soften, relieve
❌ worsen, aggravate, intensify, increase, magnify, exacerbate, heighten
📝 Early detection can help to **mitigate** the effects of many serious diseases.

**MONOTONOUS** | একঘেয়ে, নিরস, পুনরাবৃত্তিমূলক
Dull, tedious, and repetitiously lacking in variety and interest.
✅ boring, tedious, dull, repetitive, unvarying, tiresome, dreary, humdrum
❌ interesting, varied, exciting, lively, stimulating, dynamic, engaging
📝 The **monotonous** job of sorting mail became unbearable after the third week.

**MUNDANE** | সাধারণ, একঘেয়ে, পার্থিব
Lacking interest or excitement; ordinary, routine, and unimaginative.
✅ ordinary, routine, everyday, dull, commonplace, prosaic, banal, average
❌ extraordinary, exciting, unusual, interesting, fascinating, remarkable
📝 She longed for adventure and hated the **mundane** repetition of office work.

✅ outdated, old-fashioned, archaic, antiquated, outmoded, defunct, obsolescent

Stubbornly refusing to change one's opinion or action; bull-headed.

**OMINOUS** | অশুভ, ভয়প্রদর্শনকারী, অমঙ্গলের ইঙ্গিতবাহী

✅ burdensome, difficult, laborious, taxing, arduous, troublesome, heavy
❌ easy, light, effortless, simple, manageable, pleasant, comfortable

**OPULENT** | ঐশ্বর্যশালী, বিলাসবহুল, সমৃদ্ধ
Ostentatiously rich and luxurious; showing great wealth and splendour.
✅ wealthy, luxurious, lavish, grand, prosperous, rich, magnificent, sumptuous
❌ poor, modest, simple, austere, destitute, plain, frugal, ordinary
📝 The **opulent** mansion had twenty rooms, five gardens, and a private pool.

**ORNATE** | অলঙ্কৃত, বিস্তারিত সাজানো, জাঁকজমকপূর্ণ
Elaborately ornamented or decorated; complex and intricate in design.
✅ elaborate, decorated, fancy, embellished, intricate, showy, detailed, ornamental
❌ simple, plain, bare, understated, unadorned, minimal, stark
📝 The **ornate** ceiling of the palace took craftsmen over a decade to complete.

**OSSIFY** | শক্ত হওয়া, অনড় হয়ে যাওয়া
To harden into bone; to stop developing or changing; to become rigid.
✅ harden, solidify, become rigid, stiffen, fix, calcify, become set in one's ways
❌ soften, loosen, adapt, evolve, change, develop, grow
📝 His thinking had **ossified** over the years, and he refused all new ideas.

**OUTRAGEOUS** | জঘন্য, অসহনীয়, চরম অপমানজনক
Shockingly bad or excessive; highly offensive or immoral; scandalous.
✅ shocking, disgraceful, scandalous, preposterous, offensive, monstrous, extreme
❌ reasonable, acceptable, justified, moderate, normal, mild, fair
📝 The **outrageous** fee charged for the basic service drew widespread protest.

**OVERT** | প্রকাশ্য, স্পষ্ট, খোলামেলা
Done openly; not secret; clearly visible and obvious.
✅ open, obvious, visible, explicit, apparent, public, clear, unconcealed
❌ covert, secret, hidden, concealed, undercover, implicit, subtle
📝 His **overt** hostility toward the new policy surprised the board members.

## 🔤 P

✅ meticulous, careful, thorough, diligent, scrupulous, precise, exact, detailed
❌ careless, sloppy, hasty, negligent, rushed, inattentive, lazy
📝 The **painstaking** restoration of the ancient manuscript took five years.

**PANDEMIC** | বিশ্বব্যাপী মহামারী
A disease prevalent over a whole country or the world; very widespread.
✅ epidemic, widespread, universal, global, prevalent, pervasive, rampant
❌ local, contained, limited, isolated, controlled, rare, confined
📝 The **pandemic** brought economies across the world to a standstill.

**PARCHED** | শুষ্ক, তৃষ্ণার্ত, পানিশূন্য

✅ dry, arid, dehydrated, scorched, shriveled, withered, thirsty, desiccated
❌ moist, damp, wet, hydrated, saturated, lush, watered
📝 Survivors of the desert crossing were completely **parched** and dehydrated.

**PASSIVE** | নিষ্ক্রিয়, নিরামিষ, আত্মসমর্পণকারী
Accepting what happens without active response; submissive and yielding.
✅ inactive, submissive, yielding, compliant, docile, unresisting, unassertive
❌ active, assertive, aggressive, proactive, dominant, forceful, combative
📝 A **passive** stance in the face of injustice is itself a form of complicity.

**PECULIAR** | অদ্ভুত, বিচিত্র, স্বতন্ত্র
Strange or odd; unusual; belonging exclusively to a particular place or person.
✅ strange, unusual, bizarre, odd, weird, eccentric, unique, quirky, curious
❌ ordinary, normal, typical, common, familiar, conventional, standard
📝 There was a **peculiar** smell coming from the old cupboard in the corner.

**PEJORATIVE** | অবমাননামূলক, নিন্দামূলক, হেয়কর

✅ derogatory, insulting, demeaning, belittling, disparaging, critical, dismissive
❌ complimentary, praising, flattering, admiring, respectful, uplifting
📝 The use of **pejorative** language in the report was widely condemned.

**PENURY** | চরম দারিদ্র্য, অভাব-অনটন
Extreme poverty; being very poor; great lack of money or resources.
✅ poverty, destitution, need, deprivation, hardship, want, paucity, destitution
❌ wealth, affluence, prosperity, abundance, luxury, comfort, opulence
📝 After the business failed, the family lived in **penury** for several years.

**PERFIDY** | বিশ্বাসঘাতকতা, প্রতারণা, ধোঁকা
Deceitfulness and untrustworthiness; the action of betraying someone's trust.
