❌ loyalty, fidelity, faithfulness, honesty, integrity, trustworthiness, devotion
📝 The general's **perfidy** shocked everyone who had trusted him for years.

**PERILOUS** | বিপজ্জনক, ঝুঁকিপূর্ণ, ভয়াবহ
Full of danger or risk; hazardous and potentially very harmful.
✅ dangerous, hazardous, risky, treacherous, unsafe, threatening, dire, deadly
❌ safe, secure, harmless, protected, risk-free, stable, benign
📝 The **perilous** sea voyage claimed the lives of three of the twelve sailors.

**PERNICIOUS** | ক্ষতিকর, বিষাক্ত, মারাত্মক
Having a harmful effect, especially in a gradual or subtle way; deadly.
✅ harmful, damaging, destructive, deadly, toxic, injurious, malignant, lethal
❌ harmless, beneficial, helpful, safe, constructive, healing, good
📝 The **pernicious** habit of skipping sleep has long-term health consequences.

**PERSISTENT** | অদম্য, অনবরত, জেদী
Continuing firmly despite difficulty; refusing to give up; tenacious.
✅ determined, relentless, persevering, unyielding, tenacious, stubborn, steadfast
❌ yielding, flexible, giving up, irresolute, half-hearted, stopping, quitting
📝 Her **persistent** job applications finally landed her an interview at her dream company.

**PERSUADE / PERSUASION** | মানানো, বোঝানো / মানানোর কৌশল
To cause someone to do or believe something through reasoning or charm.
✅ convince, influence, cajole, coax, induce, win over, sway, tempt
❌ dissuade, discourage, deter, prevent, stop, repel, put off
📝 It took him an entire evening to **persuade** her to give the project one more try.

**PERTINENT** | প্রাসঙ্গিক, বিষয়সংক্রান্ত
Relevant or applicable to a particular matter; to the point.
✅ relevant, applicable, related, fitting, apt, appropriate, germane, suitable
❌ irrelevant, unrelated, beside the point, immaterial, inapplicable, off-topic
📝 Please make sure all comments are **pertinent** to the topic being discussed.

**PERVADE / PERVASIVE** | ছড়িয়ে পড়া / সর্বব্যাপী
To spread through every part of something; (pervasive) existing everywhere.
✅ permeate, penetrate, fill, spread, saturate, diffuse, omnipresent, widespread
❌ contain, limit, concentrate, restrict, hold back, localize
📝 A spirit of optimism **pervaded** the entire country after the election results.

The desire to promote the welfare of others through donations and generosity.
✅ charity, benevolence, generosity, humanitarianism, altruism, goodwill, giving
❌ selfishness, miserliness, greed, cruelty, misanthropy, avarice
📝 Her **philanthropy** funded hospitals, libraries, and schools in three countries.

**PHLEGMATIC** | অলস, শান্তস্বভাবী, ঠান্ডা মাথার
Having an unemotional, calm, and sluggish temperament; not easily excited.
✅ calm, unemotional, sluggish, apathetic, impassive, unexcitable, stoic
❌ emotional, passionate, excitable, energetic, animated, enthusiastic, lively
📝 He approached every crisis with the same **phlegmatic** calm that never wavered.

**PITHY** | সংক্ষিপ্ত ও অর্থবহ, সারগর্ভ
Concise and forcefully expressive; brief but full of meaning and substance.
✅ concise, brief, succinct, terse, meaningful, pointed, laconic, compact
❌ verbose, long-winded, wordy, vague, empty, meaningless, rambling
📝 The professor was famous for delivering **pithy** observations students never forgot.

**PLACID** | শান্ত, নিবিড়, স্থির, প্রশান্ত
Not easily upset or excited; calm and gentle; peaceful and tranquil.
✅ calm, serene, tranquil, peaceful, gentle, composed, unruffled, still
❌ agitated, anxious, troubled, excited, stormy, restless, turbulent
📝 The **placid** lake was perfect for an early morning canoe ride.

**PLAUDIT** | প্রশংসা, তারিফ, করতালি
Praise and approval; enthusiastic applause or acclaim from an audience.
✅ praise, applause, acclaim, approval, commendation, accolade, tribute, ovation
❌ criticism, disapproval, booing, condemnation, blame, censure
📝 The young pianist received warm **plaudits** after her debut performance.

**PLAUSIBLE** | যুক্তিসংগত, বিশ্বাসযোগ্য, সম্ভাব্য
Seeming reasonable or probable; credible, believable, and convincing.
✅ believable, reasonable, credible, probable, convincing, likely, logical, possible
❌ implausible, unlikely, incredible, unbelievable, far-fetched, absurd
📝 The alibi was **plausible** enough that investigators had to take it seriously.

**PRECARIOUS** | অনিশ্চিত, ঝুঁকিপূর্ণ, অস্থির
Not securely held or in position; uncertain and dependent on chance.
✅ unstable, dangerous, risky, uncertain, insecure, shaky, vulnerable, doubtful
❌ stable, secure, safe, certain, firm, reliable, guaranteed
📝 He lived in **precarious** financial conditions for most of his adult life.

**PREPOSTEROUS** | হাস্যকর, অযৌক্তিক, অসম্ভব
Contrary to reason or common sense; utterly absurd or ridiculous.
✅ absurd, ridiculous, outrageous, nonsensical, ludicrous, irrational, bizarre
❌ reasonable, sensible, logical, rational, plausible, sound, justified
📝 The suggestion that the Earth is flat is completely **preposterous**.

**PRETENTIOUS** | ভান করা, দেখানোর জন্য, কৃত্রিম
Attempting to impress by affecting greater importance or merit than one has.
✅ showy, ostentatious, affected, pompous, self-important, conceited, grandiose
❌ modest, genuine, humble, sincere, unpretentious, down-to-earth, authentic
📝 The **pretentious** review was more about showing off than actually critiquing the film.

**PROCURE** | সংগ্রহ করা, যোগাড় করা
To obtain something, especially with care or effort; to get or acquire.
✅ obtain, acquire, get, secure, gather, source, find, purchase
❌ lose, give away, discard, surrender, fail to get, relinquish
📝 It was difficult to **procure** fresh vegetables in such a remote location.

**PROFOUND** | গভীর, তীব্র, গুরুত্বপূর্ণ
Very great or intense; having deep insight or understanding; far-reaching.
✅ deep, intense, serious, thorough, great, significant, meaningful, comprehensive
❌ shallow, superficial, minor, trivial, unimportant, slight, basic

**PRUDENT** | বিচক্ষণ, সুচিন্তিত, সতর্ক
Acting with or showing care and thought for the future; wise in judgment.
✅ wise, careful, sensible, thoughtful, cautious, judicious, farsighted, shrewd
❌ reckless, imprudent, careless, foolish, impulsive, unwise, rash
📝 It would be **prudent** to save at least three months of expenses as an emergency fund.

**PUNCTILIOUS** | অতিসতর্ক, নিখুঁত নিয়ম মেনে চলা
Showing great attention to detail or correct behaviour; very precise.
✅ meticulous, precise, exacting, strict, thorough, scrupulous, careful, exact
❌ careless, sloppy, lazy, negligent, inattentive, casual, lax
📝 Her **punctilious** approach to grammar made her the best proofreader in the team.

## 🔤 Q

**QUASH** | দমন করা, বাতিল করা, চাপা দেওয়া
To reject or void a decision; to suppress or put an end to something forcefully.
✅ suppress, crush, squash, quell, overturn, nullify, cancel, stop
❌ allow, support, encourage, confirm, uphold, approve, permit
📝 The appeal court moved to **quash** the original guilty verdict.

**QUELL** | দমন করা, শান্ত করা
To put an end to a rebellion or other disorder; to suppress or subdue.
✅ suppress, subdue, crush, quash, calm, silence, put down, overcome
❌ incite, provoke, encourage, inflame, stir up, arouse
📝 Police were called to **quell** the disturbance outside the stadium.

**QUIXOTIC** | অবাস্তব, আদর্শবাদী কিন্তু অপ্রাসঙ্গিক
Extremely idealistic and unrealistic; impractically romantic; fanciful.
✅ idealistic, impractical, unrealistic, romantic, fanciful, utopian, dreamy
❌ realistic, practical, pragmatic, grounded, sensible, achievable
📝 His **quixotic** plan to end world hunger with one invention never went anywhere.

## 🔤 R

**RANCOROUS** | বিদ্বেষপূর্ণ, তিক্ত, শত্রুভাবাপন্ন
Having or showing bitter, long-lasting resentment or ill will; deeply hostile.
✅ bitter, resentful, hostile, acrimonious, hateful, vengeful, vindictive, malicious
❌ kind, forgiving, friendly, amicable, benevolent, peaceful, generous
📝 The **rancorous** dispute tore the organization apart over several bitter years.

**RATIFY** | অনুমোদন করা, আনুষ্ঠানিকভাবে স্বীকৃতি দেওয়া
To give formal consent to make something officially valid; to approve.
✅ approve, confirm, endorse, validate, sanction, authorize, pass, agree to
❌ reject, veto, deny, disapprove, annul, overturn, cancel
📝 Parliament voted to **ratify** the trade agreement before the deadline.

**RECKLESS** | বেপরোয়া, হঠকারী, দায়িত্বহীন
Without thinking about the danger or consequences of one's actions; hasty.
✅ careless, hasty, irresponsible, impetuous, impulsive, rash, heedless, foolhardy
❌ careful, cautious, prudent, thoughtful, responsible, deliberate, considered
📝 **Reckless** behaviour on construction sites puts everyone's life at risk.

**RECIPROCATE** | পারস্পরিক আদান-প্রদান করা, প্রতিদান দেওয়া
To respond to a gesture or action by making a corresponding one in return.
✅ return, exchange, match, respond in kind, give back, repay, mirror
❌ ignore, refuse, reject, withhold, keep, take without giving
📝 He hoped she would **reciprocate** his kindness when he needed help.

**RECONCILE** | মিটমাট করা, মেলানো
To restore friendly relations between people; to make compatible or consistent.
✅ settle, resolve, harmonize, reunite, patch up, make peace, balance
❌ divide, separate, worsen, conflict, clash, disagree, alienate
📝 The mediator worked for weeks to **reconcile** the feuding business partners.

**RECTIFY** | সংশোধন করা, ঠিক করা
To put something right; to correct or remedy a mistake or problem.
✅ correct, fix, repair, remedy, resolve, sort out, amend, put right
❌ worsen, damage, break, ignore, maintain error, repeat, overlook
📝 The company promised to **rectify** the error in the billing system within 24 hours.

**REDUNDANT** | অপ্রয়োজনীয়, অতিরিক্ত, বাহুল্যপূর্ণ
Not needed or useful; superfluous; using more words than necessary.
✅ unnecessary, superfluous, extra, excessive, surplus, repetitive, extraneous
❌ necessary, essential, needed, important, vital, required, useful
📝 With automation rising, many jobs are becoming **redundant** in manufacturing.

**RELENTLESS** | অদম্য, অবিরাম, নিষ্ঠুর
Unceasingly intense and not yielding; never stopping; harsh and without mercy.
✅ persistent, unyielding, unstoppable, ceaseless, merciless, unremitting, fierce
❌ mild, gentle, stopping, yielding, merciful, forgiving, lenient
📝 The team faced **relentless** pressure from the opposition throughout the match.

**RELUCTANT / RELUCTANCE** | অনিচ্ছুক / অনিচ্ছা
Not willing to do something and therefore slow to do it; hesitant.
✅ unwilling, hesitant, disinclined, unenthusiastic, loath, resistant, averse
❌ willing, eager, enthusiastic, happy to do, ready, keen, inclined
📝 She was **reluctant** to take the promotion because it meant moving cities.

**REMORSE** | অনুশোচনা, অনুতাপ
Deep regret or guilt for a wrong committed; a feeling of sorrow over past actions.
✅ guilt, regret, repentance, sorrow, contrition, shame, anguish, compunction
❌ pride, satisfaction, indifference, contentment, justification, arrogance
📝 He was filled with **remorse** after realizing how much his words had hurt her.

**RENOUNCE** | ত্যাগ করা, প্রত্যাখ্যান করা
To formally declare one's abandonment of a claim, right, or possession.
✅ abandon, give up, reject, disown, relinquish, forgo, surrender, repudiate
❌ claim, keep, maintain, hold, assert, pursue, retain, embrace
📝 She chose to **renounce** her inheritance to prove her independence.

**REPRIMAND** | তিরস্কার করা, ধমক দেওয়া, ভর্ৎসনা করা
A formal expression of disapproval; to formally rebuke or scold someone.
✅ rebuke, scold, admonish, criticize, censure, chide, reprove, tell off
❌ praise, compliment, commend, approve, encourage, reward, congratulate
📝 The manager gave him a sharp **reprimand** for sharing confidential information.

**REPROBATE** | নীতিহীন, দুর্নীতিগ্রস্ত, অধম মানুষ
An unprincipled person; someone morally corrupt or depraved.
✅ scoundrel, villain, wretch, degenerate, rogue, miscreant, sinner
❌ saint, virtuous person, honorable person, moral, upright
📝 The old miser was known as the town **reprobate** for his many corrupt dealings.

**RESILIENCE** | ঘুরে দাঁড়ানোর শক্তি, স্থিতিস্থাপকতা
The capacity to recover quickly from difficulties; mental and emotional toughness.
✅ toughness, strength, perseverance, adaptability, endurance, stamina, flexibility
❌ weakness, fragility, vulnerability, inability, rigidity, collapse
📝 The community's **resilience** after the earthquake impressed rescue workers worldwide.

**RETICENT** | কম কথার, মুখচোরা, চুপচাপ
Not revealing one's thoughts or feelings readily; reserved and quiet.
✅ reserved, quiet, secretive, shy, silent, withdrawn, uncommunicative, guarded
❌ talkative, open, outgoing, expressive, forthcoming, loquacious, candid
📝 She was **reticent** about her past and rarely discussed it with anyone.

**REVERENCE** | শ্রদ্ধা, সমীহ, ভক্তি
Deep respect and admiration; a feeling of awe and veneration.
✅ respect, admiration, awe, honor, veneration, esteem, deference, worship
❌ disrespect, contempt, disdain, irreverence, scorn, mockery
📝 The students stood in **reverence** as the founding principal entered the hall.

**REVERE** | শ্রদ্ধা করা, সম্মান করা
To feel deep respect or admiration for someone; to venerate.
✅ respect, admire, honor, esteem, venerate, worship, look up to, adore
❌ disrespect, despise, mock, dishonor, ridicule, scorn, belittle
📝 The scientist was universally **revered** for her decades of breakthrough research.

**RHETORIC / RHETORICAL** | বাকশিল্প, বাগাড়ম্বর
The art of effective or persuasive speaking; language designed to have a persuasive effect.
✅ oratory, persuasion, eloquence, public speaking, discourse, language
❌ silence, plain speech, candor, direct talk, bluntness
📝 His speech was full of **rhetoric** but short on concrete policy proposals.

**RIDICULOUS** | হাস্যকর, অযৌক্তিক, বেতুকা
Deserving or inviting mockery or derision; absurd; very silly.
✅ absurd, ludicrous, preposterous, laughable, nonsensical, comical, silly
❌ reasonable, sensible, logical, serious, valid, justified, rational
📝 The idea sounded **ridiculous** at first, but it eventually proved to be brilliant.

**RUTHLESS** | নির্দয়, নিষ্ঠুর, দয়াহীন
Having no pity or compassion; mercilessly harsh and cruel.
✅ merciless, cruel, heartless, callous, pitiless, brutal, relentless, unfeeling
❌ merciful, kind, compassionate, gentle, humane, forgiving, caring
📝 The **ruthless** competitor used every available tactic to eliminate rivals.
# CUET Vocabulary — Complete Notes | Part 7: S–Z
### All words from transcript | Synonyms · Antonyms · Bengali · Example

---

## 🔤 S

**SAGACIOUS / SAGACITY** | বিচক্ষণ / বিচক্ষণতা
Having keen mental discernment and good judgment; (sagacity) wisdom.
✅ wise, shrewd, astute, perceptive, intelligent, insightful, prudent, discerning
❌ foolish, naive, ignorant, unwise, shortsighted, imprudent, obtuse
📝 The **sagacious** minister anticipated the problem three years before it arose.

**SANE / SAPIENT** | সুস্থ মস্তিষ্কের / বিচক্ষণ, জ্ঞানী
(Sane) having a sound and rational mind; (sapient) wise and knowledgeable.
✅ rational, sensible, lucid, balanced, wise, intelligent, clearheaded, logical
❌ insane, irrational, unstable, foolish, confused, deluded
📝 Only a **sane** mind can navigate a complex crisis with calm and clarity.

**SAVAGE** | বর্বর, অসভ্য, নৃশংস, হিংস্র
Fierce, violent, and uncontrolled; uncivilized and extremely cruel.
✅ fierce, brutal, barbaric, uncivilized, ferocious, vicious, cruel, ruthless
❌ gentle, civilized, tame, kind, mild, peaceful, humane, cultured
📝 The **savage** attack left the village in ruins and the survivors in shock.

**SCARCITY / SCARCE** | অভাব, কমতি / বিরল, দুর্লভ
(Scarcity) insufficient supply; (scarce) not enough; available only in limited quantity.
✅ shortage, dearth, lack, deficiency, rarity, insufficiency, paucity
❌ abundance, plenty, surplus, excess, availability, sufficiency
📝 A **scarcity** of clean water forces millions of people to walk miles each day.

**SCRUPULOUS** | বিবেকী, নিখুঁতভাবে সৎ, সুক্ষ্মদর্শী
Very careful about doing what is right; thorough and precise in all details.
✅ meticulous, ethical, careful, conscientious, principled, precise, thorough
❌ unscrupulous, careless, dishonest, sloppy, negligent, corrupt
📝 A **scrupulous** accountant catches the errors that others miss.

**SEDATE** | শান্ত, গম্ভীর, স্থিরচিত্ত
Calm and dignified; not easily excited; deliberate and slow.
✅ calm, composed, dignified, serious, unhurried, grave, steady, sober
❌ excited, agitated, restless, animated, energetic, wild, nervous
📝 The professor had a **sedate** manner that made difficult topics feel approachable.

**SEDULOUS** | পরিশ্রমী, অধ্যবসায়ী, নিষ্ঠাবান
Showing dedication and great diligence; hardworking and thorough.
✅ diligent, hardworking, industrious, attentive, careful, painstaking, persistent
❌ lazy, idle, careless, negligent, sluggish, inattentive, slothful
📝 Her **sedulous** preparation ensured she topped the national examination.

**SEIZE** | দখল করা, কব্জা করা, হঠাৎ ধরা
To take hold of suddenly and forcibly; to capture; to confiscate.
✅ grab, capture, take, apprehend, confiscate, snatch, clutch, arrest
❌ release, free, give, return, surrender, relinquish, let go
📝 Police moved to **seize** the counterfeit goods before they could be sold.

**SERENE** | প্রশান্ত, নির্বিকার, শান্তিময়
Calm, peaceful, and untroubled; tranquil in appearance or manner.
✅ peaceful, tranquil, calm, placid, composed, still, unruffled, gentle
❌ anxious, disturbed, turbulent, agitated, restless, troubled, tense
📝 She remained **serene** and composed throughout the most difficult period.

**SHREWD** | বুদ্ধিমান, চতুর, দূরদর্শী
Having sharp powers of judgment; astute and clever in practical matters.
✅ astute, clever, perceptive, sharp, sagacious, intelligent, crafty, calculating
❌ naive, gullible, foolish, simple, dull, unsuspecting, trusting
📝 The **shrewd** investor waited patiently while others panicked and sold.

**SINISTER** | মন্দ উদ্দেশ্যমূলক, অশুভ, ভয়ঙ্কর
Giving the impression of evil or harm; threatening and ominous.
✅ evil, threatening, menacing, dark, wicked, malevolent, ominous, wicked
❌ good, benign, kind, harmless, innocent, bright, reassuring, pure
📝 The **sinister** figure in the doorway vanished when they turned on the light.

**SLOTH / SLOTHFUL** | অলসতা / অলস
(Sloth) reluctance to work; (slothful) lazy, sluggish, and idle.
✅ laziness, idleness, inactivity, sluggishness, torpor, lethargy, indolence
❌ diligence, energy, activity, work ethic, industriousness, eagerness
📝 His **slothful** attitude toward studying led to him failing the semester.

**SLUGGISH** | ধীরগতি, নিস্তেজ, অলস
Slow-moving or inactive; lacking energy or alertness; dull and slow.
✅ slow, lethargic, torpid, listless, inactive, drowsy, lazy, languid
❌ energetic, fast, active, lively, vigorous, alert, dynamic, brisk
📝 The economy remained **sluggish** for several quarters after the crisis.

**SOLEMN** | গম্ভীর, আনুষ্ঠানিক, ভাবগম্ভীর
Formal and dignified; serious in manner; deeply sincere and earnest.
✅ serious, grave, formal, dignified, sober, earnest, ceremonial, weighty
❌ casual, light-hearted, humorous, cheerful, informal, playful, trivial
📝 The ceremony was a **solemn** occasion observed with great respect.

**SOLACE** | সান্ত্বনা, আশ্বাস, দুঃখে সান্ত্বনা
Comfort or consolation in a time of distress or sadness.
✅ comfort, consolation, relief, support, soothing, reassurance, balm
❌ distress, pain, grief, sorrow, anguish, suffering, torment
📝 She found great **solace** in writing during the hardest months of her life.

**SOLICIT** | চাওয়া, আবেদন করা, সংগ্রহ করা
To ask for or try to obtain something from someone; to entreat.
✅ request, ask for, seek, appeal for, petition, invite, canvass, obtain
❌ give, offer, provide, donate, supply, refuse, ignore
📝 The charity began to **solicit** donations online for the flood relief fund.

**SOMBER** | মলিন, বিষণ্ণ, ভারাক্রান্ত, গাঢ় অন্ধকার
Dark and dull in color; gloomy; oppressively solemn or serious.
✅ dark, gloomy, melancholy, bleak, solemn, dismal, dreary, grave
❌ bright, cheerful, vivid, lively, colorful, joyful, light, cheerful
📝 A **somber** mood settled over the gathering as news of the tragedy spread.

**SPONTANEOUS** | স্বতঃস্ফূর্ত, আপনা থেকেই
Done or occurring as a result of a sudden natural impulse; not planned.
✅ unplanned, instinctive, impulsive, natural, voluntary, unprompted, reflexive
❌ planned, deliberate, calculated, rehearsed, forced, organized, prepared
📝 Her **spontaneous** laughter broke the tension in the room instantly.

**SPORADIC** | মাঝে মাঝে, অনিয়মিত, বিক্ষিপ্ত
Occurring at irregular intervals or only in a few places; scattered.
✅ irregular, occasional, intermittent, periodic, scattered, fitful, rare, infrequent
❌ continuous, regular, constant, frequent, steady, incessant, persistent
📝 **Sporadic** reports of the disease began surfacing across three regions.

**STERILE** | জীবাণুমুক্ত; বন্ধ্যা, উৎপাদনহীন
Free from bacteria or microorganisms; also, unable to produce offspring or ideas.
✅ barren, infertile, unproductive, clean, sanitized, aseptic, germ-free
❌ fertile, productive, contaminated, germ-ridden, fruitful, creative
📝 All surgical tools must be completely **sterile** before any procedure begins.

**STRENUOUS** | কঠোর পরিশ্রমসাধ্য, কষ্টকর
Requiring or using great effort or energy; vigorous and demanding.
✅ arduous, laborious, exhausting, vigorous, demanding, rigorous, taxing
❌ easy, effortless, relaxing, light, comfortable, gentle, simple
📝 The trainer warned them that the upcoming course would be highly **strenuous**.

**STUBBORN** | জেদী, একরোখা, অনমনীয়
Having or showing dogged determination not to change one's attitude.
✅ obstinate, headstrong, unyielding, persistent, inflexible, bull-headed, firm
❌ flexible, compliant, open-minded, cooperative, yielding, reasonable
📝 He was so **stubborn** that he refused to apologize even when proved wrong.

**SUCCUMB** | পরাজিত হওয়া, বশ মানা, মারা যাওয়া
To fail to resist pressure or a desire; to give way; to yield.
✅ yield, surrender, give in, submit, collapse, fall, capitulate, concede
❌ resist, withstand, overcome, survive, persevere, endure, fight back
📝 He ultimately **succumbed** to the pressure and resigned from his post.

**SULKY / SULLEN** | গোমড়ামুখী, মুষড়ে পড়া, বিরক্ত
Bad-tempered and sulky; showing resentful or gloomy silence.
✅ morose, glum, moody, resentful, sour, gloomy, withdrawn, discontented
❌ cheerful, happy, bright, pleasant, sunny, jovial, good-humoured
📝 She sat in **sullen** silence at the back of the room, refusing to speak.

**SUPPLE** | নমনীয়, নরম, সহজে বাঁকানো যায়
Bending and moving easily and gracefully; flexible and pliant.
✅ flexible, pliable, limber, elastic, lithe, bendable, graceful, agile
❌ rigid, stiff, inflexible, brittle, tense, unbendable, unyielding
📝 A dancer needs to maintain a **supple** body through regular stretching.

**SUPPRESS** | দমন করা, চাপা দেওয়া
To forcibly put an end to something; to prevent from being known or expressed.
✅ quash, crush, quell, silence, restrain, contain, stifle, subdue
❌ encourage, promote, allow, liberate, express, release, publicize
📝 Attempts to **suppress** the story only made the media more determined to print it.

**SWIFT** | দ্রুত, তীক্ষ্ণ, চটপটে
Happening quickly or promptly; moving very fast; without delay.
✅ fast, quick, rapid, speedy, prompt, hasty, brisk, nimble
❌ slow, sluggish, gradual, delayed, leisurely, unhurried, plodding
📝 The **swift** response of the rescue team saved lives during the earthquake.

**SYNTHETIC** | কৃত্রিম, সংশ্লেষিত, মানবনির্মিত
Made by chemical synthesis; not naturally occurring; fabricated or artificial.
✅ artificial, man-made, manufactured, fabricated, fake, engineered, processed
❌ natural, organic, genuine, pure, authentic, original, real
📝 The jacket was made entirely of **synthetic** materials, not real leather.

## 🔤 T

**TACITURN** | কম কথার, মুখচোরা, স্বল্পভাষী
Reserved or uncommunicative in speech; saying very little by nature.
✅ quiet, reserved, silent, uncommunicative, reticent, withdrawn, mute
❌ talkative, loquacious, garrulous, chatty, communicative, expressive
📝 The **taciturn** detective rarely revealed his thoughts until he was ready.

**TENACIOUS** | অদম্য, দৃঢ়প্রতিজ্ঞ, অনমনীয়
Not readily relinquishing a position or principle; determined and persistent.
✅ determined, persistent, stubborn, resolute, steadfast, strong-willed, firm
❌ weak-willed, yielding, indecisive, vacillating, inconsistent, giving up

**TEPID** | হালকা গরম, উৎসাহবিহীন, মাঝামাঝি
Lukewarm; showing little enthusiasm or conviction; only slightly warm.
✅ lukewarm, mild, indifferent, unenthusiastic, cool, half-hearted, apathetic
❌ enthusiastic, passionate, hot, cold, boiling, eager, fervent

**TETHER** | বেঁধে রাখা, আটকানো
To tie an animal to a fixed object; to limit or restrict the movement of.
✅ tie, fasten, secure, bind, leash, restrain, chain, anchor
❌ release, free, untie, loosen, let go, unleash, unchain
📝 He **tethered** his horse to the post before entering the inn.

**TIMID** | ভীতু, সংকোচপ্রবণ, ভয়ে কাঁপে
Lacking self-confidence or courage; easily frightened; nervous and shy.
✅ shy, fearful, cowardly, bashful, meek, hesitant, nervous, timorous
❌ brave, bold, confident, assertive, courageous, fearless, daring
📝 The **timid** applicant barely spoke above a whisper during the interview.

**TRANQUIL / TRANQUILITY** | শান্ত / শান্তি, নিস্তব্ধতা
Free from disturbance; calm and peaceful; (tranquility) the state of quiet.
✅ calm, peaceful, serene, still, placid, gentle, undisturbed, restful
❌ turbulent, noisy, chaotic, agitated, hectic, stormy, restless
📝 The mountain village offered a rare sense of **tranquility** far from city life.

**TREACHEROUS / TREACHERY** | বিশ্বাসঘাতক / বিশ্বাসঘাতকতা
Guilty of or involving betrayal; unreliable and dangerous; (treachery) betrayal.
✅ disloyal, traitorous, deceitful, unfaithful, false, unreliable, dangerous
❌ loyal, faithful, trustworthy, reliable, dependable, honest, devoted
📝 His **treacherous** act of leaking information to rivals destroyed the company.

**TRIVIAL** | তুচ্ছ, গুরুত্বহীন, সামান্য
Of little value or importance; not serious; small and unimportant.
✅ unimportant, minor, petty, insignificant, small, frivolous, negligible
❌ significant, important, major, serious, substantial, vital, meaningful
📝 Don't waste time arguing over **trivial** matters that won't matter tomorrow.

**TRUCULENT** | দুর্ধর্ষ, আগ্রাসী, লড়াকু
Eager or quick to argue or fight; aggressively defiant.
✅ aggressive, fierce, belligerent, combative, hostile, pugnacious, abusive
❌ peaceful, gentle, cooperative, mild, accommodating, agreeable

**TURBULENT** | অশান্ত, বিক্ষুব্ধ, উত্তাল
Characterized by conflict, disorder, or confusion; violently agitated.
✅ chaotic, stormy, violent, disorderly, rough, agitated, tumultuous, unstable
❌ calm, peaceful, stable, tranquil, orderly, smooth, settled
📝 The country went through a long **turbulent** period after the change of government.

**TURMOIL** | হৈচৈ, বিশৃঙ্খলতা, গোলমাল
A state of great disturbance, confusion, or uncertainty; chaos and disorder.
✅ chaos, upheaval, disorder, confusion, uproar, commotion, unrest, turmoil
❌ calm, order, peace, stability, tranquility, harmony, organization
📝 The entire city was thrown into **turmoil** when the earthquake struck at noon.

**TYRANT / TYRANNICAL** | স্বৈরশাসক / স্বৈরাচারী
(Tyrant) a cruel and oppressive ruler; (tyrannical) using power in a cruel way.
✅ despot, autocrat, oppressor, dictator, bully, authoritarian, absolutist
❌ democrat, servant, liberator, representative, fair leader, protector
📝 The **tyrannical** regime fell after decades of growing public resistance.

**TYRO** | নবীন, নতুন শিক্ষার্থী, অনভিজ্ঞ
A beginner or novice; a person who is new to a skill, art, or activity.
✅ beginner, novice, learner, newcomer, trainee, rookie, apprentice, greenhorn
❌ expert, veteran, professional, master, specialist, experienced, skilled
📝 Even a **tyro** could see that the design had serious structural flaws.

**VIBRANT** | প্রাণবন্ত, উজ্জ্বল, শক্তিশালী
Full of energy and enthusiasm; bright and striking in appearance.
✅ lively, energetic, bright, vivid, dynamic, animated, spirited, radiant
❌ dull, lifeless, drab, faded, boring, subdued, muted, grey

**VIRTUOUS** | সদাচারী, নৈতিক, মহৎ চরিত্রের

✅ moral, righteous, ethical, honorable, pure, good, upright, principled
❌ immoral, corrupt, wicked, dishonest, sinful, depraved, unethical

**VICIOUS** | নিষ্ঠুর, হিংস্র, বদমেজাজী
Deliberately cruel or violent; showing a desire to cause pain or harm.
✅ cruel, brutal, savage, ferocious, violent, spiteful, malicious, ruthless
❌ gentle, kind, mild, peaceful, humane, compassionate, benevolent
📝 The **vicious** attack on the journalist was condemned by press freedom organizations.

**VIGILANT** | সতর্ক, জাগ্রত, সাবধান
Keeping careful watch for possible danger or difficulties; alert.
✅ watchful, alert, attentive, cautious, observant, on guard, wary, careful
❌ careless, inattentive, negligent, oblivious, unaware, reckless, sleeping
📝 Security personnel must remain **vigilant** at all times on duty.

**VIGOR / VIGOROUS** | শক্তি, উদ্যম / সক্রিয়, জোরালো

✅ energy, strength, vitality, power, robustness, dynamism, forcefulness
❌ weakness, lethargy, fatigue, feebleness, sluggishness, inertia

✅ cautious, careful, alert, vigilant, watchful, guarded, suspicious, on guard

✅ tired, exhausted, fatigued, drained, listless, worn out, spent, flagging
❌ energetic, refreshed, lively, rested, alert, vigorous, fresh

# 📚 SUPPLEMENTARY WORDS — Additional from Transcript
## Words not yet covered in Parts 1–7

---

## 🔤 A (Additional)

**ABHOR** | ঘৃণা করা, অত্যন্ত অপছন্দ করা
To regard with disgust and hatred; to detest intensely.
✅ detest, loathe, despise, hate, execrate, find repulsive
❌ love, adore, admire, like, cherish, appreciate
📝 She **abhors** any form of dishonesty in personal or professional life.

**ABSCOND** | পালিয়ে যাওয়া, চুরি করে পালানো
To leave hurriedly and secretly, typically to avoid detection or arrest.
✅ flee, escape, run away, bolt, disappear, slip away, vanish
❌ stay, remain, appear, return, face, surrender, come back
📝 The accountant **absconded** with company funds before anyone noticed.

**ABSTEMIOUS** | সংযমী, মিতাহারী
Not allowing oneself to have much food, drink, or pleasure; restrained.
✅ temperate, restrained, moderate, self-denying, austere, frugal, disciplined
❌ indulgent, excessive, greedy, gluttonous, self-indulgent, intemperate
📝 He was known for his **abstemious** habits — one small meal a day.

**ABSTAIN** | বিরত থাকা, পরহেজ করা
To restrain oneself from doing or enjoying something; to refrain.
✅ refrain, desist, hold back, forgo, avoid, withhold, give up
❌ indulge, partake, participate, engage, consume, enjoy
📝 She chose to **abstain** from voting because she disagreed with all candidates.

**ABSTRACT** | বিমূর্ত, তাত্ত্বিক, সারসংক্ষেপ
Existing in thought or as an idea but not having a physical reality; theoretical.
✅ conceptual, theoretical, non-concrete, intangible, notional, philosophical
❌ concrete, specific, real, tangible, physical, definite, practical
📝 The professor's lecture was so **abstract** that few students could follow it.

**ACCELERATE** | ত্বরান্বিত করা, গতি বাড়ানো
To begin to move more quickly; to cause something to happen sooner.
✅ speed up, quicken, hasten, hurry, increase, advance, expedite
❌ slow down, decelerate, delay, hinder, brake, retard, impede
📝 Investment in infrastructure will **accelerate** economic development significantly.

**ACCLAIM** | প্রশংসা করা, সমাদর করা
Enthusiastic and public praise; to praise publicly and enthusiastically.
✅ praise, applaud, cheer, commend, celebrate, honour, laud, extol
❌ criticize, condemn, boo, censure, denounce, ignore, dismiss
📝 The novel received immediate critical **acclaim** after its release.

**ACCOMMODATE** | ব্যবস্থা করা, মিটমাট করা
To provide lodging or space for; to adapt to the needs of someone.
✅ house, provide for, adapt, adjust, hold, fit, contain, cater for
❌ reject, exclude, refuse, deny, expel, turn away, ignore
📝 The hotel can **accommodate** up to three hundred guests at full capacity.

**ACCOMPLISH** | সম্পন্ন করা, অর্জন করা
To achieve or complete something successfully; to carry out a task.
✅ achieve, complete, fulfill, attain, finish, carry out, realize, execute
❌ fail, abandon, leave unfinished, give up, neglect, miss
📝 She managed to **accomplish** more in one month than others do in a year.

**ACCOLADE** | সম্মান, পুরস্কার, প্রশংসা
An award or privilege granted as recognition of excellence; special praise.
✅ award, honor, tribute, prize, recognition, commendation, distinction
❌ criticism, punishment, condemnation, censure, dishonor, rebuke
📝 Winning the national award was the highest **accolade** she had ever received.

**ACQUIESCE** | মেনে নেওয়া, রাজি হওয়া নীরবে
To accept something reluctantly but without protest; to comply passively.
✅ agree, consent, comply, accept, submit, yield, go along with, concede
❌ resist, oppose, object, refuse, protest, reject, dispute, fight
📝 Though unhappy, he chose to **acquiesce** to the manager's final decision.

**ACUTE** | তীব্র, তীক্ষ্ণ, তীব্র বুদ্ধিমান
(Of an unpleasant situation) very severe; having a sharp, perceptive mind.
✅ severe, intense, sharp, keen, perceptive, serious, critical, penetrating
❌ mild, dull, moderate, slight, blunt, unimportant, minor, trivial
📝 He has an **acute** sense of observation that misses nothing.

**ADHERE** | মেনে চলা, লেগে থাকা
To stick firmly to a surface; to believe in and follow the practices of.
✅ stick, cling, follow, abide by, comply with, observe, maintain
❌ abandon, ignore, violate, defy, disobey, break, detach
📝 All employees must **adhere** to the company's code of conduct at all times.

**ADORN** | সাজানো, সুসজ্জিত করা
To make more attractive; to decorate with beautiful things.
✅ decorate, embellish, beautify, ornament, enhance, grace, garnish
❌ disfigure, strip, simplify, spoil, bare, uglify, remove
📝 Flowers were used to **adorn** every table at the wedding reception.

**ADVERSARY** | প্রতিপক্ষ, শত্রু, প্রতিদ্বন্দ্বী
One's opponent in a contest, conflict, or dispute; an enemy.
✅ opponent, enemy, rival, foe, antagonist, competitor, nemesis
❌ ally, friend, supporter, companion, partner, helper, teammate
📝 He studied his **adversary's** tactics carefully before the championship match.

**AFFLICT** | কষ্ট দেওয়া, যন্ত্রণা দেওয়া
To cause pain, suffering, or distress to; to trouble greatly.
✅ trouble, distress, torment, burden, pain, plague, beset, harm
❌ comfort, relieve, soothe, heal, help, cure, ease, benefit
📝 The disease continues to **afflict** millions of people across three continents.

**AGGRAVATE** | আরও খারাপ করা, বিরক্ত করা
To make a problem, injury, or offence worse or more serious; to annoy.
✅ worsen, intensify, exacerbate, irritate, annoy, inflame, compound
❌ improve, soothe, alleviate, mitigate, calm, ease, help, reduce
📝 Scratching the wound will only **aggravate** the infection further.

**ALLEVIATE** | উপশম করা, কমানো, হালকা করা
To make something less severe; to reduce pain, suffering, or difficulty.
✅ ease, reduce, lessen, relieve, mitigate, diminish, soothe, lighten
❌ worsen, aggravate, intensify, increase, magnify, heighten, amplify
📝 Meditation can help to **alleviate** the symptoms of chronic stress.

**ALLUDE** | ইঙ্গিত করা, উল্লেখ করা পরোক্ষে
To suggest or call attention to indirectly; to make a passing reference.
✅ hint, suggest, refer, imply, insinuate, intimate, indicate
❌ state directly, declare, specify, name outright, announce, assert
📝 He **alluded** to his difficult past without ever saying it directly.

**AMBIVALENT** | দ্বিধাগ্রস্ত, দুই মনের
Having mixed feelings or contradictory ideas about something; uncertain.
✅ uncertain, undecided, torn, conflicted, hesitant, equivocal, irresolute
❌ certain, decisive, clear, resolved, committed, definite, sure
📝 She felt **ambivalent** about the promotion — excited but also anxious.

**AMELIORATE** | উন্নত করা, ভালো করা
To make something bad or unsatisfactory better; to improve conditions.
✅ improve, enhance, better, upgrade, relieve, help, remedy, reform
❌ worsen, damage, harm, deteriorate, neglect, impair, degrade
📝 New policies were introduced to **ameliorate** working conditions for all staff.

**AMEND** | সংশোধন করা, পরিবর্তন করা
To make minor changes to a text, law, or document to correct or improve it.
✅ correct, revise, modify, alter, update, reform, rectify, change
❌ keep, maintain, ignore, worsen, leave unchanged, preserve
📝 The committee voted to **amend** three clauses before signing the agreement.

**AMPLIFY** | বৃদ্ধি করা, বিস্তৃত করা, জোরালো করা
To make something louder, larger, or more detailed; to increase in strength.
✅ increase, enlarge, expand, boost, strengthen, extend, heighten, magnify
❌ reduce, diminish, lessen, minimize, shrink, quiet, suppress
📝 Social media can **amplify** both positive messages and harmful misinformation.

**ANARCHIST / ANARCHY** | অরাজকতাবাদী / অরাজকতা
(Anarchist) one who promotes the absence of government; (anarchy) disorder.
✅ chaos, disorder, lawlessness, rebellion, upheaval, revolution
❌ order, governance, law, authority, peace, control, stability
📝 Without leadership, the organisation quickly descended into complete **anarchy**.

**ANTAGONIST** | প্রতিপক্ষ, বিরোধী পক্ষ
A person who actively opposes or is hostile to someone; an adversary.
✅ opponent, enemy, rival, adversary, foe, competitor, nemesis
❌ ally, friend, supporter, companion, protagonist, helper
📝 The **antagonist** in the story was not evil but rather deeply misunderstood.

**APPEASE** | শান্ত করা, মিটিয়ে দেওয়া
To pacify or placate someone by giving them what they want; to soothe.
✅ pacify, placate, soothe, mollify, calm, satisfy, conciliate, relieve
❌ provoke, aggravate, antagonize, anger, inflame, irritate, upset
📝 She brought gifts to **appease** her angry friend after the misunderstanding.

**ARBITRARY** | খেয়ালী, স্বেচ্ছাচারী, যুক্তিহীন
Based on random choice rather than reason or system; capricious.
✅ random, capricious, whimsical, inconsistent, illogical, unreasonable
❌ systematic, logical, rational, consistent, reasoned, principled, fair
📝 The rule seemed completely **arbitrary** and had no logical foundation whatsoever.

**ARCANE** | রহস্যময়, গুপ্ত, অল্পজ্ঞাত
Known or understood by very few people; mysterious and secret; obscure.
✅ mysterious, secret, obscure, esoteric, cryptic, little-known, hidden
❌ common, well-known, obvious, popular, familiar, accessible, clear
📝 The professor specialised in **arcane** aspects of medieval law that few had studied.

**ARCHAIC** | পুরাতন, প্রাচীন, চলনবাহির
Very old or old-fashioned; belonging to an earlier period; outdated.
✅ ancient, old-fashioned, outdated, obsolete, antique, antiquated, old
❌ modern, current, contemporary, new, fresh, up-to-date, relevant
📝 The **archaic** legal language in the contract confused even seasoned lawyers.

**ARDOR** | তীব্র উৎসাহ, আবেগ, উদ্দীপনা
Great enthusiasm or passion; intense feeling or devotion.
✅ passion, enthusiasm, zeal, fervor, devotion, intensity, eagerness
❌ apathy, indifference, coldness, disinterest, lethargy, dullness
📝 She tackled every assignment with genuine **ardor** and commitment.

**ARTICULATE** | স্পষ্টভাবে বলা, সুবক্তা
Able to express thoughts fluently and clearly; eloquent in speech.
✅ eloquent, fluent, clear, well-spoken, expressive, coherent, lucid
❌ inarticulate, tongue-tied, unclear, mumbling, confused, incoherent
📝 She was an **articulate** speaker who could explain even the most complex issues simply.

**ASCERTAIN** | নিশ্চিত করা, খোঁজ করা
To find out something for certain; to establish the truth of something.
✅ determine, discover, find out, verify, establish, confirm, learn
❌ overlook, guess, assume, ignore, neglect, miss, leave unknown
📝 Police worked around the clock to **ascertain** the cause of the accident.

**ASSIDUOUS** | পরিশ্রমী, নিরলস, সতর্ক
Showing great care and perseverance in one's work; diligent and persistent.
✅ diligent, hardworking, persistent, careful, industrious, thorough, devoted
❌ lazy, careless, negligent, sloppy, half-hearted, idle, inattentive
📝 His **assiduous** revision paid off when he scored perfectly on the exam.

**ASSUAGE** | প্রশমিত করা, শান্ত করা, মেটানো
To make an unpleasant feeling less intense; to soothe or satisfy.
✅ ease, relieve, soothe, calm, pacify, alleviate, appease, lessen
❌ worsen, intensify, aggravate, inflame, provoke, heighten, increase
📝 The cup of tea did little to **assuage** her deep anxiety about the results.

**ATONE** | প্রায়শ্চিত্ত করা, ক্ষতিপূরণ দেওয়া
To make amends for a wrong or injury; to compensate for past wrongdoing.
✅ make amends, compensate, repent, pay, repair, rectify, repay
❌ ignore, repeat, worsen, evade, avoid, deny, refuse to compensate
📝 He spent years trying to **atone** for the mistakes he made in his youth.

**AUDACIOUS** | সাহসী, দুঃসাহসী, নির্লজ্জ
Showing a willingness to take surprisingly bold risks; shamelessly bold.
✅ daring, bold, brave, fearless, reckless, brazen, impudent, gutsy
❌ cowardly, timid, cautious, fearful, hesitant, meek, restrained
📝 The **audacious** plan to cross the Atlantic solo impressed everyone who heard it.

**AUGMENT** | বৃদ্ধি করা, বাড়ানো
To make something greater by adding to it; to increase in size or value.
✅ increase, enlarge, expand, extend, enhance, boost, add to, supplement
❌ reduce, decrease, lessen, shrink, minimize, diminish, cut back
📝 The new funding will **augment** the hospital's existing medical resources.

**AVERT** | এড়ানো, বিপদ ঠেকানো
To prevent or avoid something undesirable from happening; to turn away.
✅ prevent, avoid, stop, ward off, forestall, deter, evade, stave off
❌ cause, bring about, allow, invite, permit, encourage, create
📝 Quick thinking by the pilot helped **avert** what could have been a disaster.

**AWE** | বিস্ময়, ভয়মিশ্রিত শ্রদ্ধা
A feeling of reverential respect mixed with wonder or fear.
✅ wonder, amazement, reverence, admiration, astonishment, respect, fear
❌ contempt, indifference, disrespect, boredom, disregard, mockery
📝 Standing at the edge of the Grand Canyon filled her with complete **awe**.

---

## 🔤 B (Additional)

**BAFFLE** | বিভ্রান্ত করা, হতবুদ্ধি করা
To confuse someone so much that they cannot understand; to perplex.
✅ confuse, perplex, puzzle, bewilder, mystify, stump, confound
❌ clarify, explain, enlighten, guide, help understand, simplify
📝 The strange results **baffled** even the most experienced researchers.

**BANAL** | একঘেয়ে, পুরনো ধারার, অর্থহীন
So lacking in originality as to be boring; trite and overused.
✅ trite, clichéd, ordinary, dull, unoriginal, hackneyed, stale, predictable
❌ original, fresh, exciting, novel, creative, unique, innovative
📝 The speech was full of **banal** remarks that failed to inspire the crowd.

**BANE** | বিপদের কারণ, কষ্টের উৎস
A person or thing that causes great distress or annoyance; a poison.
✅ curse, plague, annoyance, nuisance, scourge, torment, affliction
❌ blessing, benefit, advantage, boon, help, joy, comfort
📝 Procrastination was the **bane** of his academic life throughout university.

**BASTION** | দুর্গ, রক্ষার কেন্দ্র, ঘাঁটি
A projecting part of a fortification; something seen as protecting a principle.
✅ stronghold, fortress, defender, bulwark, citadel, pillar, champion
❌ weakness, vulnerability, surrendering point, open ground
📝 The university was regarded as a **bastion** of free speech and open debate.

**BELITTLE** | ছোট করা, তুচ্ছ করা
To make someone or something seem less important; to dismiss as trivial.
✅ demean, disparage, dismiss, minimize, ridicule, deprecate, mock
❌ praise, celebrate, honor, uplift, acknowledge, magnify, credit
📝 A good manager never **belittles** an employee's ideas in front of the team.

**BENIGN** | নিরীহ, দয়ালু, মৃদু
Kind and gentle; not harmful; (medically) not malignant.
✅ harmless, gentle, kind, safe, mild, non-threatening, favorable
❌ harmful, malign, dangerous, hostile, sinister, threatening, severe
📝 The doctor confirmed the lump was **benign** and posed no health risk.

**BESTOW** | দান করা, প্রদান করা
To confer or present an honor, right, or gift upon someone.
✅ give, grant, award, present, confer, donate, impart, provide
❌ take, withhold, deny, refuse, withdraw, remove, revoke
📝 The university chose to **bestow** an honorary degree upon the celebrated author.

**BLEAK** | নিরানন্দ, বিষণ্ণ, অন্ধকার
Lacking warmth or kindness; offering little or no hope; cold and bare.
✅ gloomy, cheerless, hopeless, desolate, dismal, grim, empty, cold
❌ cheerful, warm, hopeful, lively, pleasant, promising, inviting
📝 The economic outlook looked increasingly **bleak** as unemployment continued to rise.

**BOAST** | গর্ব করা, বড়াই করা

✅ brag, crow, show off, vaunt, flaunt, gloat, exaggerate, proclaim
❌ humble oneself, understate, conceal, modestly mention, deny

**BREVITY** | সংক্ষিপ্ততা, সংক্ষেপ

✅ conciseness, shortness, terseness, succinctness, laconism
❌ verbosity, length, wordiness, long-windedness, prolixity

**BURLESQUE** | তামাশা, ব্যঙ্গ অনুকরণ

✅ parody, satire, mockery, caricature, farce, spoof, send-up
❌ tribute, praise, sincere imitation, honor, reverence

❌ silence, quiet, calm, whisper, peace, tranquility, hush

**COGENT** | যুক্তিসংগত, শক্তিশালী, প্রভাবশালী

**COMPLIANT** | বাধ্য, মেনে নেওয়ার মানসিকতার

❌ resistant, defiant, disobedient, stubborn, difficult, rebellious

**CONDONE** | মাফ করা, উপেক্ষা করা
To accept or forgive behaviour that is considered morally wrong or offensive.
✅ forgive, overlook, excuse, tolerate, pardon, disregard, ignore
❌ condemn, punish, prohibit, reject, oppose, censure, penalise

**CONTRITE** | অনুতপ্ত, দুঃখিত
Feeling or expressing remorse at the recognition of having done wrong.
✅ remorseful, repentant, sorry, penitent, apologetic, regretful, ashamed
❌ unrepentant, arrogant, shameless, unapologetic, defiant, indifferent

**CONUNDRUM** | জটিল সমস্যা, ধাঁধা
A confusing and difficult problem or question; a riddle.
✅ puzzle, problem, enigma, mystery, dilemma, riddle, difficulty
❌ simple problem, clear solution, easy answer, straightforward question

**COPIOUS** | প্রচুর, বিপুল পরিমাণে
Abundant in supply; present in large quantities; extensive and plentiful.
✅ abundant, plentiful, ample, lavish, profuse, extensive, bountiful
❌ scarce, meager, sparse, insufficient, limited, lacking, deficient

**CREDULOUS** | বিশ্বাসপ্রবণ, সহজে বিশ্বাস করে
Having an excessive readiness to believe things; too easily convinced.
✅ naive, gullible, trusting, unsuspecting, over-trusting, innocent
❌ skeptical, cynical, suspicious, questioning, doubting, cautious

**CRYPTIC** | রহস্যময়, দুর্বোধ্য, গুপ্ত অর্থের

❌ clear, obvious, plain, transparent, straightforward, explicit

**CURTAIL** | কমানো, ছেঁটে ফেলা, সীমিত করা

Intensely distressing and upsetting; acutely painful or distressing.
✅ distressing, traumatic, painful, agonising, disturbing, horrifying
❌ pleasant, comforting, joyful, uplifting, soothing, encouraging
📝 The survivors shared their **harrowing** experience with investigators.

**HYPOCRITE / HYPOCRISY** | ভণ্ড / ভণ্ডামি
(Hypocrite) one who pretends to have virtues or beliefs they lack; false pretence.
✅ fraud, deceiver, liar, charlatan, pretender, fake, impostor
❌ sincere person, honest, genuine, authentic, straightforward
📝 Calling for sacrifice while living in luxury made him look like a complete **hypocrite**.

Lacking sense or meaning; pointless and silly; devoid of intelligence.

Not in harmony or keeping with the surroundings or other aspects.

Wanting to avoid activity or exertion; lazy by nature; habitually idle.
✅ lazy, idle, sluggish, lethargic, slothful, inactive, listless, inert

Never failing; always doing or happening as expected; incapable of error.

**INFAMY** | কুখ্যাতি, বদনাম, কলঙ্ক
The state of being well known for some bad quality or deed; notoriety.
✅ notoriety, disgrace, dishonour, scandal, disrepute, shame, ill-repute

**INSIDIOUS** | প্রচ্ছন্নভাবে ক্ষতিকর, ধীরে ধীরে ক্ষয়কারী
Proceeding in a gradual, subtle way but with harmful effects; treacherous.
✅ subtle, stealthy, cunning, deceptive, treacherous, slow-acting, harmful

**INTEGRITY** | সততা, নৈতিক শক্তি, সম্পূর্ণতা

Belonging naturally; essential; of the essential nature of something.

❌ extrinsic, external, acquired, added, outside, incidental

Not logical or reasonable; not guided by logic or reason.

❌ rational, logical, reasonable, sensible, sound, justified, coherent

❌ cooperative, obedient, compliant, docile, manageable, willing
📝 The **recalcitrant** student refused to participate despite the teacher's best efforts.

**RECONCILE** | মিটমাট করা, একমত করানো
To make compatible or consistent; to restore friendly relations between people.
✅ settle, resolve, harmonise, reunite, patch up, balance, make peace
❌ divide, worsen, conflict, separate, clash, disagree, alienate
📝 The two families were eventually **reconciled** after a long-standing dispute.

**RECLUSE** | একাকী মানুষ, বৈরাগী, সংসার ত্যাগী
A person who lives a solitary life and tends to avoid other people.
✅ hermit, solitary person, loner, introvert, ascetic, monk, isolate
❌ socialite, gregarious person, extrovert, outgoing person
📝 After his wife's death, he became a complete **recluse** and rarely left his house.

**REDRESS** | প্রতিকার করা, সংশোধন করা
A remedy or compensation for a wrong or grievance; to remedy or set right.
✅ remedy, resolve, address, correct, fix, compensate, repair, amend
❌ ignore, overlook, deny, leave unsettled, worsen, neglect
📝 She sought legal **redress** after being wrongfully dismissed from her job.

**REFUTE** | খণ্ডন করা, ভুল প্রমাণ করা
To prove a statement or theory to be wrong or false; to disprove.
✅ disprove, contradict, deny, rebut, invalidate, counter, discredit
❌ confirm, support, prove, validate, agree with, endorse, accept
📝 The scientist was able to completely **refute** the claims made in the study.

**RENOUNCE** | ত্যাগ করা, দাবি ছেড়ে দেওয়া
To formally declare one's abandonment of a claim, right, or possession.
✅ abandon, give up, reject, disown, relinquish, forgo, surrender
❌ claim, keep, assert, pursue, hold, retain, embrace, uphold
📝 The prince chose to **renounce** his claim to the throne for personal freedom.

**REPREHENSIBLE** | নিন্দনীয়, ভর্ৎসনার যোগ্য
Deserving censure or condemnation; morally wrong and completely unacceptable.
✅ blameworthy, condemnable, shameful, disgraceful, deplorable, wrong
❌ praiseworthy, commendable, acceptable, good, admirable, honorable
📝 His treatment of subordinates was **reprehensible** and led to his resignation.

**REPUDIATE** | প্রত্যাখ্যান করা, অস্বীকার করা
To refuse to accept; to reject as untrue, unfounded, or unjust; to disown.
✅ reject, deny, disown, renounce, disclaim, disavow, contradict, abandon
❌ accept, acknowledge, affirm, support, embrace, recognise, claim
📝 The government **repudiated** the treaty signed by the previous administration.

**RESILIENT** | স্থিতিস্থাপক, ঘুরে দাঁড়াতে সক্ষম
Able to withstand or recover quickly from difficult conditions; tough.
✅ tough, strong, adaptable, flexible, durable, resistant, bouncing back
❌ fragile, weak, brittle, delicate, unable to recover, vulnerable
📝 Communities that are **resilient** recover faster from natural disasters.

**REVERENT** | শ্রদ্ধাশীল, ভক্তিপূর্ণ
Feeling or showing deep and solemn respect; worshipful and respectful.
✅ respectful, devout, worshipful, deferential, awed, pious, admiring
❌ disrespectful, irreverent, contemptuous, mocking, scornful, rude
📝 The crowd fell into a **reverent** silence as the ceremony began.

**RHETORIC** | বাকশিল্প, বাগাড়ম্বর, প্রভাবশালী ভাষা
The art of effective or persuasive speaking and writing; impressive language.
✅ oratory, eloquence, language, persuasion, expression, discourse
❌ plain speech, direct talk, simple language, bluntness, silence
📝 The speech was impressive **rhetoric** but offered no concrete solutions.

**RIGID** | কঠোর, অনমনীয়, নিয়মনিষ্ঠ
Not able to bend or be forced out of shape; inflexible in behaviour or attitude.
✅ stiff, inflexible, firm, unyielding, strict, hard, immovable, fixed
❌ flexible, pliable, adaptable, supple, soft, yielding, loose, elastic
📝 The **rigid** interpretation of the rules allowed no room for common sense.

A supreme ruler; possessing supreme or ultimate power; fully independent.

## 📊 FINAL MASTER QUICK-REFERENCE TABLE (Supplementary Words)

| Word | বাংলা | Synonym | Antonym |
|---|---|---|---|
| Abhor | ঘৃণা করা | detest | love |
| Abscond | পালিয়ে যাওয়া | flee | remain |
| Abstain | বিরত থাকা | refrain | indulge |
| Accelerate | ত্বরান্বিত করা | speed up | slow down |
| Acclaim | প্রশংসা করা | applaud | criticize |
| Accolade | সম্মান | award | criticism |

## 🔤 A (Final Batch)

**ABIDE** | সহ্য করা; মেনে চলা
To tolerate or accept something; to remain or continue; to follow a rule.
✅ tolerate, endure, bear, accept, follow, comply, stand, put up with
❌ reject, resist, defy, oppose, refuse, disobey, break
📝 She could not **abide** the sound of someone chewing loudly at the table.

**ABOUND** | প্রচুর থাকা, ভরপুর হওয়া
To exist in large numbers or amounts; to be plentiful; to teem with.
✅ teem, overflow, be plentiful, be full of, swarm, be rich in, flourish
❌ lack, be scarce, be deficient, be absent, be missing
📝 The rainforest **abounds** with species that scientists are yet to discover.

**ABRUPT** | হঠাৎ, অপ্রত্যাশিত, রুক্ষ
Sudden and unexpected; steep; curt or rude in speech or manner.
✅ sudden, unexpected, hasty, curt, rude, sharp, steep, brusque
❌ gradual, gentle, expected, polite, smooth, courteous, leisurely
📝 His **abrupt** departure from the meeting left everyone confused and concerned.

**ABSOLUTION** | মুক্তি, পাপমোচন, ক্ষমা
Formal release from guilt, obligation, or punishment; formal forgiveness.
✅ forgiveness, pardon, exoneration, release, acquittal, clemency, discharge
❌ condemnation, blame, punishment, accusation, conviction, sentence
📝 He sought **absolution** for wrongs he had committed many years earlier.

**ACCEDE** | রাজি হওয়া, মত দেওয়া
To agree to a demand, request, or treaty; to assent or consent formally.
✅ agree, consent, comply, yield, assent, approve, concede, grant
❌ refuse, reject, deny, oppose, resist, decline, withhold
📝 After lengthy talks, the government finally chose to **accede** to the demands.

**ACCLAIM** | প্রশংসা, সমাদর, সম্মান
Enthusiastic and public praise; to praise enthusiastically and publicly.
✅ praise, applause, cheer, commend, celebrate, honour, extol
❌ criticize, condemn, boo, censure, denounce, ignore, dismiss
📝 The film received widespread critical **acclaim** after its debut screening.

**ACRIMONY** | তিক্ততা, কটুতা, বিদ্বেষমূলক মনোভাব
Bitterness or ill feeling, especially in speech or manner; harsh hostility.
✅ bitterness, hostility, resentment, animosity, rancour, spite, harshness
❌ goodwill, warmth, friendliness, harmony, kindness, pleasantness
📝 The divorce proceedings were marked by intense **acrimony** between both parties.

**ACQUIT** | বেকসুর খালাস করা, মুক্তি দেওয়া
To formally declare that someone is not guilty of a criminal charge.
✅ exonerate, clear, absolve, free, vindicate, discharge, pardon, release
❌ convict, sentence, condemn, blame, imprison, charge, find guilty
📝 The jury voted to **acquit** the defendant after just two hours of deliberation.

**ADAMANTINE** | হীরার মতো কঠিন, অনমনীয়
Unbreakable and impenetrable; extremely hard; too strong to be overcome.
✅ unyielding, unbreakable, impenetrable, rock-hard, indestructible, rigid
❌ soft, breakable, flexible, yielding, fragile, malleable, pliable
📝 Her **adamantine** resolve could not be shaken by any argument.

**ADAGE** | প্রবাদ বাক্য, পুরনো প্রজ্ঞার কথা
A short statement expressing a general truth or piece of advice; a proverb.
✅ proverb, saying, maxim, axiom, truism, motto, aphorism, wisdom
❌ falsehood, untrue statement, lie, misconception, error
📝 The **adage** "actions speak louder than words" has never been truer.

**ADULATION** | অতিরিক্ত প্রশংসা, তোষামোদ
Excessive admiration or praise; obsequious flattery or adoration.
✅ flattery, praise, excessive admiration, glorification, hero worship, fawning
❌ criticism, condemnation, insult, contempt, disapproval, censure
📝 The celebrity was surrounded by **adulation** wherever she went.

**AFFABLE** | মিষ্টভাষী, সহজে মেলামেশার, আলাপী
Friendly, easy to talk to, and pleasant in manner; approachable.
✅ friendly, pleasant, approachable, easy-going, sociable, amiable, warm
❌ unfriendly, cold, distant, hostile, rude, unapproachable, difficult
📝 Despite his fame, he remained genuinely **affable** and easy to approach.

**AGGRANDIZE** | বড় করা, গুরুত্ব বাড়ানো, নিজেকে প্রসারিত করা
To increase the power, status, or wealth of; to make appear greater.
✅ inflate, exaggerate, elevate, magnify, enhance, glorify, boost
❌ minimize, reduce, downplay, belittle, diminish, lower, humble
📝 Politicians sometimes **aggrandize** their achievements to win votes.

**ALACRITY** | উদ্যম, স্ফূর্তি, তৎপরতা
Brisk and cheerful readiness; eagerness and willingness to act.
✅ eagerness, willingness, enthusiasm, promptness, zeal, readiness, keenness
❌ laziness, reluctance, hesitation, slowness, indifference, apathy
📝 The team responded with surprising **alacrity** to the emergency call.

**ALLEGIANCE** | আনুগত্য, বিশ্বস্ততা, স্বীকৃতি
Loyalty or commitment to a superior or group; faithful devotion.
✅ loyalty, faithfulness, devotion, fidelity, commitment, dedication
❌ disloyalty, betrayal, treachery, rebellion, defection, desertion
📝 Soldiers swear an oath of **allegiance** to their nation before serving.

**ALLEVIATE** | উপশম করা, হালকা করা
To make suffering, deficiency, or a problem less severe; to ease pain.
✅ ease, reduce, lessen, relieve, mitigate, diminish, soothe, soften
❌ worsen, aggravate, intensify, increase, heighten, exacerbate
📝 Painkillers were given to **alleviate** the patient's discomfort after surgery.

**ALTRUISM** | পরোপকারিতা, নিঃস্বার্থতা
Unselfish concern for the wellbeing of others; selfless generosity.
✅ selflessness, generosity, charity, benevolence, humanitarianism
❌ selfishness, greed, self-interest, egoism, narcissism, avarice
📝 Her **altruism** led her to donate half her income to charity every year.

**AMBIENCE** | পরিবেশ, আবহাওয়া, মাহোল
The character and atmosphere of a place; the mood or feeling it creates.
✅ atmosphere, mood, environment, feeling, setting, surroundings, tone
❌ cold environment, sterile place, harsh surroundings, hostile setting
📝 The **ambience** of the candlelit restaurant was perfect for a romantic evening.

**AMELIORATE** | উন্নতি করা, ভালো করা
To make something bad or unsatisfactory better; to improve a situation.
✅ improve, enhance, better, upgrade, relieve, help, remedy, reform
❌ worsen, damage, harm, deteriorate, neglect, impair, aggravate
📝 The government introduced new programmes to **ameliorate** living conditions.

**AMICABLE** | বন্ধুত্বপূর্ণ, মিলনশীল, শান্তিপূর্ণ
Having a spirit of friendliness; without serious disagreement or rancour.
✅ friendly, peaceable, harmonious, cooperative, pleasant, cordial, agreeable
❌ hostile, antagonistic, bitter, unfriendly, contentious, aggressive
📝 The two companies reached an **amicable** settlement without going to court.

**AMNESTY** | সাধারণ ক্ষমা, মাফ করা (সরকারিভাবে)
An official pardon for people convicted of political offences; a general pardon.
✅ pardon, forgiveness, clemency, reprieve, absolution, discharge
❌ punishment, condemnation, sentence, conviction, imprisonment
📝 The government declared an **amnesty** for all minor political offenders.

**ANECDOTE** | ছোট গল্প, ঘটনার বিবরণ
A short amusing or interesting story about a real incident or person.
✅ story, tale, account, narrative, episode, incident, reminiscence
❌ fiction, fabrication, lie, invention, falsehood
📝 He began his speech with a funny **anecdote** that put the audience at ease.

**ANIMOSITY** | শত্রুতা, বিদ্বেষ, বিরাগ
Strong hostility and active dislike; active hatred between people.
✅ hostility, hatred, enmity, antagonism, bitterness, resentment, acrimony
❌ friendship, goodwill, harmony, fondness, affection, love, amity
📝 Years of **animosity** between the neighbours finally erupted into a public dispute.

**ANNIHILATE** | ধ্বংস করা, সম্পূর্ণ নির্মূল করা
To destroy utterly; to obliterate completely; to wipe out all existence of.
✅ destroy, obliterate, eliminate, wipe out, eradicate, devastate, demolish
❌ create, build, preserve, protect, save, nurture, maintain
📝 The fire **annihilated** the entire building within a matter of hours.

**ANTAGONISM** | বিরোধিতা, শত্রুতা, প্রতিরোধ
Active hostility or opposition between people or groups; conflict.
✅ hostility, opposition, conflict, enmity, antipathy, rivalry, resistance
❌ cooperation, harmony, friendship, alliance, agreement, support
📝 The **antagonism** between the two departments was slowing down the whole project.

**ANTIQUATED** | পুরাতন, অচল, কালবাহিত
Very old or old-fashioned; no longer relevant or appropriate; outdated.
✅ outdated, archaic, obsolete, old-fashioned, antique, bygone, ancient
❌ modern, current, up-to-date, contemporary, new, fresh, relevant
📝 The company's **antiquated** computer systems were long overdue for replacement.

**ANTIPATHY** | বিরাগ, ঘৃণা, অপছন্দ
A deep-seated feeling of dislike; strong aversion toward someone or something.
✅ dislike, hatred, aversion, hostility, repugnance, disgust, enmity
❌ liking, fondness, attraction, love, affection, admiration, sympathy
📝 She felt an immediate **antipathy** toward the new manager from their first meeting.

**APEX** | শীর্ষ, চূড়া, সর্বোচ্চ বিন্দু
The top or highest point of something; the peak of achievement.
✅ peak, pinnacle, summit, zenith, top, height, acme, crown
❌ base, bottom, nadir, lowest point, trough, floor, depth
📝 Winning the world championship was the **apex** of his sporting career.

**APOCRYPHAL** | সন্দেহজনক, অবিশ্বাসযোগ্য, কিংবদন্তি ঘেঁষা
(Of a story or statement) of doubtful authenticity; probably not true.
✅ dubious, questionable, unverified, legendary, fabricated, fictitious
❌ authentic, verified, genuine, reliable, true, factual, historical
📝 The story of his swimming across the river in winter is almost certainly **apocryphal**.

**APPREHENSIVE** | আশঙ্কিত, ভয়ে সচেতন, উদ্বিগ্ন
Anxious or fearful that something bad or unpleasant will happen; worried.
✅ anxious, worried, fearful, nervous, uneasy, concerned, tense, troubled
❌ confident, calm, reassured, fearless, relaxed, certain, composed
📝 She was **apprehensive** about the surgery but trusted her doctor completely.

**APT** | উপযুক্ত, যথাযথ, প্রাসঙ্গিক
Appropriate or suitable in the circumstances; having a tendency; clever.
✅ suitable, appropriate, fitting, relevant, proper, pertinent, apt, spot-on
❌ inappropriate, unsuitable, irrelevant, improper, unfit, out of place
📝 That was an **apt** description of what happened that evening.

**ARDOR** | উদ্দীপনা, উৎসাহ, তীব্র আবেগ
Great enthusiasm or passion; intense feeling or devotion to a cause.
✅ passion, enthusiasm, zeal, fervor, devotion, intensity, eagerness, fire
❌ apathy, indifference, coldness, disinterest, lethargy, dullness
📝 He approached every challenge with the same burning **ardor** he had at twenty.

**ARROGANCE** | অহংকার, দাম্ভিকতা
An attitude of superiority and contempt for others; excessive pride.
✅ pride, haughtiness, conceit, pomposity, self-importance, condescension
❌ humility, modesty, meekness, respectfulness, self-effacement
📝 His **arrogance** during the interview cost him the position he clearly deserved.

## 🔤 B (Final Batch)

**BADGER** | বারবার চাপ দেওয়া, জ্বালানো
To repeatedly ask someone in an annoying and persistent way; to pester.
✅ pester, nag, harass, hound, bother, plague, pressure, torment
❌ leave alone, respect, comfort, encourage, support, assist
📝 She **badgered** the manager daily until he finally approved her leave request.

**BALK** | বাধা দেওয়া, হঠাৎ থামা, আপত্তি করা
To hesitate or refuse to proceed; to obstruct or thwart someone.
✅ hesitate, refuse, resist, object, shy away, draw back, recoil
❌ proceed, agree, comply, continue, advance, embrace, welcome
📝 He **balked** at the idea of moving to a new city for a job he wasn't sure about.

**BELABOUR** | অতিরিক্ত ব্যাখ্যা করা, মারধর করা
To argue or elaborate a subject to an excessive degree; to beat repeatedly.
✅ overexplain, dwell on, emphasise excessively, hammer home, stress
❌ summarise, overlook, mention briefly, be concise, gloss over
📝 There is no need to **belabour** the point — everyone already understands.

**BERATE** | তিরস্কার করা, কঠোরভাবে ভর্ৎসনা করা
To scold or criticize someone angrily; to reprimand sharply and at length.
✅ scold, reprimand, rebuke, criticize, chastise, bawl out, castigate
❌ praise, commend, compliment, encourage, support, reward, applaud
📝 The coach **berated** the players after the poor first-half performance.

**BLAND** | নিরস, স্বাদহীন, অনুত্তেজক
Lacking strong features or characteristics; mild, dull, and uninteresting.
✅ dull, insipid, tasteless, boring, uninteresting, flat, uninspiring
❌ stimulating, flavourful, exciting, interesting, lively, strong, bold
📝 The soup was **bland** and needed salt, pepper, and much more seasoning.

**BLASPHEMY** | ঈশ্বর বা ধর্মের নিন্দা
The act of speaking irreverently about God or sacred things; sacrilege.
✅ sacrilege, profanity, desecration, irreverence, impiety, heresy
❌ reverence, piety, devotion, worship, respect, sacredness
📝 In many societies, **blasphemy** is still treated as a serious criminal offence.
