**DISSENT** | দ্বিমত, বিরোধিতা
The holding or expression of opinions at variance with those held officially.
✅ disagreement, opposition, objection, protest, defiance, conflict
❌ agreement, conformity, consent, accord, compliance, approval
📝 One member of the panel registered his **dissent** by leaving the room.

**DIVERGE** | বিভিন্নভাবে যাওয়া, আলাদা হওয়া
To develop in different directions; to depart from a set course or standard.
✅ separate, split, differ, branch off, deviate, vary, fork
❌ converge, unite, agree, meet, join, merge, come together
📝 Our opinions began to **diverge** as we went deeper into the discussion.

**DOCILE** | বাধ্য, সহজে নিয়ন্ত্রণযোগ্য, নম্র
Ready to accept control or instruction without resistance; submissive.
✅ obedient, submissive, manageable, tame, gentle, meek, compliant
❌ unruly, defiant, disobedient, wild, headstrong, rebellious, difficult
📝 The **docile** animal was easily guided by its handler without any resistance.

**DOLEFUL** | দুঃখী, বিষণ্ণ, শোকাকুল
Feeling or expressing great sorrow or distress; mournful and sad.
✅ sorrowful, mournful, sad, woeful, gloomy, melancholy, wistful
❌ cheerful, joyful, happy, bright, lively, upbeat, contented
📝 She gave a **doleful** sigh before delivering the news no one wanted to hear.

**DORMANT** | সুপ্ত, নিষ্ক্রিয়, ঘুমন্ত অবস্থায়
Temporarily inactive; in a state of rest or sleep; latent and not expressed.
✅ inactive, sleeping, latent, quiescent, resting, suspended, hibernating
❌ active, awake, functioning, alert, operational, alive, busy
📝 The **dormant** volcano had not erupted in nearly five hundred years.

**DUBIOUS** | সন্দেহজনক, সংশয়পূর্ণ
Hesitating or doubting; not to be relied on; of suspect quality.
✅ doubtful, suspicious, uncertain, questionable, shady, unreliable
❌ trustworthy, reliable, certain, definite, honest, genuine, credible
📝 She gave a **dubious** look when he claimed to have finished all the work alone.

---

## 🔤 E–Z (Final Critical Words)

**ECCENTRIC** | অদ্ভুত স্বভাবের মানুষ
A person or behaviour unconventionally different from the norm; odd.
✅ quirky, unusual, strange, odd, unconventional, peculiar, idiosyncratic
❌ normal, conventional, typical, ordinary, standard, mainstream
📝 Everyone in the office found the **eccentric** new hire fascinating.

**EMPATHY** | সহানুভূতি, অন্যের অনুভূতি বোঝার ক্ষমতা
The ability to understand and share the feelings of another person.
✅ compassion, understanding, sympathy, sensitivity, rapport, care
❌ apathy, indifference, callousness, coldness, insensitivity
📝 Great teachers show **empathy** for students who are struggling.

**EUPHORIA** | অত্যানন্দ, অসাধারণ আনন্দ
A feeling or state of intense excitement and happiness; elation.
✅ elation, joy, ecstasy, bliss, delight, rapture, happiness, exhilaration
❌ depression, sadness, misery, gloom, despair, anguish, suffering
📝 There was a sense of national **euphoria** after the team won the championship.

**EVOKE** | উদ্রেক করা, স্মরণ করা, জাগানো
To bring or recall a feeling, memory, or image to the conscious mind.
✅ invoke, summon, bring to mind, arouse, inspire, awaken, provoke
❌ suppress, forget, ignore, dismiss, bury, quell, extinguish
📝 The old photograph **evoked** memories of a childhood she had almost forgotten.

**EXALT** | উঁচুতে তোলা, গৌরবান্বিত করা
To hold someone or something in very high regard; to praise highly.
✅ praise, glorify, extol, elevate, magnify, honour, celebrate, laud
❌ demote, belittle, demean, condemn, criticize, lower, dismiss
📝 The nation chose to **exalt** its war heroes with a grand memorial ceremony.

**EXASPERATE** | অত্যন্ত বিরক্ত করা, মেজাজ নষ্ট করা
To irritate intensely; to make someone very annoyed or frustrated.
✅ infuriate, irritate, annoy, frustrate, anger, provoke, aggravate
❌ please, calm, satisfy, soothe, delight, comfort, appease
📝 His habit of interrupting every sentence began to thoroughly **exasperate** her.

**EXEMPLIFY** | উদাহরণ হওয়া, দৃষ্টান্ত দেওয়া
To be or give a typical example of something; to illustrate with examples.
✅ illustrate, demonstrate, typify, represent, show, personify, embody
❌ misrepresent, obscure, hide, distort, contradict, negate
📝 Her career choices perfectly **exemplify** the kind of courage this work demands.

**EXONERATE** | দোষ থেকে মুক্ত করা
To officially absolve someone from blame or a criminal charge; to clear.
✅ absolve, clear, acquit, vindicate, pardon, free, release, discharge
❌ convict, blame, condemn, charge, accuse, find guilty, sentence
📝 The new evidence completely **exonerated** the man who had spent years in prison.

**EXPLOIT** | শোষণ করা; কাজে লাগানো; কীর্তি
To make full use of and derive benefit from; to use unfairly; a bold act.
✅ use, utilize, take advantage of, abuse, capitalize on, manipulate
❌ protect, ignore, leave alone, waste, respect, safeguard
📝 The company was accused of trying to **exploit** its workers for greater profit.

**FABRICATE** | মিথ্যা তৈরি করা; বানানো
To invent false information to deceive; to construct or manufacture.
✅ invent, forge, fake, falsify, make up, concoct, manufacture, construct
❌ reveal, confirm, admit, expose, tell the truth, authenticate
📝 The report turned out to have been entirely **fabricated** by the journalist.

**FATHOM** | গভীরতা বোঝা; বুঝতে পারা
To understand a difficult problem after much thought; to measure depth.
✅ understand, grasp, comprehend, decipher, figure out, work out
❌ misunderstand, be confused, miss the point, fail to grasp
📝 She could not **fathom** how anyone could forgive such a terrible betrayal.

**FEIGN** | ভান করা, ভণিতা করা
To pretend to be affected by a feeling, state, or injury; to fake.
✅ pretend, fake, simulate, act, bluff, put on, affect, imitate
❌ feel genuinely, be authentic, express truly, be sincere
📝 He **feigned** illness to avoid attending the meeting he dreaded.

**FLOURISH** | ফলপ্রসূ হওয়া, সমৃদ্ধ হওয়া
To grow vigorously; to prosper; to be in good health or spirits.
✅ thrive, prosper, bloom, succeed, grow, blossom, develop, advance
❌ wither, decline, fail, struggle, decay, perish, die, stagnate
📝 Under her leadership, the team began to truly **flourish** for the first time.

**FORSAKE** | ত্যাগ করা, পরিত্যাগ করা
To abandon or leave someone or something; to give up or renounce.
✅ abandon, desert, leave, renounce, discard, give up, reject, quit
❌ keep, maintain, support, stay with, help, remain, protect
📝 He refused to **forsake** his principles even when it cost him everything.

**FRACAS** | হৈচৈ, মারামারি, ঝামেলা
A noisy, disruptive disturbance; a noisy quarrel or fight.
✅ brawl, uproar, fight, disturbance, scuffle, commotion, row, ruckus
❌ calm, peace, agreement, quiet, harmony, order, tranquility
📝 A **fracas** broke out near the stadium after the match ended controversially.

**FRUGALITY** | মিতব্যয়িতা, সাশ্রয়
The quality of being economical with money or food; avoidance of waste.
✅ thrift, economy, carefulness, prudence, sparingness, restraint
❌ extravagance, wastefulness, lavishness, profligacy, indulgence
📝 Years of **frugality** allowed her to retire comfortably before the age of fifty.

**GALLANTRY** | বীরত্ব, সাহসিকতা, ভদ্রতা
Courageous behaviour, especially in battle; polite attention shown to women.
✅ bravery, courage, valour, boldness, heroism, honour, chivalry
❌ cowardice, timidity, rudeness, dishonour, weakness, fear
📝 He was awarded a medal for his extraordinary **gallantry** during the rescue.

**GARRULITY** | বকবকানি, বাচালতা
The quality or tendency of talking too much; long-windedness.
✅ talkativeness, verbosity, loquacity, wordiness, long-windedness
❌ brevity, silence, conciseness, terseness, reticence, quietness
📝 His **garrulity** during meetings tried everyone's patience to the limit.

**GENIAL** | মিলনশীল, উষ্ণ, বন্ধুত্বপূর্ণ
Friendly and cheerful; pleasantly warm and cheerful in manner.
✅ friendly, warm, affable, pleasant, agreeable, jovial, good-humoured
❌ cold, unfriendly, hostile, rude, unfriendly, sullen, grumpy
📝 The **genial** host made every single guest feel immediately at home.

**GESTICULATE** | ইশারা করা, হাত নাড়া
To use gestures, especially dramatic ones, instead of speaking or to emphasise.
✅ gesture, signal, wave, motion, point, indicate with hands
❌ stand still, remain composed, use words only, be still
📝 He **gesticulated** wildly trying to get the attention of the passing driver.

**GLUTTONY** | পেটুকামি, অতিরিক্ত খাওয়া
Habitual greed or excess in eating and drinking; overindulgence.
✅ greed, overindulgence, excess, piggishness, voracity, intemperance
❌ temperance, moderation, restraint, self-control, frugality, abstinence
📝 **Gluttony** at the buffet left him feeling deeply uncomfortable for hours.

**GRACIOUS** | মার্জিত, উদার, সদয়
Courteous, kind, and pleasant, especially in a condescending way from superiors.
✅ courteous, kind, polite, benevolent, warm, considerate, magnanimous
❌ rude, cold, unpleasant, inconsiderate, ungracious, mean, abrupt
📝 The queen made a **gracious** speech thanking everyone who had served her.

**GRANDEUR** | মহিমা, জাঁকজমক, গৌরব
Splendour and impressiveness; the quality of being grand, magnificent.
✅ magnificence, splendour, majesty, grandness, glory, impressiveness
❌ simplicity, humility, modesty, bareness, insignificance, smallness
📝 The **grandeur** of the ancient palace left visitors breathless with admiration.

**GRAPPLE** | সংগ্রাম করা, মোকাবেলা করা
To engage in a close fight or struggle without weapons; to deal with something difficult.
✅ struggle, wrestle, fight, deal with, tackle, contend, cope with
❌ ignore, avoid, give up, surrender, flee, sidestep, abandon
📝 For years, the city has had to **grapple** with the problem of homelessness.

**GRATUITOUS** | অকারণ, অপ্রয়োজনীয়, বিনামূল্যে
Uncalled for; lacking good reason; unwarranted and unjustified.
✅ unnecessary, unjustified, unwarranted, unprovoked, baseless, pointless
❌ justified, warranted, necessary, reasonable, appropriate, called for
📝 The film was criticized for its **gratuitous** violence that served no narrative purpose.

**GRAVITY** | গুরুত্ব, গম্ভীরতা
Extreme importance; the quality of being serious and weighty in nature.
✅ seriousness, importance, weight, severity, gravity, solemnity, magnitude
❌ levity, triviality, insignificance, lightness, humour, playfulness
📝 She underestimated the **gravity** of the situation until it was almost too late.

**GRIEVOUS** | তীব্র, ভয়ানক, অত্যন্ত কষ্টের
(Of something bad) very severe or serious; extremely painful; tragic.
✅ severe, serious, grave, dreadful, painful, terrible, awful, dire
❌ minor, mild, slight, insignificant, trivial, unimportant, harmless
📝 The nation mourned the **grievous** loss of so many young soldiers.

**GUISE** | বেশ, ছদ্মবেশ, রূপ
An external form or appearance; a style of dress; a false pretence.
✅ appearance, form, disguise, pretence, facade, semblance, cover
❌ truth, reality, genuine form, sincerity, authenticity, openness
📝 The spy operated under the **guise** of being a humble travelling merchant.

**HACKNEYED** | মামুলি, ক্লিশে, পুরনো ধারার
Made unoriginal by overuse; lacking freshness because of overuse.
✅ overused, cliché, trite, stale, unoriginal, predictable, banal
❌ original, fresh, creative, novel, innovative, unique, interesting
📝 The essay was full of **hackneyed** phrases that added nothing to the argument.

**HALLMARK** | বৈশিষ্ট্য, গুণমানের চিহ্ন
A distinctive characteristic or feature; a mark of quality or excellence.
✅ feature, characteristic, trademark, sign, symbol, attribute, indicator
❌ flaw, defect, weakness, blemish, absence of quality
📝 Attention to detail is the **hallmark** of every product she creates.

**HAPLESS** | দুর্ভাগ্যবান, বেচারা
Unfortunate and deserving of pity; unlucky; having no good fortune.
✅ unfortunate, unlucky, wretched, ill-fated, pitiable, luckless
❌ fortunate, lucky, blessed, successful, favoured, happy, thriving
📝 The **hapless** traveller arrived at the wrong airport for the second time.

**HARBINGER** | পূর্বাভাস দেওয়া মানুষ বা জিনিস
A person or thing that announces or signals the approach of something.
✅ omen, sign, herald, signal, indicator, portent, forerunner, precursor
❌ conclusion, result, aftermath, outcome, consequence, end
📝 The first robin in spring is often seen as a **harbinger** of warmer weather.

**HARMONIOUS** | সুরেলা, সামঞ্জস্যপূর্ণ, মনোরম
Forming a pleasing or consistent whole; free from disagreement.
✅ melodious, pleasing, unified, peaceful, consistent, cooperative, agreeable
❌ discordant, jarring, conflicting, disjointed, chaotic, clashing, inharmonious
📝 The **harmonious** colours in the painting created a very calming effect.

**HASTEN** | তাড়াতাড়ি করা, ত্বরান্বিত করা
To be quick to do something; to move or travel hurriedly; to speed up.
✅ hurry, rush, accelerate, speed up, quicken, run, dash, advance
❌ slow, delay, dawdle, linger, wait, halt, impede, stall
📝 She **hastened** to correct the misunderstanding before it caused more damage.

**HAUGHTY** | অহংকারী, দম্ভী
Arrogantly superior and disdainful toward others; proud and condescending.
✅ arrogant, proud, conceited, disdainful, condescending, supercilious
❌ humble, modest, polite, down-to-earth, respectful, unassuming
📝 His **haughty** refusal to listen made it impossible to work alongside him.

**HEINOUS** | জঘন্য, নিষ্ঠুর, অত্যন্ত খারাপ
Utterly odious or wicked; shockingly evil and beyond any justification.
✅ wicked, atrocious, monstrous, evil, abominable, vile, cruel, terrible
❌ good, admirable, kind, virtuous, honourable, decent, praiseworthy
📝 The court handed down the harshest sentence for such a **heinous** crime.

**HERESY** | ধর্মদ্রোহিতা, গোড়ামি বিরোধী মত
Belief or opinion contrary to orthodox religious doctrine; unorthodox thinking.
✅ blasphemy, unorthodoxy, dissent, apostasy, heterodoxy, nonconformism
❌ orthodoxy, conformity, traditional belief, piety, devoutness
📝 In those times, questioning the church's authority was considered **heresy**.

**HIERARCHY** | শ্রেণীবিভাগ, ক্রমসম্পর্ক, মর্যাদার ক্রম
A system in which things are ranked above each other according to status.
✅ ranking, order, chain of command, structure, pecking order, grading
❌ equality, flat structure, democracy, chaos, disorder, randomness
📝 She rose through the **hierarchy** of the organization over twenty years.

**HYPOCRITICAL** | ভণ্ডামিমূলক, দুই মুখো
Behaving in a way that contradicts one's claimed beliefs; insincere.
✅ two-faced, insincere, sanctimonious, phony, deceptive, false, dishonest
❌ sincere, genuine, authentic, honest, consistent, truthful, transparent
📝 It was deeply **hypocritical** of him to preach honesty while cheating on his taxes.

**IDIOSYNCRATIC** | ব্যক্তিগত, স্বতন্ত্র, অনন্য
Relating to idiosyncrasy; peculiar or individual; unique to a person.
✅ peculiar, distinctive, individual, unique, eccentric, characteristic
❌ common, typical, ordinary, normal, conventional, standard
📝 Her **idiosyncratic** teaching style baffled some but inspired many others.

**IGNITE** | জ্বালানো, উদ্দীপিত করা
To catch fire or cause something to catch fire; to arouse a strong feeling.
✅ light, set alight, kindle, spark, trigger, inspire, arouse, inflame
❌ extinguish, dampen, douse, cool, suppress, quench, discourage
📝 The protest march helped to **ignite** a national conversation about equality.

**ILLUMINATE** | আলোকিত করা, স্পষ্ট করা
To light up; to help to clarify or explain; to make intelligible.
✅ light up, brighten, clarify, explain, enlighten, reveal, shed light on
❌ darken, obscure, confuse, hide, mislead, cloud, complicate
📝 The documentary helped to **illuminate** aspects of the conflict that were little known.

**IMBUE** | ভরিয়ে দেওয়া, রং করা, প্রভাবিত করা
To inspire or permeate with a feeling or quality; to saturate or dye.
✅ fill, inspire, infuse, instil, permeate, suffuse, saturate
❌ drain, empty, deprive, remove, strip, cleanse
📝 Her training **imbued** her with a sense of duty that lasted a lifetime.

**IMPASSE** | অচলাবস্থা, সংকট
A situation in which no progress is possible, especially because of disagreement.
✅ deadlock, stalemate, standstill, blockade, gridlock, standoff
❌ breakthrough, resolution, agreement, progress, settlement
📝 Negotiations hit an **impasse** when neither side would accept any compromise.

**IMPECCABLE** | নিখুঁত, ত্রুটিহীন
Without fault or error; in accordance with the highest standards; perfect.
✅ perfect, flawless, faultless, spotless, unblemished, irreproachable
❌ flawed, imperfect, faulty, defective, blemished, deficient
📝 The ambassador's **impeccable** manners left a lasting impression on all guests.

**IMPUDENT** | বেয়াদব, ঔদ্ধত্যপূর্ণ, নির্লজ্জ
Not showing due respect for another person; impertinent and shameless.
✅ rude, cheeky, impertinent, brazen, bold, audacious, insolent
❌ respectful, polite, humble, deferential, courteous, well-mannered
📝 The child gave an **impudent** reply that shocked everyone in the room.

**INCANDESCENT** | দীপ্তিমান, প্রচণ্ড উজ্জ্বল; অত্যন্ত রাগী
Emitting light as a result of being heated; filled with passion or rage.
✅ glowing, brilliant, luminous, radiant, blazing, furious, enraged
❌ dull, dim, extinguished, calm, composed, cool, collected
📝 He was **incandescent** with rage when he discovered what had happened.

**INCENTIVE** | প্রণোদনা, উৎসাহ দেওয়ার জিনিস
Something that motivates or encourages someone to do something.
✅ motivation, encouragement, stimulus, inducement, reward, impetus
❌ disincentive, deterrent, discouragement, punishment, obstacle
📝 A salary bonus was offered as an **incentive** for completing the project early.

**INDIGNANT** | ক্রুদ্ধ, ক্ষুব্ধ, রাগান্বিত (অন্যায়ে)
Feeling or showing anger or annoyance at what is perceived as unfair.
✅ angry, outraged, offended, resentful, annoyed, furious, righteous
❌ pleased, satisfied, calm, indifferent, happy, approving, content
📝 She was deeply **indignant** at being accused of something she had not done.

**INDULGE** | প্রশ্রয় দেওয়া, নিজেকে মেনে নেওয়া
To allow oneself or someone else to have or do what they want freely.
✅ pamper, spoil, treat, gratify, satisfy, give in to, accommodate
❌ restrain, deny, discipline, withhold, refuse, deprive, limit
📝 She decided to **indulge** herself with a long holiday after the difficult year.

**INEPT** | অদক্ষ, অনুপযুক্ত, অপারগ
Having or showing no skill; clumsy and lacking competence.
✅ incompetent, unskilled, clumsy, awkward, incapable, inefficient
❌ skilled, capable, competent, adept, proficient, expert
📝 The **inept** manager's poor decisions led the project to a complete failure.

**INFURIATE** | অত্যন্ত রাগান্বিত করা
To make extremely angry; to enrage; to fill with fury.
✅ enrage, anger, irritate, madden, incense, provoke, exasperate
❌ calm, please, soothe, delight, pacify, appease, satisfy
📝 His casual attitude to deadlines began to genuinely **infuriate** the entire team.

**INHIBIT** | বাধা দেওয়া, রোধ করা, দমন করা
To hinder, restrain, or prevent an action or process; to suppress.
✅ prevent, hinder, suppress, restrain, restrict, block, hold back
❌ encourage, enable, promote, facilitate, allow, support, release
📝 Fear of failure can **inhibit** people from taking the risks needed to grow.

**INSIGHTFUL** | অন্তর্দৃষ্টিসম্পন্ন, প্রখর দৃষ্টির
Having or showing an accurate and deep understanding; perceptive.
✅ perceptive, astute, discerning, observant, shrewd, wise, intelligent
❌ dull, unobservant, oblivious, ignorant, shallow, obtuse, unaware
📝 Her **insightful** comments changed the direction of the entire discussion.

**INSOLENCE** | ঔদ্ধত্য, অসভ্যতা, বেয়াদবি
Rude and disrespectful behaviour; impudent speech or conduct.
✅ rudeness, impudence, disrespect, arrogance, cheek, insubordination
❌ respect, politeness, courtesy, deference, humility, decorum
📝 The student's **insolence** toward the teacher was met with immediate suspension.

**INTEGRITY** | সততা, নৈতিক দৃঢ়তা
The quality of being honest and having strong moral principles; uprightness.
✅ honesty, honour, virtue, sincerity, uprightness, ethics, trustworthiness
❌ dishonesty, corruption, fraud, deception, immorality, duplicity
📝 He never compromised his **integrity**, even when it would have been easy to do so.

**INTERVENE** | হস্তক্ষেপ করা, মধ্যস্থতা করা
To come between so as to prevent or alter an event; to mediate.
✅ interfere, mediate, step in, interrupt, intercede, get involved
❌ ignore, stay out, withdraw, avoid, stand aside, let happen
📝 The teacher had to **intervene** to stop the argument from escalating further.

**INTROSPECTIVE** | আত্মবিশ্লেষণমূলক, অন্তর্মুখী
Characterized by or involving reflection on one's own thoughts and feelings.
✅ reflective, thoughtful, self-analytical, contemplative, inward-looking
❌ outgoing, extroverted, impulsive, thoughtless, unreflective
📝 She became more **introspective** after the accident, questioning her priorities.

**INVECTIVE** | গালিগালাজ, কটু সমালোচনা, তিরস্কার
Insulting, abusive, or highly critical language; verbal attack.
✅ abuse, criticism, denunciation, tirade, rebuke, condemnation, attack
❌ praise, compliment, flattery, commendation, encouragement

📝 His argument was shown to be completely **untenable** under cross-examination.

**VACILLATE** | দ্বিধাগ্রস্ত হওয়া, এক মতে অটল না থাকা
To waver between different opinions or actions; to be indecisive; to fluctuate.
✅ waver, hesitate, be indecisive, fluctuate, dither, oscillate, swing
❌ be decisive, be firm, commit, choose, stand firm, resolve, persist
📝 She continued to **vacillate** between accepting and rejecting the offer.

**VALIDATE** | বৈধ করা, নিশ্চিত করা, সমর্থন করা
To demonstrate or support the truth or value of something; to confirm.
✅ confirm, verify, support, endorse, authenticate, substantiate, approve
❌ invalidate, disprove, deny, negate, refute, reject, contradict
📝 The experiment **validated** the hypothesis that had been made six months earlier.

**VALOUR** | বীরত্ব, সাহসিকতা, যুদ্ধের সাহস
Great courage, especially in battle; brave conduct and behaviour.
✅ bravery, courage, heroism, gallantry, boldness, fearlessness, daring
❌ cowardice, timidity, fearfulness, weakness, hesitation, surrender
📝 The soldier was awarded a medal for exceptional **valour** during the siege.

**VANITY** | অহংকার, আত্মম্ভরিতা, অর্থহীনতা
Excessive pride in or admiration of one's own appearance or achievements.
✅ pride, conceit, arrogance, narcissism, self-admiration, egotism
❌ humility, modesty, self-awareness, unselfishness, simplicity
📝 His **vanity** was so extreme that he had mirrors in every room of the house.

**VENGEANCE** | প্রতিশোধ, বদলা
Punishment inflicted or retribution exacted for an injury or wrong.
✅ revenge, retribution, retaliation, payback, reprisal, comeuppance
❌ forgiveness, mercy, pardon, clemency, letting go, reconciliation
📝 He spent years plotting **vengeance** against those who had destroyed his career.

**VERBOSE** | বাচাল, অতিরিক্ত শব্দের ব্যবহারকারী
Using more words than necessary; wordy and long-winded; excessively talkative.
✅ wordy, long-winded, garrulous, prolix, rambling, loquacious
❌ concise, brief, succinct, terse, to the point, laconic, pithy
📝 The report was heavily **verbose** and could easily have been cut to half the length.

**VERBOSE PHRASES CONTRASTED:**
- Verbose: "At this point in time" → Concise: "Now"
- Verbose: "Due to the fact that" → Concise: "Because"

**VEXATION** | বিরক্তি, ক্রোধ, কষ্ট
The state of being annoyed, frustrated, or worried; something that causes this.
✅ irritation, frustration, annoyance, anger, exasperation, displeasure
❌ satisfaction, pleasure, delight, calm, contentment, relief
📝 His repeated tardiness was a constant source of **vexation** for the whole team.

**VITRIOL** | তীব্র ও ঘৃণামিশ্রিত সমালোচনা
Cruel and bitter criticism; hostile and abusive language.
✅ bitterness, venom, spite, hostility, sarcasm, malice, viciousness
❌ kindness, praise, warmth, gentle criticism, constructive feedback
📝 The editorial was full of **vitriol** aimed directly at the opposition party.

**VOCATION** | পেশা, ডাক, জীবনের লক্ষ্য
A strong feeling of suitability for a particular career or occupation; a calling.
✅ calling, career, profession, purpose, mission, occupation, duty
❌ hobby, pastime, distraction, avoidance, accidental path
📝 Teaching was not just a job for her — it was a genuine **vocation**.

**VOLATILE** | অস্থির, উদ্বায়ী, পরিবর্তনশীল
Liable to change rapidly and unpredictably; likely to develop in a bad way.
✅ unstable, explosive, unpredictable, changeable, erratic, turbulent
❌ stable, calm, consistent, predictable, even, steady, reliable
📝 The political situation remained deeply **volatile** throughout the election season.

**VORACIOUS** | অতিভোজী, লোলুপ, তীব্র আগ্রহী
Having a very eager approach to an activity; excessively greedy; hungry.
✅ insatiable, greedy, hungry, eager, avid, ravenous, gluttonous
❌ moderate, restrained, satisfied, content, temperate, abstemious
📝 She was a **voracious** reader who always had three books on the go at once.

**VULNERABLE** | দুর্বল, ঝুঁকিপূর্ণ, আক্রমণের সুযোগ থাকা
Susceptible to physical or emotional attack or harm; exposed to danger.
✅ susceptible, exposed, weak, defenceless, unprotected, helpless
❌ protected, strong, resilient, safe, invulnerable, invincible, fortified
📝 Young children are the most **vulnerable** members of any society in crisis.

**WANE** | হ্রাস পাওয়া, দুর্বল হওয়া
To decrease in vigor, power, or extent; to decline gradually.
✅ decrease, decline, diminish, fade, weaken, dwindle, ebb, subside
❌ increase, grow, strengthen, expand, rise, wax, flourish, intensify
📝 As winter approached, daylight began to **wane** noticeably with each passing day.

**WANTON** | উদ্দেশ্যহীন, অকারণে ক্ষতিকর, নৈতিকতাহীন
Deliberate and unprovoked; done without reason; sexually immoral.
✅ deliberate, unprovoked, malicious, unjustified, reckless, willful
❌ justified, provoked, restrained, moral, considered, controlled
📝 The **wanton** destruction of the ancient trees angered environmental groups.

**WARY** | সতর্ক, সাবধান, সন্দেহপ্রবণ
Feeling or showing caution about possible dangers or problems.
✅ cautious, careful, alert, vigilant, watchful, suspicious, guarded
❌ careless, trusting, naive, reckless, inattentive, unsuspecting
📝 She was **wary** of any deal that seemed too good to be true.

**WHIM** | খেয়াল, হঠাৎ ইচ্ছা, আকস্মিক ভাবনা
A sudden desire or change of mind; a capricious and often unaccountable notion.
✅ impulse, fancy, notion, caprice, urge, sudden idea, quirk
❌ plan, decision, principle, commitment, deliberate choice
📝 She bought the painting on a **whim** and never regretted it for a single moment.

**WIELD** | ব্যবহার করা, পরিচালনা করা
To hold and use a weapon or tool effectively; to exert power or influence.
✅ use, handle, employ, exercise, exert, apply, control, brandish
❌ surrender, release, relinquish, abandon, give up, ignore
📝 The prime minister knew how to **wield** political power with quiet authority.

**WILY** | কুটিল, ধূর্ত, চালাক
Good at getting what one wants through clever planning; crafty.
✅ cunning, crafty, sly, devious, shrewd, clever, calculating, tricky
❌ naive, honest, straightforward, guileless, simple, trusting
📝 The **wily** negotiator had anticipated every objection and had a reply for each.

**WOEFUL** | দুঃখজনক, করুণ, শোকপূর্ণ
Characterised by sorrow or misery; causing or deserving strong disapproval.
✅ sad, unfortunate, dreadful, pitiful, awful, terrible, deplorable
❌ wonderful, excellent, joyful, superb, praiseworthy, admirable
📝 The team's **woeful** performance in the final match disappointed all their supporters.

**YEARNING** | তীব্র আকাঙ্ক্ষা, আকুল হওয়া
A feeling of intense longing for something; deep desire or craving.
✅ longing, desire, craving, wish, hunger, ache, pining, wistfulness
❌ contentment, satisfaction, indifference, having, possessing
📝 She felt a deep **yearning** for the simplicity of her childhood years.

**ZEALOT** | গোঁড়া সমর্থক, উগ্রপন্থী
A person who is fanatical and uncompromising in pursuit of an ideal.
✅ fanatic, extremist, devotee, true believer, radical, fundamentalist
❌ moderate, pragmatist, open-minded person, skeptic, doubter
📝 He was so passionate about his cause that critics dismissed him as a **zealot**.

# 📚 EXTRA BATCH — High-Frequency CUET Words Not Yet Covered

---

## 🔤 A (Extra)

**ABATE** | কমানো, থামানো, দুর্বল করা
To become less intense, severe, or widespread; to reduce or lessen.
✅ reduce, lessen, decrease, diminish, subside, moderate, ease, wane
❌ increase, intensify, worsen, grow, strengthen, escalate, heighten
📝 The storm began to **abate** after midnight, and calm slowly returned.

**ABERRANT** | স্বাভাবিকতার বাইরে, অস্বাভাবিক, বিপথগামী
Departing from an accepted standard; deviating from what is normal.
✅ abnormal, deviant, unusual, atypical, irregular, anomalous, strange
❌ normal, typical, standard, conventional, expected, regular, orthodox
📝 The **aberrant** results in the experiment prompted a full re-examination.

**ABJURE** | শপথ করে ত্যাগ করা, প্রকাশ্যে অস্বীকার করা
To formally reject or disavow a formerly held belief; to renounce.
✅ renounce, reject, abandon, disavow, retract, recant, forswear
❌ embrace, uphold, affirm, support, maintain, defend, hold on to
📝 Under pressure, he chose to **abjure** the radical ideas he had once championed.

**ABSCOND** | পালিয়ে যাওয়া, লুকিয়ে পালানো
To leave hurriedly and secretly, typically to escape detection or punishment.
✅ flee, escape, run away, bolt, disappear, slip away, vanish, desert
❌ stay, remain, face, return, surrender, appear, come back, report
📝 The cashier **absconded** with a large sum before the fraud was discovered.

**ABSTAIN** | বিরত থাকা, পরহেজ করা, ভোট না দেওয়া
To restrain oneself from doing or enjoying something; to formally decline.
✅ refrain, desist, hold back, forgo, avoid, withhold, give up, resist
❌ indulge, partake, participate, engage, consume, enjoy, join in
📝 Three committee members chose to **abstain** when the vote was called.

**ACCOST** | সরাসরি এগিয়ে গিয়ে কথা বলা
To approach and address someone boldly or aggressively; to confront.
✅ approach, confront, address, stop, waylay, buttonhole, intercept
❌ avoid, ignore, pass by, flee from, turn away, retreat from
📝 A stranger **accosted** him on the street and demanded directions to the station.

**ACQUIESCE** | নীরবে মত দেওয়া, নিরুপায় হয়ে মেনে নেওয়া
To accept something reluctantly but without protest; to comply passively.
✅ agree, consent, comply, yield, assent, go along, concede, submit
❌ resist, oppose, object, refuse, protest, reject, dispute, fight back
📝 Though unhappy, she chose to **acquiesce** to the board's final ruling.

**ACRIMONY** | তিক্ততা, কটু কথা, তীব্র বিদ্বেষ
Bitterness or ill feeling, especially in speech or manner.
✅ bitterness, hostility, rancour, spite, animosity, resentment, venom
❌ warmth, goodwill, friendliness, kindness, harmony, affection, amity
📝 The debate was marked by unusual **acrimony** from the very beginning.

**ACUMEN** | তীক্ষ্ণ বুদ্ধি, বিচক্ষণতা, সূক্ষ্ম দৃষ্টি
The ability to make good judgements; shrewd and practical intelligence.
✅ insight, intelligence, shrewdness, astuteness, wisdom, perceptiveness
❌ stupidity, naivety, dullness, ignorance, foolishness, short-sightedness

**ADMONISH** | সতর্ক করা, মৃদু ভর্ৎসনা করা
To warn or reprimand someone firmly but not harshly; to advise seriously.
✅ warn, caution, rebuke, counsel, reprove, chide, urge, advise
❌ praise, commend, reward, encourage, support, congratulate, approve

**AFFECTATION** | ভান করা, কৃত্রিম আচরণ, নাটকীয়তা
Behaviour, speech, or writing that is artificial and designed to impress.
✅ pretence, artificiality, posturing, mannerism, show, insincerity
❌ naturalness, sincerity, authenticity, genuineness, simplicity, openness
📝 His **affectation** of speaking with a foreign accent annoyed his colleagues.

**AGGREGATE** | সমষ্টি, মোট, একত্রিত করা
A whole formed by combining several separate elements; total.
✅ total, sum, whole, collection, combination, accumulated, combined
❌ individual, separate, part, component, single, divided, fragmented

**AGGRIEVE** | অপমান করা, ক্ষতি করা, কষ্ট দেওয়া
To feel resentment at unfair treatment; to distress or offend someone.
✅ offend, wrong, harm, distress, injure, hurt, upset, trouble
❌ please, satisfy, help, benefit, comfort, soothe, reward, honour

**ALACRITY** | তৎপরতা, উৎসাহ, তাড়াতাড়ি করার ইচ্ছা
Brisk and cheerful readiness; willingness and eagerness to act promptly.
✅ eagerness, willingness, speed, enthusiasm, promptness, keenness
❌ slowness, reluctance, laziness, hesitation, apathy, unwillingness

**ALLAY** | শান্ত করা, উপশম করা, কমানো
To reduce fear, suspicion, or worry; to relieve or alleviate pain.
✅ ease, calm, soothe, relieve, pacify, quieten, lessen, reduce
❌ worsen, heighten, intensify, stoke, aggravate, increase, provoke

**ALLUDE** | পরোক্ষে উল্লেখ করা, ইঙ্গিত করা
To suggest or call attention to indirectly; to mention briefly in passing.
✅ hint, suggest, refer, imply, insinuate, indicate, touch on, mention
❌ state directly, declare, specify by name, announce plainly, assert

**AMELIORATE** | উন্নত করা, ভালো করা, কষ্ট কমানো
To make something bad or unsatisfactory better; to improve conditions.
✅ improve, better, enhance, relieve, remedy, upgrade, ease, help
❌ worsen, aggravate, damage, harm, neglect, deteriorate, impair

**AMEND** | সংশোধন করা, পরিবর্তন করা
To make minor changes in a text or law to correct or improve it.
✅ revise, correct, modify, alter, update, reform, rectify, change
❌ maintain, keep, leave unchanged, worsen, ignore, preserve, neglect

❌ hostile, cold, unfriendly, rude, disagreeable, difficult, unpleasant

**AMORPHOUS** | আকারহীন, অনির্দিষ্ট আকারের, অস্পষ্ট
Without a clearly defined shape or form; vague and hard to classify.
✅ shapeless, formless, vague, undefined, indistinct, unstructured
❌ defined, clear, structured, shaped, organized, formed, distinct

**ANATHEMA** | অভিশাপ, ঘৃণিত বস্তু বা ব্যক্তি, নিষিদ্ধ
Something or someone strongly disliked or rejected; a formal curse.
✅ abomination, curse, taboo, bane, hatred, object of loathing
❌ blessing, favourite, beloved object, treasure, admiration

**ANTITHESIS** | বিপরীত, বৈপরীত্য, সম্পূর্ণ উল্টো
A person or thing that is the direct opposite of someone or something else.

❌ similarity, parallel, likeness, match, equivalent, same

**APATHY** | উদাসীনতা, নিরুদ্বেগ, আগ্রহহীনতা
Lack of interest, enthusiasm, or concern; emotional indifference.
✅ indifference, disinterest, passivity, lethargy, unconcern, detachment
❌ enthusiasm, passion, concern, interest, energy, eagerness, care

**APPROPRIATE** | যথাযথ, উপযুক্ত; দখল করা বা নেওয়া

Involving or requiring strenuous effort; difficult and tiring.
✅ difficult, strenuous, laborious, exhausting, tough, demanding, hard
❌ easy, effortless, simple, light, comfortable, manageable, pleasant

✅ eloquent, fluent, clear, expressive, well-spoken, coherent, lucid
❌ inarticulate, tongue-tied, mumbling, unclear, incoherent, confused

**ASSIDUOUS** | পরিশ্রমী, নিরলস, নিষ্ঠাবান
Showing great care and thoroughness; hardworking and diligent.
✅ diligent, hardworking, persistent, careful, thorough, industrious
❌ lazy, careless, negligent, sloppy, half-hearted, idle, inattentive

**ASSUAGE** | প্রশমিত করা, শান্ত করা, তৃষ্ণা মেটানো

✅ ease, relieve, soothe, calm, pacify, alleviate, appease, lessen
❌ worsen, intensify, aggravate, inflame, provoke, heighten, increase

**ASTUTE** | বিচক্ষণ, চতুর, সূক্ষ্মদর্শী
Having an ability to assess situations accurately; clever and perceptive.

❌ naive, gullible, foolish, obtuse, dull, unsuspecting, shortsighted

✅ reparation, amends, penance, reconciliation, expiation, restitution
❌ wrongdoing, harm, offence, sin, injury, crime, damage

**AUDACITY** | সাহসিকতা, দুঃসাহস, নির্লজ্জতা

✅ boldness, daring, nerve, impudence, bravery, cheek, courage

✅ increase, enlarge, expand, boost, supplement, add to, enhance
❌ reduce, decrease, diminish, shrink, cut back, lessen, minimize

**AUSPICIOUS** | শুভ, মঙ্গলময়, ভালো লক্ষণের

✅ favourable, promising, propitious, encouraging, fortunate, lucky

**AVARICE** | লোভ, কৃপণতা, অর্থলিপ্সা

✅ greed, cupidity, acquisitiveness, covetousness, miserliness, meanness

Demonstrating aggression and willingness to fight; warlike.
✅ aggressive, warlike, belligerent, combative, pugnacious, hostile

❌ celebrate, cheer, praise, rejoice, be happy about, welcome

**BENEVOLENCE** | পরোপকারিতা, দয়া, উদারতা
The quality of being well meaning and kindly; generous good will.

❌ selfishness, cruelty, malevolence, unkindness, avarice, hostility

**BESEECH** | মিনতি করা, আকুলভাবে অনুরোধ করা

❌ demand, order, command, instruct, dictate, insist, require

**BLATANT** | প্রকাশ্য, ডাহা, লজ্জাহীনভাবে স্পষ্ট
Done openly and unashamedly; obvious and impossible to ignore.
✅ obvious, brazen, flagrant, glaring, undisguised, shameless, outright
❌ subtle, hidden, covert, discreet, concealed, understated, quiet

**BOISTEROUS** | হৈচৈপূর্ণ, উচ্চকণ্ঠ, উদ্দাম
Noisy, energetic, and cheerful; rough or loud in behaviour.
✅ noisy, rowdy, loud, raucous, unruly, turbulent, exuberant, lively
❌ quiet, calm, restrained, orderly, peaceful, subdued, well-behaved
📝 The **boisterous** crowd celebrated the victory long into the night.

**BOMBASTIC** | ফাঁকা বড়াই করা, অতিরঞ্জিত ভাষায় কথা বলা
High-sounding language with little meaning; inflated and empty language.
✅ pompous, verbose, grandiose, exaggerated, inflated, pretentious, wordy
❌ simple, plain, direct, modest, understated, sincere, clear

**BOON** | আশীর্বাদ, সুবিধা, বরদান
A thing that is helpful or beneficial; a timely benefit or advantage.
✅ blessing, benefit, advantage, gift, godsend, windfall, help, asset
❌ curse, burden, disadvantage, bane, nuisance, problem, affliction

**BREVITY** | সংক্ষিপ্ততা, সংক্ষেপ, কম কথায় বলা

✅ conciseness, shortness, terseness, succinctness, economy of words
❌ verbosity, length, wordiness, long-windedness, prolixity, rambling
📝 Shakespeare himself wrote that **brevity** is the soul of wit.

**CAPRICIOUS** | খামখেয়ালী, অস্থিরমনা, অনিশ্চিত

**CENSURE** | নিন্দা করা, আনুষ্ঠানিক ভর্ৎসনা

✅ uproar, noise, commotion, outcry, shouting, din, racket, protest

**CLANDESTINE** | গোপন, লুকানো, আড়ালে করা
Kept secret, especially for illicit reasons; covert and hidden.
✅ secret, undercover, hidden, covert, stealthy, surreptitious, sneaky
❌ open, public, transparent, overt, above-board, disclosed, known

**CLEMENCY** | দয়া, ক্ষমা, নমনীয়তা
Mercy and leniency shown especially toward a convicted criminal.
✅ mercy, leniency, compassion, forgiveness, grace, pardon, mildness
❌ cruelty, harshness, severity, mercilessness, punishment, strictness
📝 The prisoner appealed for **clemency** on the grounds of ill health and age.

**COERCE** | জোর করা, বল প্রয়োগ করে রাজি করানো
To persuade someone to do something using force or threats.
✅ force, compel, pressurize, intimidate, bully, threaten, oblige
❌ persuade gently, invite, encourage, ask, permit, allow, request

**COGENT** | যুক্তিসংগত, প্রভাবশালী, মজবুত যুক্তির
Clear, logical, and convincing; powerfully persuasive in argument.
✅ convincing, compelling, logical, clear, persuasive, forceful, sound
❌ weak, illogical, unconvincing, vague, flawed, incoherent, invalid

**COMMOTION** | হৈচৈ, গণ্ডগোল, বিক্ষোভ
A state of confused and noisy disturbance; fuss and disorder.
✅ uproar, chaos, disturbance, noise, upheaval, ruckus, disorder
❌ calm, peace, quiet, order, stillness, tranquility, silence

**COMPUNCTION** | বিবেকের দংশন, অনুশোচনা, অপরাধবোধ
A feeling of guilt or moral scruple about one's actions; remorse.
✅ remorse, guilt, regret, qualm, unease, prick of conscience, shame
❌ satisfaction, shamelessness, unapologetic attitude, indifference
📝 She felt no **compunction** about reporting the fraud to the authorities.

**CONVOLUTED** | জটিল, প্যাঁচালো, বোঝা কঠিন
Extremely complex and difficult to follow; intricately twisted.
✅ complicated, intricate, tangled, confusing, complex, labyrinthine

**CORROBORATE** | নিশ্চিত করা, সমর্থন করা, প্রমাণ করা

✅ confirm, verify, support, validate, substantiate, authenticate, back up
❌ contradict, refute, deny, disprove, negate, undermine, dispute

**CULPABLE** | দোষী, দায়ী, নিন্দার যোগ্য

✅ guilty, blameworthy, liable, responsible, at fault, censurable

## 🔤 D–F (Extra)

**DAUNT** | ভয় দেখানো, নিরুৎসাহিত করা
To make someone feel intimidated or apprehensive; to discourage.
✅ intimidate, frighten, discourage, unnerve, deter, frighten, put off
❌ encourage, embolden, inspire, motivate, reassure, support, strengthen

**DEBUNK** | ভুল প্রমাণ করা, মিথ্যা দাবি খণ্ডন করা
To expose the falseness or hollowness of a myth, idea, or belief.
✅ disprove, expose, refute, discredit, contradict, challenge, reveal
❌ confirm, support, validate, prove, endorse, verify, strengthen
📝 The scientist worked to **debunk** the widespread misconception about vaccines.

**DECEPTIVE** | প্রতারণামূলক, বিভ্রান্তিকর
Giving an appearance or impression different from the true one; misleading.
✅ misleading, dishonest, fraudulent, deceitful, false, illusory, tricky
❌ honest, truthful, transparent, accurate, genuine, reliable, direct
📝 The figures in the report were **deceptive** — they hid the true scale of the loss.

**DECRY** | প্রকাশ্যে নিন্দা করা, ছোট করা
To publicly denounce something; to express strong disapproval of.
✅ condemn, criticize, denounce, disparage, denigrate, deplore, attack
❌ praise, support, commend, approve, endorse, celebrate, promote

**DEFERENCE** | শ্রদ্ধাপূর্বক মেনে নেওয়া, সম্মান দেখানো
Humble submission and respect; yielding to the opinion of another.
✅ respect, submission, regard, reverence, compliance, yielding, honour
❌ disrespect, defiance, arrogance, contempt, insubordination, resistance
📝 She spoke with **deference** to the senior researcher whose expertise she respected.

**DEFUNCT** | অচল, মৃত, কার্যকর নয়
No longer existing or functioning; dead; out of use or operation.
✅ dead, obsolete, extinct, inactive, disused, non-existent, gone
❌ active, alive, functioning, operational, current, existing, working
📝 The once-popular restaurant chain had been completely **defunct** for a decade.

**DELIBERATE** | ইচ্ছাকৃত; সতর্কভাবে বিবেচনা করা
Done consciously and intentionally; careful and unhurried.
✅ intentional, planned, calculated, considered, careful, measured, studied
❌ accidental, impulsive, hasty, careless, unplanned, spontaneous
📝 Every word in her letter was **deliberate** — nothing was left to chance.

**DENIGRATE** | নিন্দা করা, ছোট করা, হেয় প্রতিপন্ন করা
To criticize unfairly; to blacken someone's reputation; to belittle.
✅ belittle, defame, slander, criticize, disparage, insult, deprecate
❌ praise, commend, defend, celebrate, honour, respect, uphold
📝 Attempts to **denigrate** her work only made her more determined to succeed.

**DESIST** | বিরত হওয়া, থেমে যাওয়া, ছেড়ে দেওয়া
To stop doing something; to cease an action; to abstain from.
✅ stop, cease, halt, refrain, discontinue, give up, abstain, quit
❌ continue, persist, carry on, proceed, resume, maintain, press on
📝 He was ordered by the court to immediately **desist** from the activity.

**DETER** | বাধা দেওয়া, নিরুৎসাহিত করা, ঠেকানো
To discourage someone from doing something through doubt or fear.
✅ discourage, prevent, dissuade, inhibit, stop, block, hinder, check
❌ encourage, motivate, inspire, allow, support, promote, enable
📝 Harsh penalties are meant to **deter** others from committing similar crimes.

**DEVOUT** | ধার্মিক, ভক্তিপূর্ণ, নিষ্ঠাবান
Having or showing deep religious feeling or commitment; sincere.
✅ pious, religious, faithful, dedicated, sincere, fervent, earnest
❌ irreligious, impious, sinful, godless, apathetic, uncommitted
📝 She was a **devout** follower who attended religious services without fail.

**DIFFUSE** | ছড়িয়ে দেওয়া, বিস্তার করা; বিক্ষিপ্ত
To spread over a wide area; to cause to become less concentrated.
✅ spread, scatter, disperse, circulate, disseminate, broadcast, distribute
❌ concentrate, gather, focus, collect, condense, centralize, limit
📝 Social media helps to **diffuse** both useful information and harmful rumours.

**DILATORY** | ধীরগতি, বিলম্বকারী, টালবাহানার প্রবণ
Slow to act; causing or intended to cause delay; tending to procrastinate.
✅ slow, tardy, delaying, procrastinating, unhurried, dawdling, sluggish
❌ prompt, quick, fast, efficient, punctual, on time, decisive
📝 His **dilatory** approach to responding to emails frustrated the entire team.

**DISCORD** | বিবাদ, মতভেদ, বেমিল
Disagreement between people; lack of harmony or agreement.
✅ conflict, disagreement, strife, friction, tension, dispute, division
❌ harmony, agreement, unity, peace, accord, concord, cooperation
📝 Financial pressures created deep **discord** within the otherwise happy family.

**DISMAY** | হতাশ করা, আতঙ্কিত করা; হতাশা
Consternation and distress caused by something unexpected; to horrify.
✅ alarm, distress, shock, horror, astonish, disappoint, unsettle
❌ reassure, comfort, delight, please, calm, encourage, impress
📝 She looked on with **dismay** as the carefully prepared event fell apart.

**DISPEL** | দূর করা, তাড়িয়ে দেওয়া
To make a doubt, feeling, or belief disappear; to drive away.
✅ eliminate, remove, dismiss, banish, scatter, drive away, clear
❌ create, encourage, increase, maintain, promote, reinforce, spread
📝 His warm welcome helped to **dispel** any doubts she had about coming.

**DISQUIET** | উদ্বেগ, অস্থিরতা, অশান্তি
A feeling of worry or anxiety; to make someone feel anxious.
✅ anxiety, worry, unease, concern, nervousness, agitation, trouble
❌ calm, peace, reassurance, contentment, serenity, comfort, ease
📝 There was a growing **disquiet** among staff about the future of the company.

**DISSIPATE** | নষ্ট করা, ছড়িয়ে ফেলা, অপব্যয় করা
To cause to disappear or lose energy; to squander money or resources.
✅ scatter, disperse, waste, squander, fade, evaporate, disappear
❌ conserve, gather, save, maintain, build, accumulate, preserve
📝 The morning fog slowly began to **dissipate** as the sun climbed higher.

**DIVERGE** | আলাদা হওয়া, ভিন্নদিকে যাওয়া
To develop in a different direction; to depart from a set course.
✅ separate, differ, branch off, deviate, fork, split, vary, differ
❌ converge, unite, agree, meet, join, merge, come together
📝 Their opinions began to **diverge** sharply as the discussion deepened.

**DOGMATIC** | গোঁড়া, নিজের মতামতে অনমনীয়
Inclined to lay down principles as undeniably true; rigidly opinionated.
✅ rigid, inflexible, opinionated, narrow-minded, dictatorial, stubborn
❌ open-minded, flexible, willing to change, receptive, adaptable
📝 His **dogmatic** approach to management prevented any innovation.

**DUBIOUS** | সন্দেহজনক, অনিশ্চিত, অবিশ্বাসযোগ্য
Hesitating or doubting; not to be relied on; suspect in quality.
✅ doubtful, suspicious, uncertain, questionable, shady, unreliable
❌ trustworthy, reliable, certain, definite, honest, genuine, credible
📝 The **dubious** deal was rejected after careful scrutiny of the terms.

**DUPLICITOUS** | ধোঁকাবাজ, দুই মুখো, প্রতারণামূলক
Deceitful; saying or doing different things to different people; two-faced.
✅ deceptive, dishonest, double-dealing, treacherous, two-faced, false
❌ honest, straightforward, sincere, genuine, transparent, trustworthy
📝 His **duplicitous** behaviour finally caught up with him when both stories crossed.

**EBULLIENT** | উচ্ছ্বসিত, প্রাণচঞ্চল, আনন্দময়
Cheerful and full of energy; overflowing with enthusiasm.
✅ enthusiastic, exuberant, lively, spirited, buoyant, vibrant, jovial
❌ subdued, gloomy, depressed, quiet, dull, listless, downcast
📝 Her **ebullient** personality lit up every room she entered.

**ELICIT** | বের করে আনা, প্রকাশ করানো
To draw out a response, answer, or reaction from someone.
✅ draw out, evoke, extract, provoke, prompt, bring out, generate
❌ suppress, prevent, block, discourage, hide, conceal, stifle
📝 The professor's question was designed to **elicit** original thinking from students.

**ELOQUENT** | বাকপটু, সুবক্তা, প্রভাবশালী বক্তা
Fluent and persuasive in speaking or writing; movingly expressive.
✅ articulate, expressive, persuasive, fluent, moving, forceful, well-spoken
❌ inarticulate, tongue-tied, fumbling, unclear, inexpressive, mumbling
📝 Her **eloquent** defence of the accused moved even the judge to reconsider.

**EMBROIL** | জড়িত করা, বিবাদে টেনে আনা
To involve someone deeply in an argument, conflict, or difficult situation.
✅ involve, entangle, implicate, drag in, engulf, catch up, entrap
❌ free, disentangle, separate, remove, protect, extract, exclude
📝 He was determined not to be **embroiled** in someone else's personal dispute.

**EMPHATIC** | জোরালো, স্পষ্ট ও নিশ্চিত, জোর দিয়ে বলা
Expressing something forcibly and clearly; definite and clear; strong.
✅ forceful, clear, strong, definite, positive, unambiguous, assertive
❌ weak, uncertain, vague, equivocal, hesitant, ambiguous, tentative
📝 She gave an **emphatic** "no" that left no doubt about her position.

**ENGROSS** | সম্পূর্ণ মনোযোগ নেওয়া, মগ্ন করা
To absorb all the attention or interest of someone; to occupy fully.
✅ absorb, occupy, captivate, engage, preoccupy, immerse, fascinate
❌ bore, distract, free, release, disinterest, repel, unoccupy
📝 She was so **engrossed** in her book that she did not hear the doorbell.

**ENNUI** | একঘেয়ে জীবনের বিরক্তি, অতৃপ্তি
A feeling of listlessness and dissatisfaction arising from lack of interest.
✅ boredom, listlessness, tedium, dissatisfaction, restlessness, apathy
❌ excitement, interest, enthusiasm, joy, engagement, passion, zeal
📝 A long spell of **ennui** set in after she finished the major project.

**ENUMERATE** | এক এক করে গণনা করা, তালিকা করা
To mention things one by one; to count or list in ordered sequence.
✅ list, count, itemize, specify, detail, name, catalogue, identify
❌ generalize, summarize, ignore, omit, group, gloss over, overlook
📝 The report **enumerated** every failure in the project management process.

**EPHEMERAL** | ক্ষণস্থায়ী, অল্পস্থায়ী, চিরস্থায়ী নয়
Lasting for a very short time; short-lived and transitory.
✅ transient, fleeting, temporary, brief, short-lived, passing, momentary
❌ permanent, eternal, lasting, enduring, perpetual, everlasting, durable
📝 Internet trends are often **ephemeral** — popular today, forgotten tomorrow.

**ERADICATE** | সম্পূর্ণভাবে দূর করা, নির্মূল করা
To destroy completely; to remove or eliminate something entirely.
✅ eliminate, destroy, remove, wipe out, abolish, uproot, exterminate
❌ preserve, protect, maintain, cultivate, nurture, support, spread
📝 The campaign aimed to completely **eradicate** malaria from the region.

**EVASIVE** | এড়িয়ে চলার প্রবণ, অস্পষ্ট উত্তর দেওয়া
Tending to avoid commitment or self-revelation; ambiguous or misleading.
✅ ambiguous, vague, elusive, slippery, non-committal, indirect, unclear
❌ direct, honest, straightforward, forthright, open, transparent, clear
📝 The minister's **evasive** answers during the press conference raised more questions.

**EXACERBATE** | আরও খারাপ করা, তীব্র করা
To make a problem or bad situation worse; to aggravate or intensify.
✅ worsen, aggravate, intensify, inflame, magnify, compound, increase
❌ improve, alleviate, soothe, reduce, calm, ease, solve, fix
📝 Delaying the decision will only **exacerbate** the crisis that is already developing.

**EXHAUSTIVE** | পুঙ্খানুপুঙ্খ, সম্পূর্ণ, সব দিক কভার করা
Including or considering all elements; thorough and complete in scope.
✅ thorough, comprehensive, complete, detailed, extensive, all-inclusive
❌ incomplete, partial, superficial, cursory, brief, selective, narrow
📝 The **exhaustive** investigation left no stone unturned in finding the truth.

**EXPLICIT** | স্পষ্ট, খোলামেলা, পরিষ্কার
Stated clearly and in detail, leaving no room for confusion or doubt.
✅ clear, direct, specific, definite, unambiguous, precise, expressed
❌ implicit, vague, ambiguous, unclear, hidden, indirect, unstated
📝 The agreement included **explicit** instructions that both parties had to follow.

**EXQUISITE** | অত্যন্ত সুন্দর, নিখুঁত, অসাধারণ মনোরম
Extremely beautiful and delicate; intensely felt; of special beauty.
✅ beautiful, elegant, perfect, delicate, refined, lovely, stunning
❌ ugly, crude, ordinary, coarse, unpleasant, rough, common
📝 The **exquisite** craftsmanship of the necklace left the jeweller speechless.

**EXTOL** | উচ্চ প্রশংসা করা, গুণকীর্তন করা
To praise enthusiastically; to speak very highly of someone or something.
✅ praise, laud, commend, celebrate, glorify, acclaim, exalt, honour
❌ criticize, condemn, disparage, belittle, dismiss, attack, denounce
📝 Teachers often **extol** the benefits of reading every single day.

**FACADE** | বাহ্যিক চেহারা, মিথ্যা ভাব, সামনের দিক
The front of a building; an outward appearance hiding the true nature.
✅ front, exterior, surface, pretence, mask, show, false appearance
❌ reality, interior, truth, genuine nature, authentic self, core
📝 His confident manner was a **facade** hiding deep uncertainty underneath.

**FASTIDIOUS** | সূক্ষ্ম রুচির, খুঁতখুঁতে, নিখুঁত পছন্দের
Very attentive to accuracy and detail; difficult to please; very careful.
✅ meticulous, fussy, picky, demanding, exacting, particular, precise
❌ careless, indifferent, sloppy, easygoing, uncritical, casual, lax
📝 The **fastidious** editor combed through every paragraph for the smallest error.

**FAWN** | তোষামোদ করা, খুশি করতে মরিয়া হওয়া
To seek favour by flattery; to behave obsequiously toward someone.
✅ flatter, grovel, toady, be sycophantic, curry favour, ingratiate
❌ criticize, stand up to, oppose, ignore, show independence, challenge
📝 He was embarrassed watching his colleagues **fawn** over the visiting celebrity.

**FERMENT** | গাঁজন করানো; রাজনৈতিক বা সামাজিক অস্থিরতা
A state of agitation or unrest; to cause turbulence or excitement.
✅ agitation, unrest, turmoil, upheaval, stir, excitement, commotion
❌ calm, stability, order, peace, tranquility, quiet, harmony
📝 Political **ferment** had been building for months before the protests erupted.

**FIASCO** | সম্পূর্ণ ব্যর্থতা, বিপর্যয়, হাস্যকর ব্যর্থতা
A complete failure, especially a ludicrous or humiliating one.
✅ disaster, failure, catastrophe, debacle, flop, collapse, ruin
❌ success, triumph, achievement, victory, accomplishment, breakthrough
📝 The launch event was an embarrassing **fiasco** that made headlines for all wrong reasons.

**FLAGRANT** | প্রকাশ্যে জঘন্য, নির্লজ্জ, বিশেষভাবে লক্ষণীয়
Conspicuously or obviously offensive; shockingly bad or wrong.
✅ blatant, obvious, glaring, outrageous, brazen, shameless, notorious
❌ subtle, hidden, concealed, minor, discreet, understated, slight
📝 The **flagrant** abuse of power by the official was documented on camera.

**FLOUNDER** | কষ্ট পাওয়া, সামলাতে পারছে না এমন
To struggle helplessly or make erratic movements; to be in difficulty.
✅ struggle, stumble, have difficulty, be confused, thrash about, lose way
❌ thrive, manage well, succeed, sail through, progress, master
📝 Without clear leadership, the organisation began to **flounder** badly.

**FORBEAR** | সহ্য করা, নিজেকে সংযত রাখা
To restrain oneself from doing or saying something; to hold back.
✅ refrain, hold back, restrain, resist, abstain, stop oneself, desist
❌ indulge, proceed, continue, act, say, express, carry on
📝 She managed to **forbear** from commenting even when she strongly disagreed.

**FOREBODING** | অশুভ পূর্বাভাস, অমঙ্গলের আশঙ্কা
A feeling that something bad will happen; an omen of coming evil.
✅ premonition, dread, anxiety, omen, warning, apprehension, doom
❌ hope, confidence, optimism, assurance, calm, expectation of good
📝 She had a strange **foreboding** all morning before hearing the bad news.

**FORTHRIGHT** | সরাসরি, খোলামেলা, সৎ
Direct and outspoken; going straight to the point without hesitation.
✅ direct, honest, candid, frank, blunt, outspoken, plain-spoken, clear
❌ evasive, indirect, dishonest, subtle, guarded, secretive, deceptive
📝 His **forthright** assessment of the situation was uncomfortable but invaluable.

**FRUGALITY** | মিতব্যয়িতা, সাশ্রয়ী মনোভাব
The quality of being economical with food or money; not wasteful.
✅ thrift, economy, carefulness, prudence, restraint, sparingness
❌ extravagance, wastefulness, lavishness, indulgence, profligacy
📝 Her **frugality** throughout her working life allowed her a comfortable retirement.

---

## 🔤 G–Z (Extra Critical Words)

**GARRULOUS** | বকবকে, অতিরিক্ত কথা বলার প্রবণতার
Excessively talkative, especially on trivial matters; chatty.
✅ talkative, loquacious, chatty, verbose, long-winded, wordy, babbling
❌ quiet, reticent, silent, reserved, brief, concise, taciturn
📝 The **garrulous** uncle kept the entire dinner table entertained for hours.

**GERMANE** | প্রাসঙ্গিক, বিষয়সম্পর্কিত, উপযুক্ত
Relevant and appropriate to the matter in hand; pertinent and fitting.
✅ relevant, pertinent, applicable, appropriate, fitting, apt, related
❌ irrelevant, unrelated, inapplicable, beside the point, off-topic
📝 Her comment was entirely **germane** to the question being discussed.

**GLEAN** | সামান্য সামান্য করে সংগ্রহ করা
To gather information or material from various sources in small amounts.
✅ gather, collect, pick up, acquire, harvest, extract, obtain, find
❌ waste, scatter, lose, overlook, discard, ignore, abandon
📝 She managed to **glean** enough information from the reports to draw conclusions.

**GRATIFY** | সন্তুষ্ট করা, আনন্দিত করা, পূরণ করা
To give someone pleasure or satisfaction; to fulfil a desire or wish.
✅ satisfy, please, fulfil, delight, appease, indulge, accommodate
❌ dissatisfy, disappoint, frustrate, annoy, deny, refuse, upset
📝 It **gratified** her enormously to see her students winning national awards.

**GRIEVANCE** | অভিযোগ, অভিমান, অন্যায়ের কষ্ট
A real or imagined wrong causing resentment; a formal complaint.
✅ complaint, objection, protest, resentment, injustice, wrong, issue
❌ satisfaction, contentment, gratitude, praise, approval, pleasure
📝 Workers submitted a formal **grievance** over the new overtime policy.

**GUILE** | ধূর্ততা, চালাকি, প্রতারণামূলক কৌশল
Sly or cunning intelligence used to deceive; craftiness.
✅ cunning, deceit, craftiness, slyness, trickery, deception, wiles
❌ honesty, candour, openness, sincerity, guilelessness, naivety
📝 The diplomat used **guile** rather than confrontation to achieve the agreement.

**HARBINGER** | পূর্বাভাস বহনকারী, আগাম সংকেত
A person or thing that announces the approach of something; a forerunner.
✅ herald, omen, precursor, forerunner, sign, signal, portent, indicator
❌ aftermath, consequence, result, conclusion, follower, outcome
📝 Dark clouds are often a **harbinger** of the storm that soon follows.

**HEGEMONY** | আধিপত্য, কর্তৃত্ব, প্রভাবশালী অবস্থান
Leadership or dominance, especially of one country or group over others.
✅ dominance, leadership, control, supremacy, authority, power, command
❌ subordination, weakness, dependence, submission, equality, balance
📝 The empire maintained its **hegemony** through a combination of trade and military power.

**HYPERBOLE** | অতিরঞ্জন, অত্যুক্তি
Exaggerated statements not meant to be taken literally; overstatement.
✅ exaggeration, overstatement, embellishment, overemphasis, excess
❌ understatement, litotes, plain statement, truth, accuracy
📝 Saying "I've told you a million times" is a classic example of **hyperbole**.

**IDIOSYNCRASY** | ব্যক্তিগত বৈশিষ্ট্য, স্বতন্ত্র অভ্যাস
A mode of behaviour or thinking peculiar to an individual; an oddity.
✅ quirk, peculiarity, oddity, eccentricity, mannerism, habit, trait
❌ conformity, normality, standard behaviour, convention, commonplace
📝 His habit of underlining every sentence was just one of his many **idiosyncrasies**.

**IMMUTABLE** | অপরিবর্তনীয়, স্থায়ী, বদলানো যায় না
Unchanging over time; not able to be changed; fixed and permanent.
✅ unchangeable, fixed, constant, permanent, invariable, inflexible
❌ mutable, changeable, flexible, variable, adaptable, unstable
📝 The laws of physics are considered essentially **immutable** constants.

**IMPETUOUS** | বেপরোয়া, হঠকারী, তাড়াহুড়ো করা
Acting or done quickly without thought or care; impulsive and rash.
✅ impulsive, rash, hasty, reckless, headstrong, spontaneous, brash
❌ cautious, deliberate, careful, thoughtful, restrained, measured
📝 His **impetuous** decision to resign without a plan shocked all his colleagues.

**INCISIVE** | তীক্ষ্ণ, ধারালো, সরাসরি বক্তব্য
Intelligently analytical and clear-thinking; sharp and concise in thought.
✅ sharp, keen, piercing, clear, penetrating, astute, direct, acute
❌ vague, unclear, dull, rambling, unfocused, shallow, muddled
📝 Her **incisive** analysis of the data impressed even the senior economists.

**INCONGRUITY** | অসামঞ্জস্য, অমিল, বেমানানভাব
The state of being incongruous; inconsistency; lack of harmony.
✅ inconsistency, mismatch, incompatibility, absurdity, contrast
❌ consistency, harmony, compatibility, congruence, fit, agreement
📝 The **incongruity** of wearing formal shoes with gym clothes caught everyone's eye.

**INDOLENCE** | অলসতা, কর্মবিমুখতা, নিষ্ক্রিয়তা
Habitual laziness or disinclination to exertion; sloth.
✅ laziness, sloth, sluggishness, inertia, lethargy, idleness, torpor
❌ diligence, industriousness, energy, activity, hard work, motivation
📝 His **indolence** cost him three job opportunities that year.

**INEXORABLE** | অনিবার্য, থামানো যায় না, নিষ্ঠুরভাবে অবিরাম
Not to be persuaded, moved, or stopped; relentless and unyielding.
✅ relentless, unstoppable, inevitable, unceasing, implacable, merciless
❌ stoppable, flexible, avoidable, manageable, yielding, preventable
📝 The **inexorable** advance of technology continues to reshape every industry.

**INFER** | অনুমান করা, সিদ্ধান্তে পৌঁছানো
To deduce or conclude from evidence and reasoning; to imply.
✅ deduce, conclude, gather, assume, reason, understand, derive
❌ state directly, assert, declare, announce, say explicitly
📝 From the evidence, it was possible to **infer** that the meeting had been planned.

**INGENUOUS** | সহজ, সরল, নিষ্পাপ, বিশ্বাসপ্রবণ
Innocent and unsuspecting; naively simple; lacking guile or cunning.
✅ naive, innocent, simple, trusting, candid, open, artless, sincere
❌ sophisticated, cunning, deceptive, sly, worldly, devious, guarded
📝 His **ingenuous** expression made it hard to believe he was being dishonest.
